### [1] [Heating of black-Si by microwaves at 2.45 GHz: effect of nano-needle orientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13683)
*Massimiliano Zamengo,Haoran Mu,Hsin-Hui Huang,Tomas Katkus,Darius Gailevičius,Saulius Juodkazis,Junko Morikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 黑硅（black-Si）通过SF6/O2等离子刻蚀形成纳米针表面织构后，可在2.45 GHz微波下被有效加热。在圆柱TM010谐振腔中对比竖直与倾斜纳米针样品的加热行为，发现竖直纳米针升温最高（约260 °C），且谐振频率在温度超过约120–150 °C后出现2–3 MHz下移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面硅（flat Si）在微波2.45 GHz波段高反射且吸收可忽略，难以直接微波加热；研究旨在探究将其转化为黑硅后能否借助纳米针表面织构实现微波加热，并比较不同纳米针取向的影响。

Method: 以SF6/O2等离子刻蚀（plasma etching）制备竖直与倾斜（取向）纳米针黑硅，与平面硅在相同条件下的圆柱TM010谐振腔（cylindrical TM010 resonator）中对比。样品水平置于150微米厚盖玻片上以统一高度与位置，并用K型热电偶（K-type thermocouple）校准辐射温度计；采用2-3-4 W、每步60 s的分步微波功率协议，并原位监测腔体共振。

Result: 竖直纳米针黑硅达到约260 °C，系统性高于平面硅（约190 °C）；倾斜纳米针黑硅则在3 W时出现延迟但陡然的升温起始。所有样品的共振频率f均出现2–3 MHz下移，且漂移在温度超过约120–150 °C时一致出现，与硅中热激活载流子产生相符。同一温度下各样品的腔体负载表现为不同的f与品质因子Q（quality factor），指示纳米针阵列的有效介电常数（effective permittivity）具取向依赖性，与倾斜黑硅的强形状双折射（form birefringence）一致。

Conclusion: 黑硅纳米针织构可显著增强硅的微波加热能力，且加热与谐振响应依赖纳米针取向。文中讨论了该方法在定量温度与介电常数测定方面的应用潜力及挑战。

Abstract: Silicon, which is highly reflective and has negligible absorption at the microwave 2.45 GHz range, can be heated when it is turned into black-Si with a surface texture of nano-needles made by SF6/O2 plasma etching. Microwave heating of flat Si and of black-Si with vertical and with tilted (oriented) nano-needles was compared under identical conditions in a cylindrical TM010 resonator. Samples were placed horizontally on a 150-micrometer-thick cover glass, which standardized their height and position inside the cavity and allowed a common calibration of the radiation thermometer against a K-type thermocouple. Under a stepwise protocol of only 2-3-4 W of microwave power (60 s per step), black-Si with vertical needles reached ~ 260 C, systematically exceeding flat Si (~ 190 C), while black-Si with oriented needles showed a delayed but abrupt onset of heating at 3 W. In situ monitoring of the cavity resonance revealed a 2-3 MHz downshift of the resonance frequency f for all samples, with the drift consistently appearing once the sample temperature exceeded ~ 120 - 150 C, consistent with thermally activated carrier generation in Si. At a given temperature, each sample loads the cavity at a different f and quality factor Q, indicating an orientation-dependent effective permittivity of the nano-needle array, in line with the strong form birefringence of tilted black-Si. Application potential and challenges in the quantitative temperature and permittivity determination are discussed.

</details>


### [2] [Giant second-harmonic generation in few-atomic layer metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14468)
*Alessio Zaccone*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该理论表明，三维量子限域（quantum confinement）对费米能级态密度（DOS）的增强有约10.1%的上限，但通过二阶磁化率（second-order susceptibility）中的共振分母可使电子相空间重分布被强烈放大，从而解释Ag(111)薄膜中观测到的巨大二次谐波产生（SHG）效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超薄金属中的量子限域会重构电子相空间，但其导致的态密度变化通常并不显著，因此难以解释实验中观测到的光学响应大幅增强，需要建立能够放大相空间重分布效应的机制。

Method: 基于长波长电子态被抑制的三维限域理论（three-dimensional confinement theory），推导弱限域支（weak-confinement branch）上费米能级DOS增强的上界，并利用二阶磁化率中的共振分母构建最小双共振闭合（minimal double-resonance closure）模型。

Result: 理论上界为 (4/3)^{1/3}-1 ≃ 10.1%；当膜厚趋近 L_c=(2π/n)^{1/3}（n为载流子密度）且光学通道落在退相干线宽（dephasing linewidth）内时出现巨大放大。对Ag(111)实验拟合得到 n_eff ≃ 3.6×10^20 cm^-3、|χ^(2)| 增强十倍，对应厚度归一化SHG效率约100倍提升及非振荡的厚度依赖。

Conclusion: 量子限域本身对DOS的增强虽受限，但经二阶非线性响应的共振放大可产生数量级的光学效率提升，该框架可自洽解释Ag(111)薄膜的SHG实验观测。

Abstract: Quantum confinement restructures the electronic phase space of ultrathin metals, but the resulting density-of-states (DOS) change is not generically large. We show that, within a three-dimensional confinement theory based on the suppression of long-wavelength electronic states, the Fermi-level DOS enhancement on the weak-confinement branch is bounded by $(4/3)^{1/3}-1\simeq10.1\%$. Instead, confinement-induced redistribution of electronic phase space can be strongly amplified by resonant denominators in the second-order susceptibility. A minimal double-resonance closure predicts giant amplification when the film thickness approaches $L_c=(2π/n)^{1/3}$, where $n$ is the carrier density, and a dominant optical pathway lies within a dephasing linewidth of resonance. Applied to recent Ag(111) measurements, the theory gives an effective phase-space carrier density parameter $n_{\rm eff}\simeq3.6\times10^{20}\,\mathrm{cm}^{-3}$, a tenfold enhancement of $|χ^{(2)}|$, and hence the observed $\sim100$-fold increase of thickness-normalized second-harmonic-generation efficiency, together with the observed non-oscillatory thickness dependence.

</details>


### [3] [Up/down-conversion of infrared light by few-layer graphene polytypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15866)
*Patrick Johansen Sarsfield,Takaaki V. Joya,Takuto Kawakami,Mikito Koshino,Vladimir Falko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 少层石墨烯（few-layer graphene）薄膜在红外波段具备上/下转换（up/down-conversion）能力，其中 ABCB 混合堆垛四层与经非对称封装的菱方（rhombohedral）三层/四层展现出显著二阶非线性（second-order nonlinearity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于利用破缺反演对称性（broken inversion symmetry）材料中的双光子过程，寻找可在红外光谱范围实现上/下转换的材料，并探索其在光纤中制备关联光子对的应用可能。

Method: 识别具备红外上/下转换能力的少层石墨烯薄膜；重点关注非中心对称的 ABCB 混合堆垛四层二维晶体，并对菱方三层与四层采用非对称封装（asymmetrical encapsulation）以促进二阶非线性。

Result: 在 ABCB 四层中实现 [$ω_1,ω_2$] 光子对与 $0.7$eV$<Ω=ω_1+ω_2<1.1$eV 光子之间高效且近共振的上/下转换；并在 $0.7$eV$<Ω<0.9$eV 范围观察到菱方三层与四层的显著二阶非线性。

Conclusion: 少层石墨烯薄膜为红外波段的光子对产生与转换提供平台，并可能实现涂覆少层石墨烯的光纤中"in fiber"产生具有关联偏振（correlated polarizations）的光子对。

Abstract: Optical nonlinearity of materials with broken inversion symmetry enables two-photon processes where an incoming pair of photons can generate an up-converted photon with the combined frequency, or a high-energy photon can be split into a correlated pair of down-converted photons. Here, we identify few-layer graphene films that offer up/down-conversion capability in the infrared spectral range. For mixed stacking tetralayers (ABCB), which are non-centrosymmetric 2D crystals, we find highly efficient nearly resonant up/down-conversion of [$ω_1,ω_2$] photon pairs into/from a photon with $0.7$eV$<Ω=ω_1+ω_2<1.1$eV. We also note that a pronounced second-order nonlinearity in the spectral range of $0.7$eV$<Ω<0.9$eV can be promoted in rhombohedral tri- and tetralayers by asymmetrical encapsulation. Potentially, for fibers coated with few-layer graphene, this opens the door for ``in fiber" production of photon pairs with correlated polarizations.

</details>

### [4] [Local Response Theory of Electrified Interfaces from Screened Effective Charges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13542)
*Nicolas G. Hörmann,Nicolas Bergmann,Karsten Reuter*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出“屏蔽电化学玻恩有效电荷（SEBEC）”，作为混合总能量导数，用于衡量电化学边界条件下原子自由度对界面充电的力响应，从而为电化学界面提供类似固体物理玻恩有效电荷的局域响应框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理电子结构模拟可给出基态全局性质（如偏压依赖能量、自由能、功函数、电容），但对这些量如何通过界面充电响应的系统性局域描述仍缺失。作者希望将电化学界面的响应理论提升至绝缘固体响应的概念严谨水平。

Method: 引入屏蔽电化学玻恩有效电荷（SEBEC），定义为混合总能量导数，测量电化学边界条件下原子自由度对界面充电的力响应。其功能类比于现代极化理论中的玻恩有效电荷（Born effective charge），用于明确分解全局响应。

Result: SEBEC为全局响应提供无歧义分解，并在结构弛豫、电容及电化学斯塔克调谐（electrochemical Stark tuning）等方面生成与固态响应理论相同的形式结构。

Conclusion: SEBEC构成一个局域响应框架，使电化学界面研究更接近绝缘固体中已长期确立的概念严谨性。

Abstract: First-principles, electronic-structure simulations provide direct access to ground-state, global system properties. In the context of computational electrochemistry these are, e.g., bias-dependent energies, free energies, work functions, and capacitances. However, a comparably systematic local description of how these quantities respond to biasing via interfacial charging is still missing. Here, we introduce the screened electrochemical Born effective charge (SEBEC), a mixed total-energy derivative that measures the force response of an atomic degree of freedom to interfacial charging under electrochemical boundary conditions. SEBECs play the role for electrochemistry that Born effective charges play in the modern theory of polarization: they provide an unambiguous decomposition of global response and generate the same formal structures for structural relaxation, capacitance, and electrochemical Stark tuning that are familiar from solid-state response theory. The broader implication is a local response framework that brings electrochemical interfaces closer to the conceptual rigor long established for insulating solids.

</details>


### [5] [Quantum interpretation of classical LiF simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13910)
*Federico Brivio,Luca Salasnich*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作指出，经典 LiF 光谱模拟所需的“较高模拟温度”本质上可由量子振动统计导出；通过匹配经典与量子谐振子能量定义有效温度，并借助解析声学-光学模型及第一性原理振动态密度进行定量验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，经典 LiF 模拟在高于物理温度 T 的模拟温度 T* 下才能复现低温实验光谱，但该温度重标度的来源缺乏解释。本文旨在为这一现象提供物理机制层面的说明。

Method: 通过匹配经典与量子谐振子（harmonic oscillator）能量，定义有效温度 T_eff(T)；并使用解析声学-光学（acoustic-optical）模型与第一性原理（first-principles）LiF 振动态密度 D(ε) 对该有效温度进行评估。

Result: 所得有效温度复现了文献报道的光谱温度在低温区间的特征尺度，并在经典极限（classical regime）下趋近于物理温度。

Conclusion: 结果表明，零点运动（zero-point motion）与 Planck-Bose-Einstein 统计是该温度重标度现象的根源。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that classical LiF simulations can reproduce low-temperature experimental spectra when performed at a simulation temperature $T^*$ higher than the physical temperature $T$. We show that the characteristic scale of this rescaling follows naturally from quantum vibrational statistics. An effective temperature $T_{\rm eff}(T)$ is obtained by matching classical and quantum harmonic energies and is evaluated using an analytic acoustic-optical model and a first-principles LiF vibrational density of states $D(\varepsilon)$. The resulting temperature reproduces the low-temperature scale of the reported spectral temperature and approaches the physical temperature in the classical regime. Our results identify zero-point motion and Planck-Bose-Einstein statistics as the origin of the reported temperature scale.

</details>


### [6] [Unbiased first-principles construction of complete tensorial spin Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13912)
*Haichang Lu,Boyang Deng,Hiroshi Katsumoto,John Robertson,Weisheng Zhao,Stefan Blügel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一套无需先验假设、从密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT）出发构建对称完备张量自旋哈密顿量（tensorial spin Hamiltonian）的通用框架，并在 AMATIS 中实现自动化；应用于二维范德华磁体后，复现了已知相互作用并揭示了手性相互作用、高阶张量相互作用与 p 波交替磁（altermagnetic）电子结构等隐藏物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁基态预测依赖预先选取相互作用项的自旋哈密顿量（spin Hamiltonian），可能遗漏支配复杂磁序的微观相互作用。因此需要一种无偏（unbiased）、第一性原理的哈密顿量构建方式，以避免人为选择相互作用所带来的偏差。

Method: 构建直接从密度泛函理论（density-functional theory）出发的对称完备张量自旋哈密顿量（symmetry-complete tensorial spin Hamiltonian）框架，严格施加量子自旋代数（quantum spin algebra）与晶体对称性（crystallographic symmetry），并通过 AMATIS 实现自动化。该框架被应用于代表性的二维范德华磁体（two-dimensional van der Waals magnets）。

Result: 该框架复现了已有的磁性相互作用，并发现超越常规自旋模型的隐藏物理：稳定亚稳态斯格明子（skyrmions）的手性相互作用（chiral interactions）、重构磁相图并确立稳定竞争性多 Q 相（multi-Q phases）的高阶张量相互作用（higher-rank tensorial interactions），以及涌现的 p 波交替磁（p-wave altermagnetic）电子结构。

Conclusion: 无偏张量哈密顿量构建为传统手工选取自旋模型相互作用的做法提供了具预测能力的替代方案，可实现非常规磁相（unconventional magnetic phases）的第一性原理发现。

Abstract: Magnetic ground states are commonly predicted using spin Hamiltonians whose interaction terms are selected a priori, potentially overlooking the microscopic interactions that govern complex magnetic order. Here, we introduce a general framework for the unbiased first-principles construction of symmetry-complete tensorial spin Hamiltonians and its automated implementation in AMATIS. The framework constructs the Hamiltonian directly from density-functional theory while rigorously enforcing quantum spin algebra and crystallographic symmetry. Applied to representative two-dimensional van der Waals magnets, the framework reproduces established magnetic interactions and uncovers hidden physics beyond conventional spin models, including chiral interactions that stabilize metastable skyrmions, higher-rank tensorial interactions that reconstruct the magnetic phase diagram and establish stabilizing competing multi-Q phases, and an emergent p-wave altermagnetic electronic structure. Our results demonstrate that unbiased tensorial Hamiltonian construction provides a predictive alternative to the conventional practice of manually selecting spin-model interactions, enabling first-principles discovery of unconventional magnetic phases.

</details>


### [7] [Towards a Metal-Organic Framework with Pore-Confined Electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13939)
*Julia H. Baratta,Andrew S. Rosen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用从头算（ab initio）方法系统考察金属有机框架（metal-organic framework, MOF）电子化物（electride），揭示孔道限域电子（pore-confined electrons）的形成机制与稳定化设计规则，并证明其可直接驱动化学反应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子化物具有低功函数、强给电子性与超导性等特性，而MOF具备永久孔隙率与化学可调性；将两者结合有望构建兼具孔道电子与结构可设计性的新材料体系，但此类MOF电子化物的稳定机制与功能尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 使用从头算（ab initio）方法与密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算，研究通过阴离子-电子交换（anion-electron exchange）过程在MOF中稳定孔道限域电子的机制，并据此提出设计规则、筛选候选MOF电子化物。

Result: 所研究的材料中存在占据态电子局域于孔道空间，其能带略低于或穿越费米能级（Fermi level）；所建立的设计规则可指导孔道限域电子的稳定化，并获得候选MOF电子化物。概念验证显示孔道限域电子可在无需表面位点吸附的情况下显著降低H2解离的反应活化能垒。

Conclusion: 纳米多孔材料中的孔道限域电子可能催生一类全新的催化模式，即反应在孔道空间内由以电子为中心的活性位点（electron-centered active sites）驱动。

Abstract: Electrides are an unconventional class of materials in which electrons are localized in crystallographic void spaces rather than solely around atomic nuclei, giving rise to appealing properties such as low work functions, strong electron-donating character, and even superconductivity. Here, we use ab initio methods to investigate metal-organic framework (MOF) electrides, a new class of materials that combines the interstitial electrons of electrides with the permanent porosity and chemical tunability of MOFs. These materials host pore-confined electrons: occupied electronic states localized in the pore space and with bands slightly below or crossing through the Fermi level. Using density functional theory calculations, we establish several design rules for stabilizing pore-confined electrons in MOFs via an anion-electron exchange process and identify candidate MOF electrides. As a proof-of-concept, we also demonstrate that the pore-confined electrons can directly facilitate chemical reactions, substantially lowering the activation barrier for H2 dissociation without requiring adsorption at a surface site. We envision that pore-confined electrons in nanoporous materials may enable a fundamentally new type of catalysis in which chemical reactions take place in the pore space, driven by electron-centered active sites.

</details>


### [8] [Interfacial Engineering Enabled High-Resolution Stretchable Metal-Level Conductive Lines and Transparent Conductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14311)
*Ke Chen,Eric Tianjiao Zhao,Weichen Wang,Yepin Zhao,Kostas Parkatzidis,Lukas Michalek,Yuran Shi,Elizabeth Zhang,Tianyang Chen,Ruiheng Wu,Hao Lyu,Yating Yao,Yujia Yuan,Tianhao Chen,Ruby Rong Zhou,Shiyuan Wei,Junyi Zhao,Jeffrey B. -H. Tok,Zhenan Bao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过材料设计与界面工程（interfacial engineering），开发硫醇官能化导电聚合物/金杂化叠层（thiol-functionalized conducting polymer/gold hybrid stack），可用标准光刻实现4微米线宽，同时保持高拉伸性与类金属导电率（>40,000 S/cm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可拉伸电子（stretchable electronics）向高集成密度与更小特征尺寸发展，作为其结构骨干的可拉伸导体（stretchable conductors）须同步缩小。现有导电材料难以在高分辨率下同时实现高拉伸性与高导电性。

Method: 采用材料设计与界面工程（interfacial engineering），构建硫醇官能化导电聚合物/金杂化叠层（thiol-functionalized conducting polymer/gold hybrid stack），并借助标准光刻（standard photolithography）进行图案化。

Result: 该叠层可图案化至4微米线宽，同时维持高拉伸性、类金属导电率（metal-like conductivity，>40,000 S/cm）与环境稳定性。据此演示了优于氧化铟锡（indium tin oxide, ITO）品质因数（figure-of-merit）的网格状可拉伸透明电极，以及以可拉伸导线互连的1000像素每英寸（pixel-per-inch）图像。

Conclusion: 该工作有助于拓展下一代功能软电子（functional soft electronics）的应用范围，包括电子皮肤（e-skins）与生物电子学（bioelectronics）。

Abstract: As stretchable electronics advance toward higher integration density and finer feature sizes, stretchable conductors, which serve as the architectural backbone of these electronics, must be scaled down accordingly. However, achieving both high stretchability and high electrical conductivity at high resolution remains a challenge using existing conductive materials. Here, through material design and interfacial engineering, we develop a thiol-functionalized conducting polymer/gold hybrid stack that can be patterned down to 4 micrometer linewidths using standard photolithography, while maintaining high stretchability, metal-like conductivity (>40,000 S/cm), and environmental stability. Leveraging this capability, we demonstrate grid-based stretchable transparent electrodes that surpass the figure-of-merit of indium tin oxide, as well as a 1000-pixel-per-inch image interconnected with stretchable lines. This work helps to broaden the scope of next-generation functional soft electronics, including e-skins and bioelectronics.

</details>


### [9] [Quantitative control and recording of materials-synthesis processes using an automated experimentation platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14928)
*Yusuke Hashimoto,Takaya Muramoto,Hikari Terada,Harim Song,Yuan Wang,Takaaki Tomai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一套低成本、易部署的自动化实验平台，强调可靠自动化与过程参数的定量记录，而非完全自主。平台由商用仪器（机械臂、电动移液器、网络摄像头、电子天平）与3D打印夹具组成，并由基于大语言模型（large language model, LLM）的AI代理生成控制代码。以ZIF-8合成验证，发现颗粒粒径分布强烈依赖电动移液器的分液速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的材料开发需要大量高质量材料数据，而实验的完全自主化虽被期待，但技术门槛高、采用有限。研究因此转向更易落地的目标：可靠自动化与实验过程的定量记录。

Method: 将商用仪器（机械臂、电动移液器、网络摄像头、电子天平）组合，用3D打印制作夹具等部件，并由基于大语言模型的AI代理生成控制代码来操作仪器。平台应用于双溶液混合实验系统，用于合成ZIF-8金属有机框架（metal-organic framework, MOF）。

Result: 产物中出现白色悬浮相，X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction, XRD）测量确认其为ZIF-8。颗粒粒径分布（particle size distribution）强烈依赖电动移液器的分液速度，该参数在人工操作中难以控制或记录；该依赖关系在重复实验中得到重现，验证了自动化合成的可重复性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，人工操作中很少被定量的过程参数的控制与记录，可决定材料数据的质量。所有控制代码、CAD模型与文档均已公开，以促进实验室规模实验自动化的推广。

Abstract: Data-driven materials development requires the collection of large amounts of high-quality materials data. Full autonomy of materials experiments is anticipated, but its technical hurdles are high and its adoption remains limited. In this study, we constructed a simple, easy-to-deploy automated experimentation platform that focuses not on full autonomy but on the reliable automation and quantitative recording of experimental processes. Specifically, commercially available instruments such as robot arms, electric pipettes, web cameras, and an electronic balance are combined, components such as fixtures are fabricated with a 3D printer, and the instruments are operated by control code generated by an AI agent based on a large language model. As a demonstration, we applied the platform to a two-solution mixing experimental system and synthesized ZIF-8, a metal-organic framework. A white suspension phase was observed in the product, and X-ray diffraction measurements confirmed that it was ZIF-8. We also found that its particle size distribution depends strongly on the solution dispensing speed of the electric pipette, which is a parameter that is difficult to control or record in manual operation. This dependence was reproduced in repeated runs, confirming the repeatability of the automated synthesis. This result is a good example showing that the control and recording of process parameters that are rarely quantified in manual work can govern the quality of materials data. All control code, CAD models, and documentation are made publicly available to encourage the spread of laboratory-scale automation of experiments.

</details>


### [10] [From bending to stretching driven peeling of heterogeneous adhesives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15417)
*Laurent Ponson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用断裂力学方法建立黏附剂剥离前沿的运动方程，将黏附剂视为Föppl–von Kármán薄膜，揭示拉伸与弯曲变形的竞争及其对黏附异质性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清黏附剂剥离过程中其弹性变形（拉伸与弯曲）与剥离驱动力之间的复杂相互作用，并理解界面非局域弹性（non-local elasticity）的产生机制。

Method: 基于断裂力学（fracture mechanics）途径，推导界面黏附前沿的运动方程；将黏附剂描述为Föppl–von Kármán薄膜，先处理直线黏附前沿，再研究黏附异质性（adhesion heterogeneities）的影响。

Result: 重现均质黏附剂中剥离强度（peel strength）随剥离角变化的特征（大角度由弯曲主导、低角度由拉伸主导）；黏附剂形状可用单一弯曲长度尺度描述；异质情形下黏附前沿变形由非局域界面弹性支配，其强度随剥离角减小，低角度下扰动弛豫更慢且增韧（toughening）效应更强。

Conclusion: 黏附剂的弹性变形对其剥离行为起核心作用；所提框架揭示变形与剥离驱动力间的相互作用，可用于设计性能增强的异质黏附剂，并为界面问题中非局域弹性的涌现机制提供见解。

Abstract: We study theoretically the peeling behavior of adhesives. Adopting a fracture mechanics approach, we derive the equation of motion of the adhesion front propagating at the interface between the adhesive and the substrate from which the peel strength is inferred. The originality of our approach lies in the description of the interplay during peeling between the stretching and the bending modes of deformation of the adhesive that is described as a F{\''o}ppl-Von Karman's thin film. Considering first a straight adhesion front, we retrieve the most salient feature of homogeneous adhesives, namely a peeling angle dependent peel strength driven by bending at large angles and by stretching at low angles. We also derive the shape of the adhesive that can be described using a single bending length scale derived from our model. We then investigate the impact of adhesion heterogeneities. We evidence that the deformations of the adhesion front are governed by a non-local interface elasticity the strength of which decreases with the peeling angle. This phenomenon reflects the transition between a stretching dominated peeling at low angle to a bending driven peeling at large angles that is captured in our model. This transition impacts the stability of adhesive fronts that relaxe more slowly from perturbations and gives rise to a stronger toughening effect in presence of a disorder distribution of adhesion energy at low peeling angles. Overall, this study sheds light on the central role played the elastic deformations of adhesives on their peeling behavior. The proposed framework unfolds the complex interplay between the deformation of adhesives and the peeling driving force that may be leveraged to engineer heterogeneous adhesives with enhanced properties. It also provides rich insights on the mechanisms underlying the emergence of non-local elasticity in interface problems.

</details>

### [11] [Fuzzy Erdos-Renyi random graphs: Spectral statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13432)
*J. A. Mendez-Bermudez*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文在随机矩阵理论（random-matrix-theory, RMT）框架下，对模糊 Erdos-Renyi（ER）随机图的谱统计量进行了系统的数值研究，涵盖无向与有向、厄米与非厄米邻接矩阵系综，并指出 ER 模型的平均度可作为其谱性质的标度参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将模糊图（fuzzy graph）的隶属度视为随机变量，从而把模糊 ER 图模型与随机矩阵理论的分析工具相衔接，以刻画由此产生的随机稀疏邻接矩阵的谱特征。

Method: 方法为：将模糊 ER 图中顶点与边的隶属度（membership values）设定为随机变量，使相应邻接矩阵（adjacency matrix）成为含随机元素的稀疏矩阵；随后对无向与有向模糊图的厄米（Hermitian）与非厄米（non-Hermitian）邻接矩阵系综，运用标准 RMT 度量分析其特征值（eigenvalues）与特征向量（eigenvectors）。

Result: 结果表明，ER 模型的平均度（average degree）可作为相应模糊图设定下谱性质的标度参数（scaling parameter）。

Conclusion: 结论为：在 RMT 数值框架下，模糊 ER 图的谱统计行为可通过平均度进行标度描述，该参数统一刻画了无向/有向及厄米/非厄米邻接矩阵系综的谱特征。

Abstract: Within a random-matrix-theory (RMT) approach, we perform a detailed numerical study of the spectral statistics of fuzzy Erdos-Renyi (ER) random graphs. Specifically, we consider the membership values of the vertices and edges of a fuzzy ER graph model as random variables. In this way, the corresponding adjacency matrices become sparse matrices with random entries. Then, we apply standard RMT measures to characterize the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of ensembles of Hermitian and non-Hermitian adjacency matrices of both undirected and directed fuzzy graphs. We demonstrate that the average degree of the ER model serves as the scaling parameter of the spectral properties of the corresponding fuzzy setups.

</details>


### [12] [On Rare Nonresonant Regions and Subdiffusive Transport in an Interacting Disordered Quantum Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13834)
*A. Scardicchio,S. L. Sondhi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作对一维无序量子自旋链中支撑亚扩散输运（subdiffusive transport）的稀有非共振区（rare nonresonant regions）进行直接数值实现，逐尺度计算非共振存活概率与算符增生。存活概率随系统尺寸至多指数衰减，其尺度分辨的失败率支持证明所需的可求和机制（summability mechanism）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期 De Roeck、Giacomin、Huveneers 与 Prosniak 关于亚扩散输运的工作依赖迭代 Schrieffer-Wolff 构造，但其证明技术性强、常数保守，缺乏独立且物理透明的检验。该研究旨在直接数值实现这一构造，以定量刻画非共振区在逐尺度流动中的存续行为。

Method: 对有限系统直接实现迭代 Schrieffer-Wolff 构造，计算无序实现（disorder realization）在流动各尺度上保持非共振的概率，同时分别监测算符增生（operator proliferation）与缀饰局域可观测量（dressed local observables）的空间局域化。将存活概率按尺度分解，提取各 Schrieffer-Wolff 步骤对应的失败率。

Result: 在数值可达范围内，存活概率随系统尺寸至多指数衰减。首个尺度的失败率由解析方法得到，与数值结果高度一致；更高尺度的失败率在所研究范围内快速下降。最小耦合下，存活概率超出严格下界（rigorous lower bound）六至七个数量级。

Conclusion: 结果支持指数稀有但参数意义下很长的非共振区所需的可求和机制，这类区域构成导致亚扩散输运的绝缘瓶颈（insulating bottlenecks）。同时表明证明中所需常数相当保守，其物理机制在可达尺度上定量可见；并展示直接数值实现可作为技术性构造性证明的独立、物理透明检验，随着机器辅助证明（machine-assisted proofs）日益普遍，该方法可能更具价值。

Abstract: We study rare nonresonant regions in a canonical one-dimensional disordered quantum spin chain by directly implementing the iterated Schrieffer-Wolff construction underlying the recent work of De Roeck, Giacomin, Huveneers and Prosniak on subdiffusive transport. For finite systems, we compute the probability that a disorder realization remains nonresonant through successive scales of the flow, while separately monitoring operator proliferation and the spatial localization of dressed local observables. The survival probability decays at most exponentially with system size over the numerically accessible regime. Resolving this decay scale by scale yields failure rates associated with successive Schrieffer-Wolff steps. The first rate is obtained analytically in excellent agreement with numerics, while higher-scale rates decrease rapidly throughout the regime studied. These results support the summability mechanism required for exponentially rare but parametrically long nonresonant regions, which provide the insulating bottlenecks responsible for subdiffusive transport. At the smallest couplings studied, the survival probability under our conditions exceeds the rigorous lower bound by six to seven orders of magnitude, demonstrating how conservative the constants required by the proof are while confirming that its underlying physical mechanism is quantitatively visible at accessible scales. More broadly, our results show how direct numerical implementations can provide an independent and physically transparent test of technically demanding constructive proofs, a methodology that may become increasingly useful as machine-assisted proofs become more common.

</details>


### [13] [OFC-like Behavior in Experimental Granular Piles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14166)
*K. Duplat,A. Douin,E. Saurety,G. Simon,E. Altshuler K. J. Måløy,O. Cochet-Escartin,O. Ramos*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 二维颗粒实验显示，随粒子间摩擦（dissipation，耗散）增大，雪崩尺寸指数从 τ=1.58 升至 1.83，而上截断仍与系统尺寸成正比，与 OFC 模型非保守区的反常特征一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为标度不变的雪崩动力学（scale-invariant avalanche dynamics）与临界性（criticality）相关并具普适指数，耗散应使系统偏离临界点并抑制大事件；OFC 模型（Olami–Feder–Christensen model）却在非保守区表现出非普适指数（可超过平均场值 τ=3/2）且大事件持续存在，作者希望检验这些反常特征是否为真实耗散系统的普遍行为。

Method: 采用二维颗粒系统（two-dimensional granular system）开展实验，通过增大粒子间摩擦从而增大耗散，测量雪崩尺寸指数与上截断随耗散的变化；并与 OFC 模型在记忆效应（memory effects）、局域动力学（local dynamics）及大事件可预测性方面进行比较。

Result: 随摩擦（耗散）增大，雪崩尺寸指数由 τ=1.58 增至 τ=1.83，上截断（upper cutoff）仍与系统尺寸成正比；实验与 OFC 模型在记忆效应、局域动力学上相似，并出现优于随机的（better-than-random）大事件可预测性。

Conclusion: 系统并非永久处于临界态，而是经历具有不同极端事件倾向的构型演化；结果表明类 OFC 动力学（OFC-like dynamics）不仅是特定模型的反常特性，也可能为理解真实耗散系统中 τ>3/2 的标度不变动力学提供相关框架。

Abstract: Scale-invariant avalanche dynamics are commonly associated with criticality and robust, universal size exponents. Dissipation is generally expected to drive the system away from the critical point, progressively suppressing large events. The Olami--Feder--Christensen (OFC) model challenges this picture: in its non-conservative regime, the avalanche-size exponent is non-universal and can exceed the mean-field value $τ=3/2$, while system-spanning events persist even at large dissipation. Here, we show that these apparently anomalous features are also observed experimentally in a two-dimensional granular system displaying scale-invariant avalanche dynamics. By increasing interparticle friction, and therefore dissipation, the avalanche-size exponent increases from $τ=1.58$ to $τ=1.83$, while the upper cutoff remains proportional to the system size. We further identify similarities between experiment and the OFC model in their memory effects, local dynamics, and the emergence of better-than-random predictability of large events. The latter indicates that the system does not remain permanently critical, but instead evolves through configurations with different propensities to generate extreme events. Our results suggest that OFC-like dynamics are not merely an anomalous feature of a particular model, but may provide a relevant framework for understanding scale-invariant dynamics with $τ>3/2$ in real dissipative systems.

</details>


### [14] [Statistical Landscape Exploration and Weak-Field Defects in Selective Oscillator Ising Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14550)
*Ömer Önder,Aydın Cem Keser*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文以 Sherrington-Kirkpatrick（SK）自旋玻璃模型为测试平台，提出含选择性淘汰与复制的振荡器伊辛机方案 OIM+，系统刻画其生成系综的统计结构。结果显示 OIM+ 能探索结构化低能区域，残留缺陷为稀疏的弱局域场缺陷，可由短程 Metropolis-Hastings 精修局部修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡器伊辛机（oscillator Ising machines, OIMs）为组合优化提供模拟动力学途径，但其生成系综（ensemble）的统计结构尚缺乏了解。作者意在填补这一空白，明确该动力学的探索能力与内在局限。

Method: 在 SK 自旋玻璃模型上引入选择性振荡器方案 OIM+，将连续退火轮次（annealing epochs）与基于能量的复制体（replica）淘汰和复种相结合。通过重叠分布（overlap distributions）、汉明距离（Hamming distances）、层次聚类（hierarchical clustering）与局域场统计对生成态进行表征，并叠加短程 Metropolis-Hastings 精修与纯 Metropolis 退火、G-set Max-Cut 基准进行对照。

Result: OIM+ 在反复选择下仍保留非平凡的构型系综并探索结构化低能区域；其特征性局限为稀疏的弱局域场残留缺陷（weak-local-field residual defects）。短程 Metropolis-Hastings 精修优先修正这些缺陷，表明振荡器动力学承担主导性的集体探索，剩余修正主要是局域的。弱场主要源于竞争性相互作用项之间的相互抵消（cancellation），而非耦合普遍偏弱；该行为在反铁磁偏置（antiferromagnetically biased）SK 系综上同样成立，混合方法达到与显著更长的纯 Metropolis 退火相当的合并平均能量。

Conclusion: 完整协议在标准 G-set Max-Cut 基准上亦取得有竞争力的性能。结果提示集体模拟搜索与定向局域精修之间存在一种自然的分工。

Abstract: Oscillator Ising machines (OIMs) provide an analog dynamical approach to combinatorial optimization, but comparatively little is known about the statistical structure of the ensembles they generate. Using the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick (SK) spin-glass model, we introduce a selective oscillator scheme, OIM+, in which successive annealing epochs are combined with energy-based elimination and repopulation of replicas. We characterize the resulting states through overlap distributions, Hamming distances, hierarchical clustering, and local-field statistics. OIM+ explores structured low-energy regions while retaining a nontrivial ensemble of configurations despite repeated selection. We identify sparse weak-local-field residual defects as a characteristic limitation of the dynamics. A short Metropolis-Hastings refinement preferentially corrects these defects, indicating that the oscillator dynamics performs the dominant collective exploration while the remaining corrections are predominantly local. The weak fields arise mainly from cancellation among competing interaction terms rather than uniformly weak couplings. The same behavior persists for an antiferromagnetically biased SK ensemble, where the hybrid method reaches a pooled mean energy comparable to a substantially longer pure Metropolis anneal. The complete protocol also achieves competitive performance on standard G-set Max-Cut benchmarks. These results suggest a natural division of labor between collective analog search and targeted local refinement.

</details>

### [15] [Superconducting Diode Effect in Josephson $\varphi_0$ Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13399)
*A. Janalizadeh,Y. M. Shukrinov,M. R. Kolahchi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在含Rashba自旋轨道耦合的铁磁弱连接Josephson结中，扩展自由能并引入高阶梯度项后，线性梯度耦合本身仅产生有限动量库珀配对而不改变临界电流对称性，其与高阶梯度项的相互作用才是Josephson二极管效应（Josephson diode effect）的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准$\varphi_0$结模型仅在弱交换场（$h \lesssim T_c$）下成立，而实际铁磁势垒通常处于$h \gg T_c$区间，此时常规Ginzburg-Landau梯度展开失效，需引入高阶梯度项以保证自由能稳定性，因此需重新审视该区间内超导二极管效应的物理机制。

Method: 在铁磁弱连接Josephson结（含Rashba自旋轨道耦合）的自由能展开中，于常规Ginzburg-Landau梯度展开基础上增加高阶梯度项，以分析线性梯度耦合与高阶梯度项的联合作用。

Result: 单独考虑线性梯度耦合时，体系出现有限动量库珀配对（finite momentum Cooper pairing），但临界电流（critical current）对称性保持不变；线性项与高阶梯度项的相互作用导致Josephson二极管效应。

Conclusion: 在强交换场区，Josephson二极管效应不能仅由线性梯度耦合解释，而源自线性梯度项与高阶梯度项的相互竞争，表明高阶梯度项在该区间的自由能描述中不可或缺。

Abstract: We investigate the superconducting diode effect in a Josephson junction with a ferromagnetic weak link in the presence of Rashba spin-orbit coupling. While the standard $\varphi_0$ junction model is valid only for weak exchange fields $h \lesssim T_c$, realistic ferromagnetic barriers typically operate in the regime $h \gg T_c$, where the conventional Ginzburg-Landau gradient expansion becomes insufficient and requires the inclusion of higher order gradient terms to ensure stability of the free energy. By extending the free energy with such higher order term, we demonstrate that the linear gradient coupling alone leads to finite momentum Cooper pairing while leaving the critical current symmetry unchanged. The interplay between this linear term and the higher order gradient one leads to the Josephson diode effect.

</details>


### [16] [Disorder-driven transport in ZrN thin films grown by ion-beam-assisted sputtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13568)
*Maximo DiPreta,Samuel Jiang,Serhii Kruhlov,Yi Li,Valentine Novosad,Tomas Polakovic*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过改变氮分压、溅射功率与沉积温度，实现离子束辅助溅射（IBAS）生长氮化锆（ZrN）薄膜从无序金属/超导向绝缘输运的可控调控，并揭示局域化物理与超导能标的解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究生长条件对ZrN薄膜结构与电子输运性质的调控能力，并检验无序驱动局域化与超导电性之间的关系，以建立可调平台研究金属导电、电子局域化与超导之间的交叉行为。

Method: 采用直流离子束辅助溅射（dc ion-beam assisted sputtering, IBAS）在不同氮分压、溅射功率与沉积温度下生长ZrN薄膜，并进行XRD结构表征、低温输运与磁输运测量，用三维Mott变程跳跃（3D Mott variable-range hopping, VRH）及相边界拟合分析数据。

Result: 增加氮流量导致方块电阻升高、超导转变温度（Tc）被抑制并转为非金属导电，而XRD显示长程结晶性无明显变化。绝缘区低温输运符合3D Mott VRH，特征温度T0随方块电阻在四个数量级范围内单调增大；但Tc与T0无系统依赖关系。磁输运既不满足标准BCS模型，也不符合脏极限二类Werthamer-Helfand-Hohenberg模型，相边界可用自由指数幂律描述，n≈3.47，外推μ0Hc2(0)≈6.4 T，相干长度为7 nm。

Conclusion: IBAS生长的ZrN可作为宽范围可调平台，用于研究无序驱动输运及金属导电、电子局域化与超导之间的交叉；结果显示局域化物理与超导能标存在解耦。

Abstract: We investigate the structural and electronic transport properties of zirconium nitride (ZrN) thin films grown by dc ion-beam assisted sputtering (IBAS) as a function of nitrogen partial pressure, sputtering power and deposition temperature. Variation of growth conditions enables controlled tuning from disordered metallic and superconducting behavior to insulating transport. Increasing nitrogen flow drives an increase in sheet resistance, suppression of superconducting transition temperature, and non-metallic conduction. XRD shows no measurable change in long range crystallinity or order. In the insulating regime, low-temperature transport is well described by 3D Mott variable-range hopping (VRH), with the characteristic temperature $T_0$ increasing monotonically with sheet resistance over four orders of magnitude. Despite this strong correlation between $T_0$ and disorder, $T_c$ exhibits no systematic dependence on $T_0$, indicating a decoupling between localization physics and the superconducting energy scale. Magnetotransport measurements are not well described by the standard BCS model nor the dirty type-II Werthamer-Helfand-Hohenberg model. The phase boundary is instead captured by a free-exponent power law, $μ_0 H_{c2}(T) = μ_0 H_{c2}(0)[1 - (T/T_c)^{n}]$ with $n \approx 3.47$, and extrapolates to $μ_0 H_{c2}(0) \approx 6.4$ T. The films have an extracted coherence length of 7 nm. These results establish IBAS-grown ZrN as a broadly tunable platform for investigating disorder-driven transport and the crossover between metallic conduction, electronic localization, and superconductivity.

</details>

### [17] [Black Holes, Gravitational Waves and Space-Time Singularities Lemaitre Conference 2024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13266)
*Massimo Bianchi,Sergio Luigi Cacciatori,Matteo Galaverni,Gabriele Gionti,Fabio Scardigli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该社论介绍了 Lemaitre 会议 2024 的专题文集，汇集十九篇论文，聚焦宇宙学、引力与量子理论交叉领域的前沿开放问题，并以 Lemaitre 的科学遗产为统一主线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会议旨在纪念 Georges Lemaitre 的科学遗产，召开于其逝世五十七年后、及其 1927 年膨胀宇宙论文百年诞辰前夕。文集动机在于梳理宇宙学、引力与量子理论交界处最紧迫的开放问题。

Method: 该文为社论（editorial）性质的导言，采用专题文集（topical collection）的组织方式，将十九篇贡献按主题归类，涵盖 $Λ$CDM、暴胀、弦论与超引力建模、Swampland 约束、量子宇宙学波函数、奇点处理、原初黑洞、量子引力与视界热力学等方向。

Result: 文集收录的研究涉及哈勃张力（Hubble tension）与 DESI 数据下 $Λ$CDM 的前景、弦论与超引力中的暗能量建模、量子宇宙学波函数的观测者依赖性、时空奇点（spacetime singularities）的消除或一致穿越、原初黑洞及其引力波信号、背景无关的量子引力表述与视界热力学、以及引力情境下的量子—经典跃迁。

Conclusion: 文集以 Lemaitre 的遗产为反复出现的统一线索，他最早区分坐标奇点与物理奇点并提出“视界（horizon）”一词；其开创性视野持续指引宇宙学与基础物理前沿研究。文集亦包含月球宇宙学观测提案与 Lemaitre 原初原子假说起源的历史重建。

Abstract: This editorial introduces the topical collection arising from the Lemaitre Conference 2024 - the second in a series of meetings dedicated to the scientific legacy of Georges Lemaitre - held at the Vatican Observatory in Castel Gandolfo, 17-20 June 2024, fifty-seven years after Lemaitre's death and on the eve of the centenary of his foundational 1927 paper on the expanding universe. The nineteen contributions collected here address the most pressing open problems at the interface of cosmology, gravitation, and quantum theory: the Hubble tension and the future of $Λ$CDM in light of recent DESI data; inflationary cosmology and dark-energy model-building in string theory and supergravity, together with the Swampland constraints that bound them; the observer-dependence of the quantum-cosmological wave function; the removal, or consistent crossing, of spacetime singularities - from the canonical quantization of Lemaitre's own 1933 dust model to string-theoretic pre-Big-Bang bounces and many-body constructions of singularity-free black-hole cores; primordial black holes and their gravitational-wave signatures; the search for a background-independent formulation of quantum gravity and a first-principles account of horizon thermodynamics; and the foundational status of the quantum-to-classical transition in gravitational contexts. A proposal for lunar-based cosmological observations, and a historical reconstruction of the genesis of Lemaitre's primeval-atom hypothesis, complete the collection. A recurring unifying thread was the legacy of Lemaitre himself - who first distinguished coordinate from physical singularities and coined the term "horizon" - whose pioneering vision continues to guide research at the frontier of cosmology and fundamental physics.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波去噪需覆盖全部自旋、进动双星多样性，因为恢复的波形是参数估计、广义相对论检验与族群研究的基础。匹配滤波（matched filtering）在下一代探测器事件率提升后成本过高，而现有深度学习方法多局限于狭窄参数空间，难以进行有原则的比较与可靠部署。

Method: 在统一条件下训练五种神经网络架构，覆盖完整的天体物理自旋双黑洞参数空间，进行首个受控去噪比较。提出多尺度频率感知（Multi-Scale Frequency-Aware）架构，按频率区间设置并行分支以对应并合信号的频谱解剖；并基于去噪残差构建族群级不确定度带、验证其校准。

Result: 多尺度频率感知架构以更少参数实现最佳保真度，超越更大规模模型。该网络无需再训练即可从模拟训练泛化到真实 LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA 数据，尽管仅用单台探测器的模拟噪声训练，仍能跨三个观测运行期、两台探测器恢复已确认事件的并合形态。在质量比扩展与类层级并合遗迹自旋族群上通过压力测试；在无已知信号的真实噪声上输出几乎处处被抑制，少数例外源于噪声瞬变。

Conclusion: 匹配网络结构与并合频谱解剖是优于蛮力扩展的统一原则。该统计量可区分信号与噪声，为未来探测研究提供动机；公开的权重为后续扩展提供了可部署、可复现的基准。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave denoising must handle the full diversity of spinning, precessing binaries, since the recovered waveform underpins parameter estimation, tests of general relativity, and population studies. Matched filtering achieves this at a cost that becomes prohibitive as next-generation detectors push event rates higher; deep learning offers real-time reconstruction, but current methods are developed on narrow parameter spaces, precluding principled comparison and reliable deployment. We present the first controlled comparison of five neural-network architectures for gravitational-wave denoising, trained identically across the full astrophysically-motivated spinning binary-black-hole parameter space. A unifying principle emerges: matching network structure to the spectral anatomy of a coalescence -- inspiral, merger, ringdown -- outperforms brute-force model scaling. Our Multi-Scale Frequency-Aware architecture embodies this via dedicated parallel branches per frequency regime, achieving the best fidelity while using fewer parameters than larger models. It generalizes from simulated training to real LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA data without retraining, recovering merger morphology across confirmed events spanning three observing runs and both detectors, despite training on a single detector's simulated noise. We construct population-level uncertainty bands from denoising residuals, validate their calibration, and stress-test the framework on extended mass ratios and a spin population resembling hierarchical-merger remnants. Applied to real noise with no known signal, the network suppresses its output almost everywhere, with rare exceptions traced to noise transients rather than a general weakness -- indicating the statistic discriminates signal from noise and motivating a future detection study. Released weights give a deployable, reproducible benchmark for future extensions.

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the thermodynamic consistency of the apparent horizon in effective loop quantum cosmology (LQC). By retaining the standard definitions of horizon thermodynamic quantities, we find that the modified LQC dynamics leads to a departure from the classical equilibrium thermodynamic description. This deviation can be naturally described by a nonequilibrium formulation characterized by an internal entropy production term. The resulting entropy production consistently recovers the classical and de Sitter limits. These results suggest that the apparent horizon in effective LQC admits a coherent nonequilibrium thermodynamic interpretation.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于 Kerr 度规（Kerr metric）解析计算由透镜横向运动所导致的引力透镜相对频移 Δν/ν，将先前仅考虑横向速度的 Schwarzschild 情形扩展至含自旋参数的框架。

Result: 推导给出 Kerr 度规下由横向运动引起的相对频移表达式，该效应可用于测量透镜的横向速度或其自旋参数，从而为缓解微引力透镜中自旋参数的简并提供新的观测手段。

Conclusion: 近期微引力透镜观测已将黑洞识别为可行的透镜候选体，而星系中心超大质量黑洞的观测为通过测量透镜源频移来检验 Kerr 度规的观测效应提供了理想实验室。

Abstract: In this study, we calculate the relative frequency shift ($Δν/ ν$) caused by gravitational lensing resulting from the transverse motion of a spinning lens described by the Kerr metric \cite{Kerr:1963ud}. While the effect of transverse velocity on the frequency shift has previously been investigated using the Schwarzschild metric \cite{Rahvar_2020,10.1093/mnras/stae1938}, extending this to the Kerr metric provides a new window for measuring the transverse velocity of a lens or its spin parameter, a parameter often subject to degeneracy in gravitational microlensing observations. Recent microlensing observations have revealed black holes as viable lensing candidates \cite{2022ApJ...933...83S}. Since spinning black holes form naturally from stellar evolution, astrophysical black hole lenses are best described by the Kerr metric. Furthermore, recent observations of supermassive black holes at the centers of galaxies provide an ideal laboratory for testing the observational effects of the Kerr metric by measuring the frequency shifts of lensed sources.

</details>

*Wen-Xiang Chen*

TL;DR: 该工作将已知的量子Oppenheimer–Snyder（qOS）黑洞外部的局部正则热力学结果置于显式的、经过正则化处理的正则系综（canonical ensemble）框架中，识别出两个鞍点并在其合并处解释热容发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有qOS热力学分析已报告对数熵项与热容发散，但缺乏一个透明且明确界定适用范围的正则系综组织框架。本文意在填补这一表述层面的空白，而非提出新的黑洞解或奇点消解机制。

Method: 采用文献中已有的qOS时空，在固定量子参数α下将外视界改写为无量纲形式，导出质量、霍金温度（Hawking temperature）、熵与热容的精确表达式；随后将其纳入带有正则化的正则系综（canonical ensemble）框架，通过非壳层正则作用量（off-shell canonical action）分析鞍点结构。

Result: 非壳层正则作用量在近极端支（near-extremal branch）上具有正热容的局部极小值，在大黑洞支（large-black-hole branch）上具有负热容的局部极大值；两个鞍点在 r_h/√α=√((4+2√7)/3) 处合并，此处温度达到最大且热容发散。所得表达式重现了此前的对数熵项与热容发散。

Conclusion: 由于渐近平坦的配分函数（partition function）在缺少边界数据时不可归一化，该发散被解释为Davies型局部稳定性转变（Davies-type local stability transition），而非自身的全局一阶相变。本文主要贡献是对已知qOS热力学结果给出透明的正则鞍点组织，并精确陈述其适用域与局限。

Abstract: We study the local canonical thermodynamics of the quantum Oppenheimer--Snyder (qOS) black-hole exterior previously obtained from loop quantum cosmology. The spacetime is adopted from the literature; no new black-hole solution, regular extension, or singularity-resolution mechanism is claimed here. At fixed quantum parameter $α$, we rewrite the outer horizon in a dimensionless form and obtain exact expressions for the mass, Hawking temperature, entropy, and heat capacity. These expressions reproduce the logarithmic entropy term and the heat-capacity divergence reported in earlier analyses. We then place those results in an explicit, regulated canonical-ensemble framework. The off-shell canonical action has a local minimum on a near-extremal branch with positive heat capacity and a local maximum on a large-black-hole branch with negative heat capacity. The two saddles merge at $r_h/\sqrtα=\sqrt{(4+2\sqrt{7})/3}$, where the temperature is maximal and the heat capacity diverges. Because the asymptotically flat partition function is not normalizable without boundary data, this divergence is interpreted as a Davies-type local stability transition, not by itself as a global first-order phase transition. The principal contribution of this work is therefore a transparent canonical-saddle organization of known qOS thermodynamic results, together with a precise statement of its domain and limitations.

</details>

*Robert B. Mann,Behnam Pourhassan,Ali Dehghani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文汇集证据表明，渐近反德西特（AdS）与德西特（dS）黑洞的逆等周不等式（RII，${\cal R}_\text{RII} \ge 1$）具根本重要性，其破坏会导致热力学不稳定、非物理解或裸奇点；超熵（superentropic）黑洞违反RII且不满足力学与热稳定性要求，作者据此提出多项猜想并将其推广至dS空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆等周不等式（reverse isoperimetric inequality, RII）此前被猜想对渐近AdS与dS黑洞成立，但其地位与违反后果尚缺系统论证。该文旨在说明RII的基本重要性，并厘清违反RII（即超熵黑洞）所对应的物理含义。

Method: 以热力学稳定性判据为工具，检验违反RII的超熵黑洞是否满足力学稳定性要求 ${κ_T} \geqslant {κ_S} \geqslant 0$ 与热稳定性要求 ${C_P} \geqslant {C_V} \geqslant 0$；并通过构造dS空间中的首个超熵实例、考察AdS扩展黑洞热力学（extended black hole thermodynamics）的 $Λ\to 0$ 极限来进行论证。

Conclusion: 逆等周不等式的违反与热力学不稳定、非物理解或裸奇点相联系，因而RII具有基础性意义；超熵黑洞因同时违背力学与热稳定性判据而整体热力学不稳定，该结论被推广至dS空间。此外，作者猜想超熵黑洞无渐近平坦极限，并提出基于ADM质量与热力学体积的“热体积不等式”（thermo-volumetric inequality）作为更强的渐近平坦不等式，同时指出PII弱于RII。

Abstract: A collection of evidence is presented showing that the conjectured reverse isoperimetric inequality (RII) for asymptotically anti-de Sitter (AdS) as well as de Sitter (dS) black holes, ${\cal R}_\text{RII} \ge 1$, is of fundamental importance and its violation (${\cal R}_\text{RII} < 1$) gives rise to either thermodynamic instabilities, or physically unreasonable solutions, or naked singularities. We show that AdS black holes violating reverse isoperimetric inequality, known as superentropic black holes, satisfy neither mechanical stability requirement of ${κ_T} \geqslant {κ_S} \geqslant 0$ nor thermal stability requirement of ${C_P} \geqslant {C_V} \geqslant 0$. This property makes them thermodynamically unstable for the whole range of parameter space and explains all the diverse behaviors so far reported. We conjecture this statement holds for all superentropic black holes. We confirm that there is no counterexample to the thermodynamic instability conjecture of superentropic black holes and extend this to dS space by presenting the first example of superentropicity in dS space. By bringing evidence, we then propose two other conjectures: 1) there is no asymptotically flat limit of superentropic black holes, and 2) there exist two stronger versions of Penrose isoperimetric inequality (PII) for asymptotically flat black holes; the first is the asymptotically flat limit of the RII and the second is a new inequality in terms of the ADM mass and the thermodynamics volume, that we call thermo-volumetric inequality, obtained via insights from the $Λ\to 0$ limit of extended black hole thermodynamics in AdS. We show that the Penrose isoperimetric inequality (PII) is weaker than the reverse isoperimetric inequality (RII), as demonstrated explicitly for $D=4,5$ and for all black hole families we have examined.

</details>

*G. Alencar,Kirill A. Bronnikov,T. M. Crispim,Diego Sáez-Chillón Gómez,Marcos V. de S. Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出一种由无质量虫洞种子构造有质量黑洞反弹（black bounce）几何的通用框架，并将其应用于广义 Ellis-Bronnikov 虫洞，得到广义 Bardeen 型时空族。研究揭示其轨道与光环结构的丰富性，并借助 EHT 对 Sgr A* 的观测给出参数约束，同时指出在现有分辨率下与标准黑洞存在强观测简并。

A recent object-relative, Lorentz-invariant weighting of internal electromagnetic modes renders selected one-loop contributions of quantum electrodynamics finite without counterterms. Applied to linearized gravity, the graviton self-coupling reaches order unity at the Planck wave number, so that the mode content available to a loop is suppressed beyond it. Any suppression exceeding a threshold fixed by the diagram renders the one-loop integrals finite; no cutoff is imposed and no mode structure above the Planck scale need be derived. For pure gravity the same power counting extends to every loop order, and the leading suppression already meets the resulting condition for every topology with more than one vertex; a single class of single-vertex insertions is excluded. </details>

Abstract: We consider collisions between particles moving freely in the Schwarzschild background. We suggest classification of scenarios that lead to unbounded energy output in the center of mass frame. Also, we scrutinize collisions in the vicinity of the bifurcation point and reveal the role of resting observers in the inner region beyond the horizon. In some scenarios $E_{c.m.}$ not only diverges but becomes seemingly infinite that is, as a matter of fact, impossible according to the principle of so-called kinematic censorship. We resolve a corresponding paradox and argue, why this principle remains valid.

This model also meets the conditions for the Universe to reach thermodynamic equilibrium in the far future. Additionally, we investigate the halo mass function and cluster number counts within this modified gravity framework. All findings are compared to the fiducial $Λ$CDM profile, indicating that the added entropic correction affects the history of expansion, the growth rate of structures, and the dark matter halo abundance. We find that the more massive structures are less abundant and develop at later times, which aligns with the hierarchical model of formation of large-scale structures.

Result: 两种最先进模型总体一致，但约20%的事件在至少一个一维边缘后验（one-dimensional marginal posterior）上存在可见差异；旧模型在质量谱（mass spectrum）中若干关键事件上常给出定性不同的结论。此外，发现已发表的 LVK GWTC-2.1 SEOBNRv4PHM 结果及 GW200105 后续重处理中存在一处缺陷（bug）：在所采用的采样率下，模型的奈奎斯特频率（Nyquist-frequency）限制排除了部分允许参数空间，对七个低质量事件人为截断了偏离等质量（equal mass）的质量比后验（mass-ratio posterior）。

Conclusion: 刻画引力波源性质的不确定性需要同时使用多个最先进波形模型并采用经过仔细验证的分析设置；同时应关注已发表结果的潜在代码或设置缺陷。

Previous analyses of GWTC-3 event candidates using SEOBNRv4PHM, IMRPhenomXPHM, and NRSur7dq4 have sometimes arrived at notably different conclusions about event properties. Subtle analysis settings and code differences can also produce notable differences. We revisit 60 publicly available events from the first three observing runs, released across GWTC-1, GWTC-2, GWTC-2.1, and GWTC-3, using a consistent analysis framework and three waveform models: two state-of-the-art time-domain models with higher-order modes (SEOBNRv5PHM and IMRPhenomTPHM) and one older frequency-domain model without higher-order modes (IMRPhenomPv2). The two state-of-the-art models agree well overall, although about 20\% of events have a visible difference in at least one one-dimensional marginal posterior. By contrast, the older model often arrives at qualitatively different conclusions for key events across the mass spectrum. We also identify a bug in published LVK GWTC-2.1 SEOBNRv4PHM results and in a later reprocessing of GW200105. At the adopted sampling rates, the model's Nyquist-frequency restriction excluded part of the allowed parameter space and artificially truncated the mass-ratio posterior away from equal mass for seven low-mass events. These results emphasize the need to use multiple state-of-the-art waveform models and carefully validated analysis settings to characterize uncertainty in gravitational-wave source properties.

</details>

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在宇宙探测器（Cosmic Explorer）中，使用贝叶斯方法可无污染地探测信噪比为5的高斯宇宙学随机引力波背景，但波形系统误差会在低频（<20 Hz）引入偏差，导致对随机背景参数的错误推断。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代引力波天文台（如宇宙探测器）为探测宇宙学随机引力波背景提供了独特机会，可洞察其他手段无法触及的早期宇宙。研究需检验在存在可分辨的双黑洞并合非高斯前景时，能否准确测量高斯宇宙学背景。

Result: 在一天模拟数据中，成功以信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）为5恢复高斯宇宙学背景，且无未分辨信号污染或数据排除。但引入波形系统误差后，随机背景参数推断出现偏差，甚至在本无背景时错误推断存在背景；偏差主要由低频（<20 Hz）信号失配驱动，尤其是恢复波形缺失注入信号所含物理效应时。

Conclusion: 研究提出可用于诊断测量是否受波形系统误差严重偏差的指标。结果表明，要在下一代探测器（next-generation detectors）中准确恢复原初随机引力波背景，仍需改进波形建模（waveform modeling）或采用其他缓解技术。

Abstract: Next-generation gravitational-wave observatories like Cosmic Explorer will provide an unprecedented opportunity to probe the cosmological stochastic gravitational-wave background, providing insight into the early Universe that is inaccessible with other means. We investigate the prospects for measuring a Gaussian cosmological gravitational-wave background in Cosmic Explorer in the presence of a distinguishable, non-Gaussian foreground of binary black hole mergers, using a Bayesian search that simultaneously fits binary black hole and background parameters. We successfully recover a Gaussian cosmological background with a signal-to-noise ratio of 5 in one day of simulated data in Cosmic Explorer without contamination from unresolved signals or data exclusion. However, when introducing waveform systematic errors to our analysis, our inference on the stochastic background parameters becomes biased and can falsely infer the presence of a background when there is none. The bias is driven by signal mismatches at low frequencies (<20 Hz), especially when the recovery waveform is missing physical effects included in the injected signals. While we propose a metric for diagnosing if measurements will be strongly biased by waveform systematics, our results suggest improvements in waveform modeling or other mitigation techniques will be required to accurately recover a primordial stochastic gravitational-wave background in next-generation detectors.

</details>

*Rishabh V. More*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出以线性响应中 Kramers-Kronig 关系的残差作为无模型指标，用于判别触变性（thixotropy）与黏弹性（viscoelasticity）材料在扫描过程中是否处于非平稳状态，并在八个模型及已发表的二氧化硅悬浮液谱上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用单次 chirp（啁啾）激励获得的谱与 Kramers-Kronig 关系之间的残差，且不引入任何模型（model-free），以此作为非平稳性的度量；并检验该残差在小振幅极限、平稳状态及有界重构（bounded restructuring）条件下的行为。

Conclusion: Kramers-Kronig 残差可作为区分触变性与黏弹性的无模型判据，并可用于从实验谱中定位结构时间尺度。

Abstract: Thixotropic and viscoelastic materials both retain their deformation history and are difficult to distinguish. However, a stationary linear response obeys the Kramers-Kronig relations. A structure that evolves during a sweep renders the spectrum inconsistent with them, so the residual from one chirp and no model measures nonstationarity. It survives the small-amplitude limit, returns to its baseline at any stationary state, and, for bounded restructuring, peaks near the structural time. We verify this on eight models and demonstrate it on published spectra of a thixotropic silica suspension.

</details>

Conclusion: 雪崩未必弛豫受驱无序材料，而可将应力重新分配至更高承载构型；该机制可能对实验室断层与天然断层的物理研究具有相关性。

Abstract: Slowly sheared granular materials generally store mechanical energy and dilate between abrupt failures that release energy and compact the material. Most strikingly, some avalanches both dilate the layer and increase the elastic energy transmitted to the resisting boundary. These events reorganize force chains beyond the shear band and produce a distinct acoustic response. The results show that an avalanche need not relax a driven disordered material; it can instead redistribute stress into a more highly loaded configuration, a mechanism with potential relevance to the physics of both laboratory and natural faults.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米颗粒可显著补强弹性体，却矛盾地在较低应变下引发空化；这一现象的微观起源历经数十年研究仍未定论，故本文旨在揭示其机制。

Result: 纳米颗粒网络的初始堵塞（jamming）导致基体内负压（negative pressure）积累，在补强同时逼近空化极限；该危机最初通过颗粒网络的屈服（yield）得以避免，但随后的各向异性再堵塞（anisotropic rejamming）事件引发失控的负压积累，最终导致空化与失效。

Conclusion: 结果确认纳米颗粒网络诱导的热力学空化（thermodynamic cavitation）是弹性体纳米复合材料中空洞（void）形成的根源，并表明填料的集体动力学（collective filler dynamics）可作为控制极限失效的潜在切入点。

Abstract: Nanoparticles can dramatically reinforce elastomers while paradoxically causing cavitation at lower strains. Despite decades of research, the microscopic origin of this behavior has remained unsettled. Here, molecular dynamics simulations reveal that cavitation and failure arise from the nanoparticulate reinforcement mechanism itself. Initial jamming of the nanoparticulate network leads to a buildup of negative pressure in the elastomer matrix, reinforcing it and simultaneously driving it towards a cavitation limit. This crisis is initially averted by yield of the particle network. However, an anisotropic rejamming event of the nanoparticles ultimately drives a runaway negative pressure buildup that leads to cavitation and failure. These results identify nanoparticle-network-induced thermodynamic cavitation as the origin of void formation in elastomeric nanocomposites, and they establish collective filler dynamics as a potential point of control of ultimate failure.

</details>

*Gregory Kozyreff,Lucie Domino,Basile Radisson,Hadrien Bense*

TL;DR: 该研究证明，加速旋转夹持端可使屈曲条带（buckled strip）在低于鞍结分岔（saddle-node bifurcation）阈值的条件下发生跳变（snap-through），并给出能量判据与临界慢化（critical slowing down）标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跳变通常由控制参数越过分岔阈值触发，但实际加载往往以有限速率进行；本文旨在探究加载速率如何改变有效跳变阈值。

Method: 对一个夹持端旋转的屈曲条带，结合实验、理论与数值模拟，分析其鞍结分岔行为与动力学响应。

Result: 当驱动时间短于准静态过程但长于基本弯曲周期时，条带大部分加载过程接近瞬时准静态平衡，可据此导出能量判据，给出远低于鞍结分岔点的动态失稳阈值；在接近分岔点时，准静态平衡演化出现几何放大（geometric boost），使更慢的驱动即可引发跳变，并基于该放大与最不稳定振动模式的临界慢化导出不同标度律。

Conclusion: 有限速率加载可在准静态失稳阈值到达前触发跳变，揭示了速率调控跳变阈值的一般机制。

Abstract: Snap-through is a sudden and large mechanical deformation that is usually triggered by driving a control parameter beyond a bifurcation threshold. Here, we study a buckled strip that undergoes a saddle-node bifurcation as one of its clamps is rotated. We show experimentally, theoretically, and numerically that the effective snapping threshold can be markedly lowered by speeding up the rotation. For actuation times that are short but nevertheless longer than the fundamental flexural period, the strip is observed to remain close to the instantaneous quasi-static equilibrium for most of the loading process. This allows us to derive an energetic criterion that accurately estimates a dynamic instability threshold well below the saddle-node bifurcation point. On the other hand, very near the bifurcation point, the evolution of the quasi-static equilibrium undergoes a geometric boost, which entrains the general motion of the beam. As a result, a slower actuation of the clamp than predicted by the energy argument suffices to produce snap-through. A distinct scaling is derived in that limit, based on the aforementioned boost and critical slowing down of the least stable vibration mode of the beam. More broadly, our results show how finite-rate forcing can trigger snap-through before the quasi-static instability threshold is reached

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 2d 维标准系统（canonical systems）在半正定（positive semidefinite）哈密顿量 H(t) 且迹（trace）恒等于 1 的约束下，探讨其 H-可积解的存在性与解空间结构。

Method: 以半正定哈密顿量 H(t) 及条件 tr H(t) ≡ 1 为设定，对系统的 H-可积解（H-integrable solution）进行分析，并考察其能量关于 H 保持有限的性质。

Conclusion: 在 tr H(t) ≡ 1 的半正定哈密顿量条件下，2d 维标准系统的 H-可积解具有存在性保证，且其解空间维数受 d 限制。

Abstract: In this paper we study $2d$-dimensional canonical systems with positive semidefinite Hamiltonians $H(t)$ satisfying $\operatorname{tr} H(t) \equiv 1$. We first establish that every such system possesses at least one nontrivial $H$-integrable solution, ensuring the existence of solutions whose energy with respect to $H$ remains finite. We then show that the space of all $H$-integrable solutions has dimension at most $d$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将 Winfree 型振荡模型拓展到高阶形式，以刻画振荡联想记忆（oscillatory associative memory）的存储性能，并借助局部稳定性（local stability）这一判据理解记忆容量受串扰噪声（crosstalk noise）限制时系统状态的演化规律。

Result: 结果表明，随着记忆负载（memory load）增加，串扰噪声驱动系统发生铁磁（ferromagnetic）、混合（mixed）与自旋玻璃（spin-glass）相变的级联；两种 Winfree 模型的相边界被显式导出，所提出的辅助场调制方案可针对不同相区工作，电路设计与数值模拟结果支持上述理论预测。

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普适性依赖结合具有不同代数性质的算子，而这些性质取决于编码信息的希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）的物理结构。全同对称粒子系统可映射为角动量系统，从而用旋转与二次自旋压缩相互作用实现普适控制，但该框架下的代数普适性此前存在更强（且可能有误）的断言，需加以澄清与修正。

Conclusion: 论文修正了先前关于代数生成的过强主张，确立了抽象动力学李代数的精确结构，并据此导出实用的二次普适性判据（degree-two universality criterion），同时讨论了有限维 SSRC 普适性。整体上澄清了固定扇区普适性与抽象代数普适性之间的关系。

Abstract: Universality relies on combining operators with different algebraic properties, which depend on the physical structure of the Hilbert space encoding the information. Systems of identical symmetric particles---including bosons and symmetric collections of spin-$1/2$ particles---can be mapped to angular-momentum systems, where rotations supplemented by quadratic, spin-squeezing interactions provide universal control. We revisit universality in this framework and confirm that such linear and quadratic operations provide universal control on every fixed-particle-number subspace. At the abstract level, however, we show that the dynamical Lie algebra generated by all degree-at-most-two elements does not exhaust the full universal enveloping algebra of $\mathfrak{su}_{\mathbb{R}}(2)$, contrary to a stronger claim made previously: higher powers of the quadratic Casimir element cannot be generated by commutators and linear combinations. This obstruction does not conflict with fixed-sector universality because, under a fixed irreducible spin-$n/2$ representation, all powers of the Casimir element act as scalar multiples of the identity. We determine the resulting abstract dynamical Lie algebras exactly and show that the central-free and full degree-at-most-two algebras map, respectively, onto $\mathfrak{su}_{\mathbb{R}}(n+1)$ and $\mathfrak{u}_{\mathbb{R}}(n+1)$, establishing special-unitary and full-unitary universality. </details>

Result: 对所有整数 q≥2、ℓ≥1，证明任务在 (q−1)ℓ 为偶数时恰需 n 条消息，为奇数时需两条消息；偶宇称情形确立了已有纠缠辅助协议的最优性，奇宇称情形给出无需纠缠、仅用一位（one bit）即达最优的显式确定性协议。

Conclusion: 偶宇称情形解决了 Cao 等人关于平衡循环广义 Hadamard 图（balanced cyclic generalized Hadamard graphs）的猜想 6.3 的谱断言；结合显式的奇宇称二分（bipartition），确定量子色数在偶宇称下为 n、奇宇称下为 2，而经典色数（classical chromatic number）同样为 2。全部引理、定理与推论均在 Lean 中形式化验证。

Abstract: Entanglement can reduce the communication required for coding tasks, but establishing the minimum achievable cost is essential to understanding its limits. We address this question in a zero-error source-coding task where Alice receives a word and Bob knows an unordered pair of candidates containing it. Alice does not know the pair and must enable Bob to identify her word without error using shared entanglement and one classical message. The candidates satisfy a balanced-difference promise: for words in $\mathbb{Z}_q^n$ with $n=q\ell$, each residue modulo $q$ occurs exactly $\ell$ times in their coordinatewise difference. For all integers $q\geq2$ and $\ell\geq1$, we prove that the task requires exactly $n$ messages when $(q-1)\ell$ is even and two messages when it is odd. These minima allow arbitrary finite-dimensional shared states independent of the inputs and arbitrary local measurements. In even parity, this establishes optimality of an existing entanglement-assisted protocol. In odd parity, an explicit deterministic protocol achieves the optimum of one bit without entanglement. Our proof combines Fourier analysis with a combinatorial counting argument to determine the smallest eigenvalue of the associated graphs. In even parity, this resolves the spectral assertion of Cao et al.'s Conjecture 6.3 for balanced cyclic generalized Hadamard graphs. Together with an explicit odd-parity bipartition, this determines the quantum chromatic number as $n$ in even parity and $2$ in odd parity, where the classical chromatic number is also $2$. All lemmas, theorems, and corollaries are formalized and verified in Lean.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a general mechanism for engineering moment-selective temporal designs with hierarchically structured aperiodic drives.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画心肌梗死（cardiac infarction）后心肌的损伤过程，尤其是被动组织（passive tissue）负荷增大所导致的胶原（collagen）损伤。

Method: 从变分原理（variational principles）出发，推导控制损伤不可逆演化的准静态微分模型（quasi-static differential model），并将其应用于两个测试算例。

Result: 在矩形被动平板（passive slab）压头载荷算例中验证模型；与左心室电力学（left ventricular electromechanics）模型耦合后，结果显示梗死区负荷重分布可产生与实验一致的胶原支架（collagen scaffold）损伤。

Conclusion: 结果表明被动、依赖载荷的耗散现象（load-dependent dissipative phenomena）参与梗死后重构（post-infarction remodelling）。

Abstract: We propose a dissipative, irreversible damage model for anisotropic media in large deformations to address the damage process of the myocardium following a cardiac infarction. We model damage to the collagen in the cleavage planes resulting from an increased load to the passive tissue. Starting from variational principles, we derive a quasi-static differential model governing the irreversible evolution of the damage. We apply our model to two test cases. First, a rectangular passive slab geometry to which an indenter-like load is applied. Then, we couple our model with a model for the numerical simulation of left ventricular electromechanics. Our results show that a redistribution of the load within a left ventricle occurring following a cardiac infarction can produce the damage to the collagen scaffold in the infarcted area consistent with experimental results. This is indicative of the fact that passive load-dependent dissipative phenomena are implicated in post-infarction remodelling.

</details>

Result: 3C 66B 与 Mrk 501 很可能从引力波背景中凸显，具备单源搜寻价值；强环境硬化模型相比纯引力波驱动模型预言更多可分辨双星；基于相同种群拟合，SKAO 预计可分辨数十个双星，其中 3C 66B 可稳健探测。

Abstract: We assess whether electromagnetically identified supermassive black hole (SMBH) binary candidates populate the rare, high-amplitude tail of the gravitational wave (GW) amplitude distribution. Using an SMBH binary population model fitted to the NANOGrav 15-year data, we find that 3C~66B and Mrk~501 are likely to stand out from the GW background, making them promising targets for individual-source searches. Furthermore, we show that individual detections can probe the binary hardening mechanism, as models with strong environmental hardening predict a higher abundance of individually resolvable binaries than purely GW-driven models. Applying the same population fits and accounting for the confusion noise from the unresolved population, we forecast that the Square Kilometre Array Observatory (SKAO) will resolve tens of individual binaries, with 3C~66B robustly detectable.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文研究一种曲率耦合暗能量（curvature-coupled dark energy）模型，通过非最小耦合（non-minimal coupling）的quintessence场与Ricci标量耦合，可同时改变复合前（pre-recombination）与晚期加速阶段的宇宙演化。在选定参数下，模型对膨胀历史、物质成团与引力势谱产生百分之几十量级的偏离，并在弱透镜与积分Sachs-Wolfe效应中留下特征信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于构建可同时缓解H_0张力（H_0 tension）并提供晚期暗能量行为的标量-张量（scalar-tensor）模型，且需满足局域引力约束与今日标准引力的恢复要求。

Method: 采用quintessence场与Ricci标量非最小耦合的标量-张量理论，指定平移四次耦合f(φ)=α(φ²−φ_today²)²与反幂律势V(φ)=Λφ^{−σ}，其中φ_today由有效Planck质量（effective Planck mass）今日归一化条件固定；在修改版hi_class中实现并计算背景与线性宇宙学预测。

Result: 该耦合形式允许晚期有效穿越phantom divide，同时自然压制今日对标准引力的偏离并满足局域引力约束；标量场可在复合前修正膨胀历史，移动声学尺度（acoustic scale）以缓解H_0张力，并在低红移保持暗能量相关性。相对ΛCDM，膨胀历史、物质成团与度规势谱（metric-potential spectra）出现百分之几十的偏离；低多极弱透镜功率被压制约O(20–40%)，晚期积分Sachs-Wolfe信号发生量级为一的变化。

Conclusion: 结果表明该模型具有广泛且依赖尺度的现象学，需进一步开展完整参数空间分析，并将当前线性处理扩展为专门的非线性N体（N-body）实现。

Abstract: We study a curvature-coupled dark energy model that can modify cosmological evolution both before recombination and during the late-time accelerated era. The model belongs to the class of scalar-tensor theories, in which a quintessence field is non-minimally coupled to the Ricci scalar. We specify the model through a shifted quartic coupling, $f(\varphi)=α(\varphi^2-\varphi_{\rm today}^2)^2$, and an inverse power-law potential, $V(\varphi)=Λ\varphi^{-σ}$, where $\varphi_{\rm today}$ is a constant fixed by requiring the effective Planck mass to recover its present-day normalization. We implement the model in a modified version of $\mathtt{hi\_class}$ and compute its background and linear cosmological predictions.
  This specific form of non-minimal coupling allows an effective crossing of the phantom divide at late times while naturally suppressing deviations from standard gravity today and satisfying local gravity constraints. At the same time, the scalar field can modify the expansion history before recombination, shifting the acoustic scale in the direction required to alleviate the $H_0$ tension, while remaining dynamically relevant as dark energy at low redshift.
  For the parameter choices studied here, we find tens-of-percent deviations from $Λ$CDM in the expansion history, matter clustering, and metric-potential spectra. The modified evolution of the gravitational potentials leaves characteristic signatures in relativistic observables, with weak-lensing power suppressed by $\mathcal{O}(20$-$40\%)$ at low multipoles and order-unity changes in the late Integrated Sachs-Wolfe signal. Together, these results reveal a broad, scale-dependent phenomenology that motivates both a full parameter-space analysis and an extension of the present linear treatment to a dedicated non-linear $N$-body implementation.

</details>

### [65] [The Gaudin model and the super Bethe ansatz for unitarizable modules over the general linear Lie (super)algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14176)
*Bintao Cao,Wan Keng Cheong,Ngau Lam*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文研究一般线性李（超）代数在可酉化最高权模张量积上的 Gaudin 代数作用，证明奇异权空间为循环模、相应 Gaudin 代数为 Frobenius 代数，并在一般位置可对角化且有简单谱，同时建立超版本的 Bethe ansatz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gaudin 代数与 Bethe ansatz 是可积系统与表示论的核心工具；本文关注其在一般线性李（超）代数及无限维可酉化最高权模张量积上的结构，尤其是奇异权空间这一与 Bethe 向量相关的重要子空间。

Method: 设 $\mathcal{A}^{\bf z}_{\mathfrak{g}}$ 为关于互异复数序列 ${\bf z}$ 的 Gaudin 代数，$\mathbb{L}$ 为（无限维）可酉化最高权 $\mathfrak{g}$-模的 $\ell$ 重张量积，固定奇异权 $ξ$，研究 $\mathbb{L}^\text{sing}_ξ$ 上的 Gaudin 代数 $(\mathcal{A}^{\bf z}_{\mathfrak{g}})_{\mathbb{L}^\text{sing}_ξ}$。

Result: 证明 $\mathbb{L}^\text{sing}_ξ$ 是循环 $\mathcal{A}^{\bf z}_{\mathfrak{g}}$-模；$(\mathcal{A}^{\bf z}_{\mathfrak{g}})_{\mathbb{L}^\text{sing}_ξ}$ 为 Frobenius 代数；对一般位置的 ${\bf z}$，该代数是可对角化的且具有简单谱。

Conclusion: 在奇异权空间上建立了 $\mathcal{A}^{\bf z}_{\mathfrak{g}}$ 的 Bethe ansatz 超版本，从而把 Gaudin 代数、Frobenius 代数结构与 Bethe ansatz 统一于李超代数框架。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{A}^{\bf z}_{\mathfrak{g}}$ be the Gaudin algebra for the general linear Lie (super)algebra $\mathfrak{g}$ with respect to a sequence ${\bf z} \in \mathbb{C}^\ell$ of pairwise distinct complex numbers, and let $\mathbb{L}$ be any $\ell$-fold tensor product of (infinite-dimensional) unitarizable highest weight $\mathfrak{g}$-modules. Fix a singular weight $ξ$ of $\mathbb{L}$. We show that the singular weight space $\mathbb{L}^\text{sing}_ξ$ is a cyclic $\mathcal{A}^{\bf z}_{\mathfrak{g}}$-module, and that the Gaudin algebra $(\mathcal{A}^{\bf z}_{\mathfrak{g}})_{\mathbb{L}^\text{sing}_ξ}$ for $\mathbb{L}^\text{sing}_ξ$ is a Frobenius algebra. We also show that $(\mathcal{A}^{\bf z}_{\mathfrak{g}})_{\mathbb{L}^\text{sing}_ξ}$ is diagonalizable with a simple spectrum for a generic ${\bf z}$. Furthermore, we establish a super version of the Bethe ansatz for $\mathcal{A}^{\bf z}_{\mathfrak{g}}$ on $\mathbb{L}^\text{sing}_ξ$.

</details>

### [66] [Conifold Gap for the Quintic Threefold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13921)
*Huai-Liang Chang,Shuai Guo,Lei You,Huaigong Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了五次超曲面（quintic threefold）、其他光滑 Fermat Calabi–Yau 超曲面及局部 $\mathbb P^2$ 的 Gromov–Witten（GW）理论，以及相应超曲面的 FJRW 理论的全亏格 conifold gap 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机，仅指出证明目标为上述几何对象的全亏格 conifold gap 猜想。

Method: 证明采用混合自旋 P-场（Mixed Spin P-field, MSP）理论与类似的主空间（master-space）构造；核心对象为锥-顶点理论（cone-vertex theory），通过形式拉普拉斯变换（formal Laplace transform）将其表达为点 CohFT 的平移，并证明所得平移理论的普适 conifold gap 定理，再经主空间构造将 gap 传递至 GW 与 FJRW 理论。

Result: 建立了适用于所构造平移理论的普适 conifold gap 定理，并将该 gap 从 cone-vertex theory 传递到 GW 与 FJRW 理论，从而完成对上述若干理论的 all-genus conifold gap 猜想之证明。

Conclusion: 全亏格 conifold gap 猜想对五次超曲面、其他光滑 Fermat Calabi–Yau 超曲面、局部 $\mathbb P^2$ 的 GW 理论及相应 FJRW 理论均成立，其证明依赖于 MSP 理论、cone-vertex theory 与主空间构造的组合。

Abstract: We prove the all-genus conifold gap conjecture for the Gromov--Witten (GW) theories of the quintic threefold, other smooth Fermat Calabi--Yau threefold hypersurfaces, and local $\mathbb P^2$, as well as for the FJRW theories associated with these hypersurfaces.
  Our proof uses Mixed Spin P-field (MSP) theory and analogous master-space constructions. The central object is the \emph{cone-vertex theory}. We express this theory as a translation of the point CohFT via a formal Laplace transform and prove a universal conifold gap theorem for the resulting translated theories. The master-space construction then transfers the gap from the cone-vertex theory to the GW and FJRW theories.

</details>
