### [67] [Effective theory of the hidden-order pseudogap phase in a doped antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13391)
*Gaia De Paciani,Gesa Dünnweber,Johannes Poersch,Simon M. Linsel,Henning Schlömer,Fabian Grusdt*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出以涨落条纹（fluctuating stripes）为媒介的有效模型，将掺杂反铁磁体（doped antiferromagnets）映射为晶格规范理论（lattice gauge theory），并通过经典蒙特卡洛（classical Monte Carlo）模拟给出相图，用以解释赝能隙（pseudogap）相中隐藏奈尔序（hidden N'eel order）与T*交叉的微观起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赝能隙相的微观起源是铜氧化物（cuprate）高温超导研究中的长期难题。作者试图从掺杂反铁磁体中自旋背景序被条纹结构遮蔽的角度，解释隐藏序到顺磁金属的转变。

Method: 构建以涨落条纹（string-like domain walls）描述掺杂反铁磁体的有效模型，将奈尔序畴壁的开放端点视为晶格规范理论中的涡旋（vortices）。采用经典蒙特卡洛模拟数值确定相图，并用基于逾渗（percolation）的几何序参量诊断隐藏奈尔序。

Result: 在高温区识别出畴壁端点去禁闭（deconfined）的BKT型交叉（BKT-type crossover），并将其解释为从隐藏序区进入上方顺磁金属的T*交叉。在低温区识别出条纹不稳定性（stripe instabilities）。

Conclusion: 该有效模型为赝能隙相及T*交叉提供了基于涨落条纹与规范理论涡旋的统一图像，其预言可在超冷费米子量子模拟器（ultracold fermion quantum simulators）中检验。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of the pseudogap phase constitutes a longstanding puzzle related to the emergence of high-temperature superconductivity in cuprate materials. In this work, we develop an effective model for doped antiferromagnets in terms of fluctuating stripes, or string-like domain walls, which obscure the antiferromagnetic order of the spin background. The open ends of such domain walls of the N'eel order are treated as vortices in the resulting lattice gauge theory. We numerically evaluate the phase diagram by classical Monte Carlo simulations, using percolation-based geometric order parameters to diagnose hidden N'eel order. At high temperatures, we identify a BKT-type crossover in which the domain wall ends become deconfined. We interpret this as the $T^*$ crossover from the hidden order regime to the paramagnetic metal above. At low temperatures, we identify stripe instabilities. Predictions of our effective model can be tested in ultracold fermion quantum simulators.

</details>


### [68] [Tripartite entanglement in chaotic eigenstates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13411)
*Junjia Zhang,Ramanjit Sohal,Shinsei Ryu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究量子混沌本征态（chaotic eigenstates）的多体纠缠（multipartite entanglement）普适特征，通过三分纠缠探针（Rényi tripartite multi-entropy 与 Rényi reflected entropy）结合鞍点分析（saddle-point analysis）推导系综平均的领头阶解析表达式，并以混合场 Ising 模型（mixed-field Ising model）精确对角化进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计力学（classical statistical mechanics）被认为源自量子混沌系统的纠缠结构，其依据是本征态热化假说（eigenstate thermalization hypothesis, ETH）所量化的二分纠缠。然而近年研究表明多体态常由多体纠缠（multipartite entanglement）刻画，因此产生核心问题：混沌本征态具有何种普适的多体纠缠特征。

Method: 研究遍历二分拟设（ergodic bipartition ansatz）的三分推广，借助两个三分纠缠探针——Rényi 三分多熵（Rényi tripartite multi-entropy）与 Rényi 反射熵（Rényi reflected entropy）——并采用鞍点分析（saddle-point analysis）推导二者的系综平均领头阶解析表达式；同时计算控制 Rényi-2 反射熵的反射密度矩阵（reflected density matrix）平均。

Result: 得到两个三分纠缠量的系综平均领头阶解析表达式，并求得 Rényi-2 反射密度矩阵 ρ_{AA^*}^{(2)} 的平均。结果表明，混沌本征态中的 ρ_{AA^*}^{(2)} 可由该系综平均良好描述，且不同于普通约化密度矩阵，其在不同子系统尺度区间呈现三种定性不同的形式，可视为子系统 ETH（subsystem ETH）的三分推广。

Conclusion: 混沌本征态的多体纠缠结构可由三分拟设的系综平均刻画，反射密度矩阵呈现出依赖于子系统尺度区间的三种形式，构成子系统 ETH 的三分推广。解析预测经混合场 Ising 模型（mixed-field Ising model）的精确对角化（exact diagonalization）验证。

Abstract: It is now well-established that classical statistical mechanics emerges from the entanglement structure of quantum chaotic systems, as quantified by the (subsystem) eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH). While this statement rests on the well-studied bipartite entanglement of the eigenstates of such systems, recent years have shown that many-body states are often characterized by multipartite entanglement, raising the question: what are the universal multipartite entanglement features of chaotic eigenstates? We answer this question in part by studying a tripartite generalization of the ergodic bipartition ansatz through the lens of two tripartite entanglement probes, the Rényi tripartite multi-entropy and the Rényi reflected entropy. We derive leading-order analytical expressions for the ensemble averages of both quantities using a saddle-point analysis. Beyond these measures, we obtain the average of the Rényi-2 reflected density matrix, $ρ_{AA^*}^{(2)}$,which controls the Rényi-2 reflected entropy. We show that $ρ_{AA^*}^{(2)}$ in a chaotic eigenstate is well-described by this ensemble average which, unlike an ordinary reduced density matrix, takes three qualitatively distinct forms across subsystem-size regimes, which may be viewed as a tripartite generalization of subsystem ETH. We benchmark our analytical predictions against exact diagonalization of the mixed-field Ising model.

</details>


### [69] [Exact solutions of hidden free fermion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14134)
*Mingchen Zheng,Balázs Pozsgay,Shu Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文揭示了隐藏自由费米子（hidden-free-fermion, HFF）模型的代数起源，证明其可由扭曲多态 Perk–Schultz \(R\) 矩阵的不同 Lax 算子实现给出，并据此求解非均匀 FFD 链，发现其谱结构随耦合变化并呈现涌现立方有限尺寸能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HFF 哈密顿量虽表现出自由费米子谱，却缺乏显式二次型表示，其代数来源与周期边界行为尚属未解问题。该文旨在为这类模型提供统一的代数框架并厘清其边界条件下的谱行为。

Method: 通过扭曲多态 Perk–Schultz \(R\) 矩阵的不同 Lax 算子实现构造 HFF 模型；利用其反射退化生成有限个互相对易的转移矩阵闭合层级，并由函数关系确定完整多体谱；以非均匀自由费米子伪装（free-fermions-in-disguise, FFD）链为具体算例。

Result: 非均匀 FFD 链依耦合不同呈现 HFF、嵌套 HFF 或相互作用谱；均匀 FFD 链在一重环绕与周期边界条件下均出现涌现的立方有限尺寸能隙，同时保持精确的 \(z=3/2\) 体行为。

Conclusion: 该构造可推广至更高态与面型实现，为相互作用量子系统中依赖耦合的谱组织与多尺度行为提供了统一的代数框架。

Abstract: Hidden-free-fermion (HFF) Hamiltonians exhibit free-fermion spectra despite lacking an explicit quadratic representation, yet their algebraic provenance and periodic-boundary behavior remain open questions. Here we establish that a broad class of HFF models arises from distinct Lax operator realizations of a twisted multistate Perk--Schultz \(R\) matrix. Its reflected degeneration generates a closed finite hierarchy of mutually commuting transfer matrices, whose functional relations determine the complete many-body spectrum. As a decisive example, we solve the inhomogeneous free-fermions-in-disguise (FFD) chain, which exhibits HFF, nested HFF, or interacting spectra, depending on its coupling. The homogeneous FFD chain with both one-wrap and periodic boundary conditions exhibits an emergent cubic finite-size gap while retaining the exact \(z=3/2\) bulk behavior. The construction extends to higher-state and face-type realizations, providing a common algebraic framework for coupling-dependent spectral organization and multiscale behavior in interacting quantum systems.

</details>


### [70] [Layer- and Orbital-Selective Mott Physics Driving Dimensional Crossover in Nickelate Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13723)
*Minjae Kim,Byungmin Sohn,Sangjae Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种镍酸盐异质结（nickelate heterostructure）平台，通过层与轨道依赖的维度限域及应变诱导的轨道简并解除，实现层-与轨道选择性莫特转变（LOSMT），并由此触发维度交叉（dimensional crossover）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料设计的核心在于调控维度性与电子关联（electronic correlations）以探索涌现现象（emergent phenomena）。该工作旨在构建新的关联量子材料平台，以实现层-与轨道选择性莫特转变（layer- and orbital-selective Mott transition, LOSMT）。

Method: 采用由单层 LaAlO$_3$ 间隔层分隔的双层与单层 LaNiO$_3$ 构成的异质结，并施加来自 LaAlO$_3$ 衬底的压应变（compressive strain）；在密度泛函理论加动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT）框架下进行计算分析。

Abstract: The design of quantum materials to manipulate dimensionality and electronic correlations is a central focus in the exploration of emergent phenomena. The emergence of the LOSMT provides a new pathway for engineering correlated quantum materials toward unconventional superconductivity and Mott-transistor functionalities.

</details>

In contrast, pristine $5d^2$ Ba$_2$CaOsO$_6$ has shown a single octupolar transition with average cubic symmetry, raising the question of whether BLPS arises from chemical disorder in the doped systems. Beyond standard ${}^{43}$Ca nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectral analysis, our spin-echo nutation measurements detect a finite electric field gradient, demonstrating spontaneous symmetry breaking despite the cubic average structure. While our NMR data support the previously identified octupolar order, the BLPS phase reveals a subdominant quadrupolar contribution, establishing that the hidden order in Ba$_2$CaOsO$_6$ is intrinsically multicomponent. Such mixed multipolar states lie beyond conventional theoretical descriptions of the $5d^2$ ground state. ### [72] [Vestigial altermagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14228)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多竞争序系统中，各单一序被升温破坏后，涨落仍可通过序参量乘积形成残余（vestigial）有序；本文旨在将这一机制应用于交错磁（altermagnetism, AM），探讨其能否由 AFM 与 OO 的热熔解产生。

Conclusion: 残余 AM 可由 AFM 与 OO 的热熔解稳定存在，其相变级次受裸磁化率与相互作用调控；自能涨落给出可观测的 AM 对称准粒子寿命，为其提供实验判别依据。

Abstract: Interaction effects and fluctuations govern the phase diagram of systems with multiple competing phases. In particular, as individual phases are destroyed by increasing temperature, fluctuations can still produce a composite `vestigial' order given by a product of individual order parameters. Here, we describe vestigial Altermagnetism (AM) from the thermal melting of antiferromagnetic (AFM) and orbital orders (OO) on the square lattice. The coexistence of AFM and OO can give rise to conventional AM with ordering wave vector $\mathbf{Q}=(π,π)$. We show that a distinct `vestigial AM' phase with $\mathbf{Q}=0$ can arise from thermal melting of AFM and OO, and investigate its generic phase diagram. We find that the transition from the high-temperature disordered phase into the vestigial AM phase can be either first or second order, depending on the AFM and OO bare susceptibilities and their interactions. We then investigate the unique experimental signatures by calculating the electron spectral function for a minimal two-orbital model. Despite the fact that the vestigial AM order parameter does not couple to electrons directly, fluctuations dominate the self-energy corrections giving rise to a spin- and orbital-dependent quasi-particle lifetime with the AM symmetry.

</details>


### [73] [Quantum Hall Ferromagnetism in a Cavity Vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14838)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索腔真空涨落能否单独稳定一种电子相。文中指出现有认识中由光-物质耦合驱动的电子相变实例稀少，而该研究旨在考察在填充因子 ν=1 的量子霍尔铁磁体中，腔模增强的真空涨落是否可作为驱动连续相变并稳定新电子相的机制。

Method: 方法上先进行偶极规范下的朗道能级投影，发现状态可由电子与光子自由度的张量积（tensor product）良好描述。随后采用解析自旋波计算（spin wave calculations）、平均场理论（mean-field theory）与密度矩阵重整化群（density matrix renormalization group, DMRG）模拟进行研究。此外研究了临界点附近的薄圆柱极限（thin-cylinder limit），并通过纠缠谱简并度（entanglement spectrum degeneracies）分析两相的多体激发态。

Conclusion: 结论指出，该研究提供了仅由腔模增强真空涨落耦合而稳定的电子相的罕见实例。相变边界可由物质与腔参数精确给出并经 DMRG 验证，光子数作为序参量的探针为电子相变与致密核态提供了可能的实验签名。

Abstract: We uncover a continuous phase transition in a quantum Hall ferromagnet (QHF) at filling factor $ν=1$, driven by vacuum fluctuations of a cavity. Our analysis starts with a Landau level projection in dipole gauge, where we find the states to be well-represented by a tensor product of the electronic and photonic degrees of freedom. Through analytic spin wave calculations, mean-field theory and density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) simulations, we show that for a spatially antisymmetric cavity field, the uniform QHF state is stable only for weak light-matter coupling and gives way to states of inhomogeneous electron density above a critical coupling. These states involve "flanks" of uniform QHF fluids, separated by a "compact core" of doubly occupied orbitals with the core size being the order parameter, which we dub as "compact-core phases". While the fully spin polarized electronic states are product states, entanglement builds up between the uniform QHF flanks across the compact core in the $S_z=0$ magnetic sector, motivating an ansatz for the compact-core electronic states. The transition boundary is exactly derived for product electronic states in terms of matter and cavity parameters, and numerically confirmed by DMRG. We also study the thin-cylinder limit near the critical point where quantum fluctuations are enhanced, and the many-body excited states in both phases by focusing on the entanglement spectrum degeneracies. Remarkably, the photon number is found to probe the order parameter of the transition, providing a possible experimental signature of the electronic transition and the compact-core states. Our study offers a rare example of a phase of electrons stabilized solely by coupling to the enhanced vacuum fluctuations of a cavity mode.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: LaCrSb3 is a material exhibiting both quasi-two-dimensional spin fluctuations and three-dimensional magnetic interaction characteristics. By measuring the isothermal magnetization of single-crystals and conducting a systematic critical behavior analysis, we clarify the critical properties of its ferromagnetic phase transition and the intrinsic magnetic interaction mechanism. Based on high-precision isothermal magnetization data measured in the vicinity of the critical point, the Curie temperature for the ferromagnetic-paramagnetic phase transition is determined to be TC = 126 K, with the critical exponents obtained as $β$ = 0.376, $γ$ = 1.417 and $δ$ = 4.76 via the self-consistent iterative method based on the Arrott-Noakes equation. The reliability of these critical exponents is verified by the Widom scaling law, the magnetic state scaling equation and other analyses. A comparison with theoretical models demonstrates that the critical behavior of the magnetic phase transition in this system basically belongs to the universality class of the three-dimensional Heisenberg model. This conclusion is further confirmed by the distance-dependent decay behavior of the exchange interaction J(r), revealing the dominant role of isotropic direct exchange interactions in this system. Finally, drawing on research findings of other quasi-two-dimensional magnetic materials, this work proposes that LaCrSb3 may exhibit finite-temperature magnetic order in the two-dimensional limit, thereby possessing important theoretical research significance and promising practical application prospects.

Abstract: Orbital ordering in vanadium spinel oxides with a geometrically frustrated pyrochlore structure has been a subject of controversy, owing to competing theoretical models and the absence of direct experimental evidence. By carefully investigating multiple-scattering artifacts, we identify the low-temperature structure as belonging to the I41/amd space group. The reconstructed VED around the V sites reveals an orbital-ordered state distinct from both previously proposed real- and complex-orbital models. Our results resolve the long-standing controversy in MnV2O4 and establish a route to identifying orbital states in frustrated spin-orbital systems.

Abstract: $A$V$_3$Sb$_5$ compounds ($A$ = K, Rb, Cs) offer an experimental platform for probing the physics of kagome metals near ideal Van Hove filling. Here, we use optical spectroscopy on $A$V$_3$Sb$_5$ with mixed $A$-site compositions to reveal the effect of the alkali metal on the low-energy electronic structure. Supported by density-functional-theory calculations, we identify a band-saddle-point inversion as a chemical consequence of Cs substitution, revealing that $A$-site composition controls the orbital character of Van Hove singularities, Fermi surface topology, and electron-phonon interactions while leaving the kagome layer intact.

</details>

### [77] [Flavor Flux Correlators, Forward-Backward Asymmetries, and Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13366)
*Kyle Lee,Ian Moult*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出"味通量关联函数（flavor flux correlators）"，用于刻画味量子数的角分布与关联，并指出重味通量关联函数因量子数在重夸克质量尺度存在能隙而具有极干净的微扰可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 味（flavor）在标准模型（Standard Model）结构中至关重要，众多对撞机物理测量都包含探测到的强子的味信息；能量关联函数（energy correlators）研究的进展使能流测量可被改写为算符陈述，从而为味信息的类似重构提供了契机。

Method: 引入味通量关联函数，用以测量味量子数的角分布与关联；利用其与重夸克味流混合电弱反常（mixed electroweak anomaly）的关系，将重夸克前向-后向不对称性（forward-backward asymmetry）重新表述为该反常的宏观后果，并在次次次领头阶（next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order）解析计算该关联函数，同时使用部分子簇射（parton shower）模拟进行验证。

Result: 重味通量关联函数相较其他荷的关联函数具有独特性质：b 与 c 味量子数在重夸克质量尺度存在能隙（gapped），使其成为特别干净的微扰可观测量，非微扰幂次修正被 O(Λ_QCD/m_Q) 压低；部分子簇射模拟结果证实非微扰修正确实受到压低，与重味量子数的能隙性质一致。

Conclusion: 味通量关联函数可利用存档的正负电子对撞数据测量，也可在 Belle II 及未来对撞机上测量，为重味物理与对撞机味测量提供新的理论与实验接口。

Abstract: Flavor plays a crucial role in the structure of the Standard Model. As such, many important collider physics measurements incorporate the flavor information of detected hadrons. Recent progress in the study of energy correlators has enabled collider physics measurements of energy flux to be reformulated as operator statements. In this Letter, we introduce flavor flux correlators, which measure the angular distribution and correlations of flavor quantum numbers. We emphasize a unique feature of heavy flavor flux correlators as compared to correlators of other charges, namely the $b$ and $c$ flavor quantum numbers are gapped at the heavy quark mass scale, making them a particularly clean perturbative observables, with $\mathcal{O}(Λ_{\text{QCD}}/m_Q)$ suppressed non-perturbative power corrections. Using the relation to the anomaly, we compute the flavor flux correlator analytically at next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order and verify using parton shower simulations that non-perturbative corrections are suppressed, as expected from the gapped nature of the heavy flavor quantum numbers. In a 38-point large-$ξ$ differential analysis, the scalar term is collinear at fixed kinematics with the retained C-GFF direction, leading to nearly degenerate solutions and strong profile dependence. Its determination therefore requires polarization-sensitive observables together with process-specific complex helicity amplitudes.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用保持对称性的紧致矢量—矢量接触相互作用（vector–vector contact interaction），其有效耦合带有味依赖以编码深红外区费米子圈产生的屏蔽效应。该相互作用输入到缀饰夸克传播子的 Schwinger–Dyson 方程以及彩虹—梯形截断（rainbow-ladder truncation）下的齐次 Bethe–Salpeter 方程，并在 Schwinger 固有时（proper-time）方案中正则化，使夸克产生阈值消失而手征对称性仍破缺。弹性电磁形状因子 G_E、G_M、G_Q 在广义脉冲近似（generalized impulse approximation）下计算。

Conclusion: ρ 介子的解离由禁闭长度尺度（confinement length scale）的发散主导，而非束缚态质量与阈值的穿越，作者对这两种判据进行了对比。随着理论趋近共形性（conformality），ρ 演变为更大、更圆（larger and rounder）的客体。

The interaction is a symmetry-preserving, confining vector--vector contact interaction whose effective coupling carries a flavor dependence designed to encode the screening produced by fermion loops in the deep infrared. That interaction feeds the Schwinger--Dyson equation for the dressed-quark propagator and the homogeneous Bethe--Salpeter equation in the rainbow-ladder truncation, regularized in the Schwinger proper-time scheme so that quark production thresholds are absent while chiral symmetry remains broken. Raising N_f weakens the coupling, suppresses the dynamically generated mass and restores chiral symmetry together with deconfinement at a critical flavor number N^{c}_{f}. The vector channel responds differently from the pseudoscalar one. Because the $ρ$ is not a Goldstone boson, its mass is not protected by the symmetry that is being restored, and it simply follows the shrinking constituent scale, while the canonically normalized amplitude E_rho and the leptonic decay constant f_rho soften along the way. In this framework the rho sits above the nominal q\bar{q} threshold for every flavor number examined, so the state survives only as long as confinement removes that threshold. Dissociation is therefore governed by the divergence of the confinement length scale rather than by a crossing of the bound-state mass, and we contrast both criteria. </details>


### [81] [Boosted or Inelastic? Discriminating Interpretations of the LZ 248 keV Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14799)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该事件真正的困难不在反冲能量本身，而在于低能区缺乏伴随超出：晕族暗物质（halo dark matter）弹性散射给出单调下降谱，且提供所需动量转移 $q\simeq246$ MeV 已要求 $m_\chi\gtrsim79$ GeV。因此需要考察可规避该限制的机制。

Method: 针对两种运动学上不同的机制——通过质量劈裂 $\delta\sim\mathcal{O}(100)$ keV 抑制低能反冲的内热非弹性暗物质，以及由轻的相对论性通量提供动量的加速暗物质——采用同一套模型无关的标量–标量（scalar--scalar）、赝标量–标量（pseudoscalar--scalar）与赝标量–赝标量（pseudoscalar--pseudoscalar）有效算符进行对比分析。

Result: 两种情形能谱可区分。非弹性谱在三种算符下均位于观测能量附近，仅 $6$--$17\%$ 的事件低于 150 keV；加速谱则强烈依赖算符：标量与赝标量–标量分别有 $99\%$ 与 $92\%$ 的事件低于 150 keV，而赝标量–赝标量有 $75\%$ 高于该阈值。动量依赖对加速解释至关重要，在非弹性情形则与质量劈裂相互权衡，偏好 $\delta$ 从 $\mathcal{O}_{ss}$ 到 $\mathcal{O}_{ps}$ 再到 $\mathcal{O}_{pp}$ 单调下降。

Conclusion: 由于两种情形在整个高能窗口内均存在差异，少量额外事件即可将二者区分，该问题处于 LZ 完整曝光量（full LZ exposure）的可及范围内。

The difficulty such an event poses is not the recoil energy itself but the absence of any accompanying excess at low energy as elastic scattering of halo dark matter yields a monotonically falling spectrum, and supplying the required momentum transfer $q \simeq 246$~MeV already demands $m_χ\gtrsim 79$~GeV. We confront the event with the two kinematically distinct mechanisms that evade this limitation, endothermic inelastic dark matter, in which a mass splitting $δ\sim \mathcal{O}(100)$~keV forbids low-energy recoils and boosted dark matter, in which a light relativistic flux supplies the momentum, treating both with the same model-independent scalar--scalar, pseudoscalar--scalar and pseudoscalar--pseudoscalar effective operators. The two scenarios prove spectrally distinguishable. The inelastic spectra sit near the observed energy for every operator, placing only $6$--$17\%$ of events below 150~keV, whereas the boosted spectra depend critically on the operator: the scalar and pseudoscalar--scalar interactions place $99\%$ and $92\%$ of their events below 150~keV, while the pseudoscalar--pseudoscalar interaction places $75\%$ above it. Momentum dependence is thus essential to the boosted interpretation, but in the inelastic case it trades against the splitting, the preferred $δ$ decreasing monotonically from $\mathcal{O}_{ss}$ to $\mathcal{O}_{ps}$ to $\mathcal{O}_{pp}$. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验报告的248 keV核反冲候选事件LZ230616与晕族暗物质弹性散射不符，而内热非弹性散射虽可给出自然解释，却依赖非对角（而非对角）的核子耦合，该特征通常缺乏理论依据。本文旨在表明此非对角耦合可在辐射型狄拉克中微子质量框架中自动产生。

Method: 在标准模型（Standard Model）基础上引入三个右手中微子（right-handed neutrinos, RHNs）、每代一对矢量样中性费米子（vector-like neutral fermions），以及由惰性二重态（inert doublet）与实单态（real singlet）构成的标量部门，并施加 $\mathcal{Z}_2 \times \mathcal{Z}_4$ 对称性。$\mathcal{Z}_2$ 稳定暗物质，$\mathcal{Z}_4$ 禁止树级狄拉克汤川耦合（Dirac Yukawa coupling）及所有可重整马约拉纳质量项（Majorana mass terms），使狄拉克中微子质量经软破缺标量三重耦合 $\kappa$ 在一圈（one-loop）层次产生。

Conclusion: 该框架以单一 $\kappa$ 参数关联中微子质量生成与非弹性暗物质散射，为 LZ230616 事件提供了自洽解释。相当一部分存活参数空间处于 DARWIN 的探测范围内，可被未来实验检验。

Endothermic inelastic scattering offers a natural explanation, but it rests on a nucleon coupling that is off-diagonal rather than diagonal. The Standard Model is extended by three right-handed neutrinos (RHNs), a pair of vector-like neutral fermions for each generations, and a scalar sector comprising an inert doublet and a real singlet, governed by a $\mathcal{Z}_2 \times \mathcal{Z}_4$ symmetry. The $\mathcal{Z}_2$ symmetry stabilizes the DM, while the $\mathcal{Z}_4$ symmetry forbids the tree-level Dirac Yukawa coupling together with all renormalizable Majorana mass terms, thereby allowing the generation of Dirac neutrino masses at the one-loop level through a softly broken scalar trilinear coupling `$κ$'. Since the neutral dark-sector fields can be expressed in terms of real scalar mass eigenstates, the $Z$ boson couples to them purely off-diagonally and elastic $Z$-mediated scattering is absent identically rather than simply suppressed. The singlet--doublet mixing induced by `$κ$' governs both the inelastic rate and the one-loop Dirac neutrino mass, which therefore vanish as $κ\to 0$. We find that DM masses in the few hundred GeV to TeV range, with splittings of $\mathcal{O}(340-360)\,$ keV, simultaneously reproduce the observed relic abundance, account for the LZ event and yield neutrino masses of the correct order, while respecting elastic direct-detection limits and all relevant theoretical and experimental constraints. Result: 对拟合数据的考察揭示了实验数据中运动学条件与分箱方法（binning methods）所带来的重要影响。所得结果与既有唯象学确定结果及格点计算（lattice computation）在定性上一致。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了基于 Drell-Yan 数据的 π 介子价夸克非极化 TMDPDF 提取，其结果在定性层面支持此前唯象学与格点计算的确定，并强调实验数据分析中运动学与分箱处理需要审慎对待。

Abstract: We present an extraction of the unpolarised transverse momentum dependent (TMD) parton distribution functions (PDFs) for the valence quarks in the pion from experimental mea- surements of the Drell-Yan process. The procedure adopted in this project relies on the framework of TMD factorisation, and the inputs of nucleon TMDPDF, pion collinear PDF, as well as the Collins-Soper kernel. The non-perturbative behaviour of the TMDPDFs is parameterised using an ansatz function that contains three unknown parameters and implements an exponential decay at large transverse distances. This work employs the most recent results in perturbation theory, and utilises a precise estimation of the nucleon TMDPDF. We perform an investigation of data used in the fit. This investigation leads to important observations regarding the effects of the kinematics and binning methods in the experimental data. Our results agree qualitatively with the previous determinations from phenomenology and a lattice computation

</details>


### [84] [A DM candidate indicated at Fermi-LAT and LZ ? Connection with LHC and LC prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15413)
*Alain Le-Yaouanc,François Richard*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于 Fermi-LAT 数据中约 20 GeV 的晕中过量光子，结合 LUX-ZEPLIN 的独立探测结果，提出以自旋 2 的 Kaluza-Klein 引力子共振解释暗物质湮灭，并讨论 LHC 与 e+e- 对撞机的验证前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fermi-LAT 数据分析报告银河晕中约 20 GeV 的高能光子显著过量，而矮星系（dwarf galaxies）中缺失，二者矛盾需要解释。若采用暗物质（dark matter, DM）湮灭解释，则须规避矮星系给出的上限限制，因此需要引入额外机制调和两区域差异。

Method: 作者引入自旋 2 共振（spin 2 resonance）耦合于这些暗物质天体，将星系晕与矮星系中的差异归因于两者速度分布（speed distributions）不同，并将该共振与 LHC 数据所指示的 Kaluza-Klein（KK）引力子（graviton）共振塔相联系，替代常见的 SUSY（supersymmetry）解释路径。

Conclusion: LHC 可对所提出的 KK 引力子解释进行确认，并有望探测暗物质粒子，进而为未来 e+e- 对撞机所需能量范围的确定提供依据。

Abstract: Recently an analysis of Fermi-LAT data has claimed a significant excess of energetic photons centred around 20 GeV, in the halo of our galaxy but absent in dwarf galaxies. This excess can be interpreted as coming from dark mater (DM) objects with a mass of few 100 GeV which annihilate into hadronic jets containing photons mainly from neutral pions. This observation is independently backed up by a completely independent observation of the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) detector of a DM candidate in the same region of mass. To avoid contradiction with upper limits coming from dwarf galaxies, one is led to assume the presence of a spin 2 resonance coupled to these objects, which explains this difference as due to different speed distributions in the two regions. This work recalls that LHC data indicate a tower of Kaluza Klein graviton resonances. This solution is at variance with a SUSY interpretation of DM and therefore does not necessary call for a 1.1 TeV Higgsino, as often proposed. If these KK resonances are coupled to e+e-, as indicated by LHC data, an e+e- collider could observe these DM objects. LHC could confirm our KK graviton interpretation and, eventually, detect the DM particles. This would allow to define de energy needed at a future e+e- collider.

</details>


### [85] [Effect of inelastic scalar dark matter in hidden $U(1)$ scenario after the LZ nuclear recoil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15600)
*Arindam Das,Takaaki Nomura*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究标准模型（Standard Model, SM）的隐藏 $U(1)_H$ 扩展，引入两个 SM 单态复标量场，其中一个因 $U(1)_H$ 电荷为另一场（获得真空期望值）之半，可作为可行的非弹性暗物质（inelastic Dark Matter, DM）候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是构建可解释观测暗物质丰度并复现 LZ 合作组近期高核反冲能量事件（high nuclear recoil energy event）的暗物质模型，同时检验其与现有束流倾倒（beam-dump）及对撞机实验限制的兼容性。

Method: 先估算满足观测暗物质遗迹丰度（relic abundance）的参数区域（经由 DM 湮灭），再用这些参数研究非弹性 DM-原子核散射以复现 LZ 事件；相互作用由 $U(1)_H$ 自发破缺后产生的暗光子（dark photon, $A^\prime$）传递，该暗光子与 SM 光子存在动能混合（kinetic mixing）。

Result: 估计了动能混合作为 $A^\prime$ 质量函数的限制，并将其与来自不同束流倾倒和对撞机实验的现有实验限制进行比较。

Conclusion: 这些参数有望被未来的寿命前沿（lifetime frontier）与能量前沿（energy frontier）实验探测。

Abstract: We study a hidden $U(1)_H$ extension of the Standard Model (SM) introducing two SM singlet complex scalar fields where one of them can be considered as a viable inelastic Dark Matter (DM) candidate due to its $U(1)_H$ charge being half of the charge of the other one which develops a vacuum expectation value. First we estimate a parameter region satisfying observed DM relic abundance through DM annihilation and further use these parameters to study inelastic DM-nucleus scattering to reproduce the recent high nuclear recoil energy event found by LZ collaboration. These interactions are mediated by a dark photon $(A^\prime)$ which is evolved after the spontaneous breaking of $U(1)_H$ symmetry and having a kinetic mixing with the SM photon. Hence we estimate bounds on the kinetic mixing as a function of $A^\prime$ mass and finally compare it with existing experimental bounds from different beam-dump and collider experiments. These parameters could be probed by lifetime and energy frontier experiments in future.

</details>


### [86] [Xenon Isotope Filtering at the Kinematic Edge of Inelastic Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15634)
*Waqas Ahmed,Ammara Ahmad,Mansoor Ur Rehman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究银河晕（Galactic halo）速度上限附近同位素依赖的吸热暗物质（endothermic dark matter）散射，提出同位素分辨的相干接触速率并分离运动学过滤与核压制效应，指出单一"有效氙核"近似在速度边缘存在约8%的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受高能氙反冲（xenon recoil）搜寻启发，需考察氙同位素质量微小差异如何改变吸热散射的最低入射速度，并在所需速度接近截断晕分布末端时被强烈放大。

Method: 构建同位素分辨的相干接触速率（isotope-resolved coherent-contact rate），将运动学过滤（kinematic filtering）与高动量核压制分离；在给定参数下计算各同位素份额，并比较纯同位素靶与自然氙靶的响应及单一有效氙核近似。

Result: 取m_χ=1.1 TeV、δ=365 keV、E_R=248 keV时，纯运动学基准给出f_136^kin≈0.289；经Helm权重后最大贡献为f_132≈0.346，136Xe份额降至约0.094。固定探测质量、微观耦合与晕条件下，225–271 keV积分响应相对自然氙约为0.404（129Xe）、1.235（132Xe）、1.693（136Xe）。单一有效氙核使基准窗口速率改变约8%，并产生更大谱畸变。

Conclusion: 定量结果依赖高速晕假设与所采用的核响应模型，但同位素依赖的运动学支撑不依赖这些假设。该计算属于理论层面诊断，而非LZ似然拟合或逐事件同位素鉴别。

Abstract: Motivated by high-energy xenon recoil searches, we study isotope-dependent endothermic dark-matter scattering near the upper speed boundary of a truncated Galactic halo. Small differences among xenon nuclear masses shift the minimum incident speed and can be strongly amplified when the required velocity approaches the end of the halo distribution. We formulate the isotope-resolved coherent-contact rate and separate this kinematic filtering from high-momentum nuclear suppression. For $m_χ=1.1~\TeV$, $δ=365~\keV$, and $E_R=248~\keV$, the kinematic-only benchmark gives $f_{136}^{\rm kin}\simeq0.289$, while after Helm weighting the largest contribution is $f_{132}\simeq0.346$ and the $^{136}$Xe fraction falls to $\simeq0.094$. For idealized pure-isotope targets at fixed detector mass, microscopic coupling, and halo, the integrated $225$--$271~\keV$ responses relative to natural xenon are approximately $0.404$, $1.235$, and $1.693$ for $^{129}$Xe, $^{132}$Xe, and $^{136}$Xe, respectively. A single effective-xenon nucleus also fails to reproduce the full isotope sum accurately near the edge, changing the benchmark window rate by about $8\%$ and producing larger spectral distortions. These quantitative results depend on the high-speed halo and on the assumed nuclear response; the isotope-dependent kinematic support does not. The calculation is a theory-level diagnostic rather than an LZ likelihood fit or event-by-event isotope identification.

</details>


### [87] [A Unified Numerical Study of Axion Stars: From the Nonrelativistic Regime to General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13638)
*Parisa Arabameri,Paola Arias,Francisco Colipí-Marchant,Enrico D. Schiappacasse*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作对轴子星（axion star）基态结构进行统一数值比较，覆盖牛顿薛定谔-泊松描述、一阶与二阶相对论有效场论以及完整爱因斯坦-克莱因-戈尔登系统，从而界定各近似层次的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子星质量-半径关系常用不同阶的相对论近似计算，各描述何时可靠尚不明确，因此需要统一比较以判断牛顿、相对论修正与完整广义相对论描述的适用边界。

Method: 在四种描述中采用相同的吸引型四次自相互作用，扫描参数 |λ̃|=(M_Pl/f_a)^2，确定最大质量及对应包围质量半径；并在 max|φ|/f_a=O(1) 的最大质量序列部分，于完整广义相对论中显式检验时间谐波截断与势截断。

Result: 所有描述在大 |λ̃| 时均恢复稀薄星标度律，而在弱与中等耦合区差异显著；相对论有效场论系统性地在牛顿与完整广义相对论结果之间插值。高阶谐波展开收敛迅速，恢复完整单余弦势仅使最大质量改变百分之几量级、R_95 改变数个百分点量级。

Conclusion: 结合相对论有效场论向完整广义相对论的系统收敛，结果表明弱耦合区相对薛定谔-泊松预测的显著偏离反映非相对论结构描述的失效，为判断轴子星结构所需近似层次提供了系统基准，数值实现已公开于 Axion Star Solvers 仓库。

Abstract: Axion-star mass-radius relations are commonly computed using different orders of relativistic approximation, making it important to determine where these descriptions remain reliable. We perform a unified numerical comparison of axion-star ground-state configurations in the Newtonian Schrödinger-Poisson description, first- and second-order relativistic effective field theories, and the full Einstein-Klein-Gordon system for a real scalar field. Using the same attractive quartic self-interaction in all four descriptions, we scan $|\widetildeλ|=(M_{\rm Pl}/f_a)^2$ and determine the maximum masses and corresponding enclosed-mass radii. All descriptions recover the common large-$|\widetildeλ|$ dilute-star scaling, while substantial differences appear at weak and moderate coupling. The relativistic EFTs interpolate systematically between the Newtonian and full-GR results. For part of the maximum-mass sequence where $\max|φ|/f_a=O(1)$, we test the temporal-harmonic and potential truncations explicitly in full GR. The higher-harmonic expansion shows rapid convergence, while restoring the complete single-cosine potential changes the maximum mass only at the percent level and $R_{95}$ at the several-percent level. Together with the systematic convergence of the relativistic EFT descriptions toward full GR, these results show that the large weak-coupling departure from the Schrödinger-Poisson prediction reflects the breakdown of the nonrelativistic structural description. Our results provide a systematic benchmark for determining when Newtonian, relativistically corrected, or fully general-relativistic descriptions are required for axion-star structure. The numerical implementation used in this work is available in the Axion Star Solvers repository at https://github.com/Parisa-Arabameri/AxionStar.

</details>


### [88] [Reliable Hybrid Neural Surrogates for Multidimensional Grids: Application to Double Parton Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14048)
*R. Kord Valeshabadi,S. Rezaie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出混合神经网络（hybrid neural-network）方法，用于双部分子分布（double parton distributions, DPDs）的紧凑存储与快速求值；对GS09致密网格仅需存储0.694%的活跃味数值，显著降低磁盘、峰值内存与初始化开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于网格（grid-based）的存储方式（如LHAPDF对共线部分子分布函数PDFs的实现）用于双部分子分布（DPDs）时负担过重。共线PDF仅依赖一个纵向动量分数与一个因子化标度，而不等标度DPD对每个部分子味组合都依赖两个动量分数与两个独立因子化标度，直接制表该四维依赖需要显著更多的磁盘空间与运行内存。

Method: 针对与y无关的不等标度DPD，构建混合神经网络方案：神经网络复现参考网格的多数数值以压缩网格，仅在神经预测未达所需精度的点保存原始数值，从而由神经模型覆盖网格主体、以稀疏表覆盖剩余难点。该方法在PDFxTMDLib中以C++原生实现，运行时不依赖Python或PyTorch。

Result: 在本文研究的基于GS09的致密网格上，仅0.694%的活跃味数值需存入稀疏表。与压缩致密网格相比，混合方法将磁盘占用降低10.6倍、峰值内存使用降低31.6倍、初始化时间约降低10.6倍；同时求值吞吐量达到致密网格速率的约85%。

Conclusion: 该混合方案以适度的求值速度损失换取显著更低的内存与启动成本，适用于DPD的紧凑存储与快速求值。

Abstract: The conventional grid-based approach used for storing and evaluating ordinary parton distribution functions (PDFs), as implemented for example in LHAPDF, becomes much more demanding for double parton distributions (DPDs). While a collinear PDF depends on one longitudinal momentum fraction and one factorization scale, an unequal-scale DPD depends on two momentum fractions and two independent factorization scales for every parton-flavor combination. Direct tabulation of this four-dimensional dependence therefore requires considerably more disk space and runtime memory.
  In this work, we develop a hybrid neural-network method for compact storage and fast evaluation of $y$-independent unequal-scale DPDs. The network compresses the reference grid by reproducing most of its values, while the original values are stored only at points where the neural prediction does not reach the required accuracy. In this way, the neural model captures the bulk of the grid, with a sparse table covering the remaining difficult points.
  For the GS09-based dense grid studied here, only $0.694\%$ of the active flavor values need to be stored in the sparse table. Compared with the compressed dense grid, the hybrid method reduces the disk footprint by a factor of $10.6$, peak memory use by a factor of $31.6$, and initialization time by about a factor of $10.6$. At the same time, its evaluation throughput reaches about $85\%$ of the dense-grid rate. The method is implemented natively in C++ within PDFxTMDLib and requires neither Python nor PyTorch at runtime. The hybrid scheme therefore offers much lower memory and startup costs, with only a modest reduction in evaluation speed.

</details>


### [89] [Did LZ see modified gravity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15118)
*Basabendu Barman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文检验 LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）合作组近期报道的高能核反冲事件是否可解释为非标准宇宙学历史下的冻入（freeze-in）暗物质（dark matter, DM）信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期 LZ 合作组报道了高能核反冲事件，其物理起源尚不明确；作者希望评估该事件能否作为非标准宇宙学历史中冻入暗物质的信号，从而拓展直接探测实验的物理适用范围。

Method: 考虑在太初核合成（Big Bang nucleosynthesis）之前一段修正膨胀时期，由标准模型热浴（Standard Model thermal bath）产生暗物质；在满足观测到的暗物质丰度、宇宙学与对撞机约束的前提下，识别可容纳 LZ 事件的可行参数区域。

Result: 在同时满足暗物质丰度、宇宙学和对撞机约束的条件下，识别出能够容纳该 LZ 高能核反冲事件的可行参数区域。

Conclusion: 结果表明直接探测实验具有探测非标准宇宙学历史、乃至更广泛的替代引力理论的潜力。

Abstract: We investigate whether the recently reported high-energy nuclear recoil event by the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) collaboration can be interpreted as a signal of freeze-in dark matter (DM) in a non-standard cosmological history. We consider DM production from the Standard Model thermal bath during an era of modified expansion preceding Big Bang nucleosynthesis. Requiring consistency with the observed DM abundance, cosmological and collider constraints, we identify viable parameter regions that can accommodate the LZ event. Our results demonstrate the potential of direct detection experiments to probe non-standard cosmological histories and, more broadly, alternative theories of gravity.

</details>


### [90] [Self-field of a moving string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15366)
*Richard A. Battye,Lukasz P. Bunio,Steven J. Cotterill*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估新提出的量 \(J_s\) 在抑制弦自场（self-field）对谱贡献方面的有效性，指出高度对称模型不足以反映复杂形状弦的自场贡献，并通过正弦扰动直弦的数值模拟加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在场论模拟（field theory simulations）中，追踪衰变宇宙弦（cosmic strings）发出的轴子（axions）需要将自场贡献从谱中剥离；近期提出的 \(J_s\) 旨在抑制该自场贡献，但其有效性尚需检验。

Method: 对无限长直弦计算自场对 \(J_s\) 谱以及通常计算的 \(φ\partial_{t}α\) 谱的贡献；随后以正弦扰动的直弦进行数值模拟，并采用自场扣除法（self-field subtraction method）分离自场与辐射贡献，同时与未连接段模型（unconnected segment model）进行对比。

Result: \(J_s\) 方法较之通常方法有实质改进，但无限长直弦这一高对称模型无法涵盖复杂形状弦（网络模拟中的典型情形）自场贡献的全貌；在正弦扰动直弦中，\(J_s\) 谱仍由弦振荡的 \(n=1\) 模主导，该模可归因于自场，在扣除自场后被大幅抑制，且主要来源于沿平行于弦方向的变分，而未连接段模型恰缺失这一来源。

Conclusion: \(J_s\) 虽为实质改进，但仅凭未连接段模型或高对称模型不足以判定其抑制自场贡献的有效性；评估需考虑复杂形状弦及平行方向的变分贡献。

Abstract: We discuss the effectiveness of the recently proposed quantity $J_{s}$ in suppressing the contribution of a string's self-field to its spectrum, which is used in field theory simulations to track the emission of axions by decaying cosmic strings. We compute the contribution of the self-field to the spectrum of this quantity and to the usually computed spectrum of $φ\partial_{t}α$ for an infinitely long straight string. Although we demonstrate that the $J_s$ approach is a substantial improvement, we also point out that this highly symmetric model doesn't capture the full contribution of the self-field to the spectrum emitted by strings of a more complex shape, which are typical in network simulations. We then illustrate this point numerically using a simulation of a sinusoidally perturbed straight string. For this simple configuration, we managed to separate the contribution from the self-field and the radiation to the spectrum of $J_{s}$, using the self-field subtraction method. This allows us to show that the spectrum of $J_{s}$ is still dominated by the $n=1$ mode of the string's oscillation, which can be attributed to the self-field and is largely suppressed when the self-field is removed. We also demonstrate that this mode is predominantly sourced by variations along the direction parallel to the string, which is missing in the unconnected segment model, used by arXiv:2512.13653 to claim the effectiveness of $J_{s}$ in suppressing the self-field's contribution to the spectrum.

</details>

### [91] [Constructing Concrete Colour Tensors for Simple Lie Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13365)
*Jacob L. Bourjaily,Michael Plesser,Philip Velie*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文介绍 Mathematica 软件包 colour_tensors，用于在任意规范理论中具体构造涉及任意荷粒子的显式颜色张量（colour tensors），并据此确定颜色张量间的线性关系、计算与散射振幅相关的表示论量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涉及任意荷粒子的规范理论中，颜色张量的显式构造及相关的表示论（representation theory）计算通常较为困难，缺少便捷的具体实现工具。

Method: 基于 Mathematica 实现软件包 colour_tensors，在任意规范理论（gauge theory）中具体构建涉及任意带电粒子的显式颜色张量。

Result: 该工具可直接确定颜色张量之间的线性关系（linear relations），并计算若干原本困难的表示论量，例如部分振幅（partial amplitudes）之间的颜色干涉（colour-interference）。

Conclusion: colour_tensors 为规范理论中颜色张量的显式构造及相关表示论计算提供了实用实现，有助于散射振幅（scattering amplitudes）相关研究。

Abstract: We describe the implementation and use of colour_tensors: a Mathematica package built for the concrete construction of explicit colour tensors involving arbitrarily charged particles in any gauge theory. These can be used to directly determine linear relations among colour tensors and compute a number of otherwise difficult, representation-theoretic quantities relevant to scattering amplitudes such as colour-interference between partial amplitudes.

</details>


### [92] [The index from a nonpoint and RG flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13374)
*Monica Jinwoo Kang,Craig Lawrie,Jaewon Song*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 将双滤过仿射概型的代数几何构造推广至具有非平凡希格斯分支的理论，对 Argyres–Douglas 理论 $\mathcal{D}_p(SU(N))$ 给出由 $\mathfrak{sl}(N)$ 卡西米尔不变量的普适势所确定的概型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有代数几何构造仅适用于希格斯分支平凡的情形，需要推广以覆盖具有非平凡希格斯分支的 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ 超共形场论（superconformal field theory），并刻画其 OPE 退耦结构。

Method: 对 $\mathcal{D}_p(SU(N))$ 理论，用 $\mathfrak{sl}(N)$ 卡西米尔不变量构成的普适势确定概型，该概型为“加厚”的幂零轨道闭包；通过称为 syzygy maximization 的程序固定精确势。希格斯分支重整化群（renormalization group）流通过将同一普适势拉回到相应 Slodowy 切片实现。

Result: 所得概型复现 4d 数据；底层分次环给出相关顶点算子代数（vertex operator algebra）的 Zhu $C_2$-代数的猜想性表示；双滤过在保留 R 对称信息的同时细化 Arakawa 的 associated scheme；并给出 $\mathcal{D}_p(SU(N), O)$ 理论 Macdonald 指标的系统确定方法。

Conclusion: 该构造为具有非平凡希格斯分支的理论提供了统一的代数几何框架，涵盖普适势、syzygy maximization 与 Slodowy 切片拉回机制，并首次系统给出此前无一般表达式的 $\mathcal{D}_p(SU(N), O)$ 理论 Macdonald 指标。

Abstract: We extend the algebro-geometric construction of bifiltered affine schemes that encode both the Higgs branch and the Macdonald index of 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal field theories to theories with non-trivial Higgs branches. For the Argyres--Douglas theories $\mathcal{D}_p(SU(N))$, we find that a universal potential, given by a function in the Casimir invariants of $\mathfrak{sl}(N)$, determines the scheme. The scheme is given by a `fattened' nilpotent orbit closure, encoding the OPE decoupling structure of the 4d theory. A procedure we call syzygy maximization fixes the precise potential in a broad range of examples, and the resulting schemes reproduce the 4d data. The underlying graded ring gives conjectural presentations of Zhu's $C_2$-algebra of the associated vertex operator algebra, while the bifiltration refines Arakawa's associated scheme by retaining the R-symmetry information. We find that Higgs branch renormalization group flows are realized by pulling back the same universal potential to the corresponding Slodowy slices, including flows for which the infrared Higgs branch is a point. In particular, our construction provides a systematic method for determining the Macdonald indices of the $\mathcal{D}_p(SU(N), O)$ theories, for which no general expressions were previously known.

</details>


### [93] [Integrable Chiral Quantum Field Theories with Spacetime-Dependent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13377)
*Pradip Kattel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类可积的手性费米子量子场论（integrable chiral fermionic quantum field theories），其相互作用同时依赖于空间与时间。构造从满足辫子幺正性（braiding unitarity）与杨–巴克斯特方程（Yang–Baxter equation）的幺正、正则、差分形式两体散射矩阵出发，通过对特征坐标的独立重参数化实现真实的时空依赖，并以手征 SU(2) Gross–Neveu 实现为例说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是构建相互作用显含空间与时间依赖的可积量子场论，突破通常空间均匀或仅含时间依赖的设定。现有可积构造多以均匀或单纯时间依赖情形为主，本文希望给出能容纳真实时空依赖的统一框架。

Method: 以自由左右行粒子携带的、沿轨迹保持不变的特征坐标（characteristic coordinate）为基础，令散射矩阵依赖这些坐标之差；多体振幅在坐标排序扇区上构成离散联络（discrete connection），其局部平坦性由杨–巴克斯特方程保证，空间圆上的周期性则给出表达全局平坦性的量子 Knizhnik–Zamolodchikov 差分方程（qKZ difference equations）。再借助满足交换约束与 qKZ 约束的参考振幅，由这些输运确定固定粒子数的精确波函数。

Result: 推导出时空依赖相互作用满足的特征形式，并以手征 SU(2) Gross–Neveu 实现给出具体例证。结果表明，对右行与左行特征坐标进行独立重参数化可得到真正的时空依赖；而当采用等斜率仿射映射（equal-slope affine maps）时，则退化为空间均匀、仅含时间依赖的情形。

Conclusion: 该构造将辫子幺正性与杨–巴克斯特方程同 qKZ 差分方程结合，为带有真实时空依赖相互作用的可积手性费米子场论提供了系统框架，并统一了均匀/纯时间依赖情形作为特例。

Abstract: We construct a class of integrable chiral fermionic quantum field theories with interactions depending on both space and time. The construction starts with a unitary, regular, difference-form two-body scattering matrix that satisfies braiding unitarity and the Yang--Baxter equation. Each freely moving right- or left-moving particle carries a characteristic coordinate that remains constant along its trajectory, and the scattering matrix depends on differences of these coordinates. The many-body amplitudes form a discrete connection over coordinate-ordering sectors. The Yang--Baxter equation gives its local flatness, while periodicity on a spatial circle produces quantum Knizhnik--Zamolodchikov difference equations expressing global flatness. In terms of a reference amplitude satisfying the exchange and qKZ constraints, these transports determine the exact fixed-particle-number wavefunction. We derive the characteristic form of the spacetime-dependent interaction and illustrate the construction with a chiral $SU(2)$ Gross--Neveu realization. Independent reparametrizations of the right- and left-moving characteristics yield genuine space-time dependence, whereas equal-slope affine maps recover the spatially homogeneous, time-dependent case.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 定义乘积熵为两个密度矩阵构造的归一化乘积算符的 Rényi 或 von Neumann 熵，并建立其与 SVD 纠缠熵的对偶关系。在二维 CFT 中，对低激发本征态推导基态与初级激发之间子系统乘积熵的解析表达式；在量子淬火（quantum quench）动力学中，利用准粒子图像（quasiparticle picture）在标度极限（scaling limit）下给出时间演化。

Result: 对偶关系表明，两纯态子系统的 SVD 纠缠熵精确等于其互补子系统的乘积熵，从而提供清晰的物理诠释与绕开约化跃迁矩阵的高效计算路径。在二维 CFT 中得到的解析表达式经临界 Ising 链显式验证；在全局淬火后，子系统乘积熵呈现热化与复现（revivals）的不同序列，而在局域算符淬火下则出现特征平台，其常数值由插入算符决定。临界 Ising 链的大量数值计算以极高精度确认了上述解析预言。

Conclusion: 乘积熵为刻画两量子态间的关联提供了具有对偶结构与明确物理意义的度量，并在 CFT、可积自旋链及淬火动力学中获得一致验证。该框架兼具解析可处理性与计算效率，有望推广至更一般的量子多体与场论情形。

Abstract: We propose a new product entropy, defined as the Rényi (or von Neumann) entropy of a normalized product operator constructed from two density matrices. We establish a duality showing that the SVD entanglement entropy of a subsystem for two pure states is exactly equivalent to the product entropy of the complementary subsystem. This connection provides both a transparent physical interpretation in terms of the spectral diversity of the subsystem state product and a computationally efficient route that bypasses the reduced transition matrix. For low-lying eigenstates, we derive analytical expressions for the subsystem product entropy between the ground state and primary excitations in two-dimensional conformal field theories, explicitly verified against the critical Ising chain. In quantum quench dynamics, the quasiparticle picture yields time evolution in the scaling limit: following a global quench, the subsystem product entropy exhibits distinct sequences of thermalization and revivals, whereas under a local operator quench, it develops characteristic plateaus whose constant values are determined by the inserted operator. Extensive numerical calculations on the critical Ising chain confirm the analytical predictions with excellent accuracy.

### [96] [Modular flow and the area operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14165)
*Xi Dong,Donald Marolf,Pratik Rath*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在 AdS/CFT 对应下，JLMS 公式将边界模块哈密顿量与 HRT 面积算符及体模块哈密顿量相联系；本文借助"大"码框架指出，当模块流作用于不同经典背景上的态时，体模块哈密顿量可达 1/G 量级并主导 A/4G 项，但在特定条件下流动态仍由纯 HRT 面积流描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的全息码讨论基于固定经典背景上的微扰激发，此时 A/4G 项在 G→0 极限下主导，无法刻画改变经典背景的边界模块流。因此需要一个能处理作用于不同经典背景态的 JLMS 公式框架。

Method: 采用近期关于"大"码（large codes）的讨论作为框架，推导联系体模块流与边界模块流的 JLMS 公式，并考察由某一态定义的模块流作用于另一态时的情形。

Result: 当两态对应的经典背景差别足够大时，体模块哈密顿量并非次主导，其量级可达 1/G，并可超过 A/4G 项。若两态的经典背景相同、仅相差与 HRT 面积共轭的数据，则在温和假设下且在大范围流参数内，模块流后的态仍由单独的 HRT 面积流所给出的经典背景描述。

Conclusion: 体模块哈密顿量的主导地位取决于所作用态的经典背景差异；在满足特定共轭数据条件时，A/4G（HRT 面积流）仍能单独给出有效的经典背景描述。

Abstract: In the context of the AdS/CFT correspondence, the Jafferis-Lewkowycz-Maldacena-Suh (JLMS) formula relates the boundary modular Hamiltonian to the HRT area operator and the corresponding bulk modular Hamiltonian. In standard discussions of a holographic code built from perturbative excitations on a fixed classical background, the $A/4G$ term dominates over the bulk modular Hamiltonian in the limit $G \to 0$. However, this setup is insufficient to describe the action of boundary modular flows that change the classical background. In contrast, our recent discussion of `large' codes provides a useful framework to obtain a JLMS formula that relates bulk and boundary modular flows acting on appropriate states in the code. Here, one sees that the bulk modular Hamiltonian is not in fact subdominant when the modular flow defined by one state acts on another state peaked around a sufficiently different classical background. Instead, the bulk modular Hamiltonian can be of order $1/G$ and can dominate over the $A/4G$ term. Nevertheless, we show that if the two states have the same classical background except for data conjugate to the HRT area then, under mild assumptions and over a large range of flow parameters, the modular-flowed state is described by a classical background given by the HRT-area flow alone.

</details>


### [97] [Holographic Casimir Effect for Mixed Boundary Conditions and non-CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14296)
*Bing-Ji Ding,Hua-Chao Liu,Rong-Xin Miao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 AdS/BCFT 框架下引入膜局域标量场（brane-localized scalar field），研究非共形情形下的全息卡西米尔效应（holographic Casimir effect），并给出卡西米尔振幅的界限、质量依赖行为及引力对偶解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全息卡西米尔效应研究多集中于共形场论（CFT），而膜局域标量场通常会破坏边界共形对称性，为在非共形场论（non-CFTs）中研究该效应提供了动机。

Method: 采用 AdS/BCFT 模型，标量场在条带（strip）两端取不同值以产生混合边界条件（mixed boundary conditions）；在二维时空使用全息 g-定理（holographic g-theorem）推导卡西米尔效应的全息界限，并在更高维分析质量平方 \(m^2\) 不同取值下的行为。

Result: 高维情形下，当 \(m^2=0\) 时膜局域标量场降低卡西米尔振幅，当 \(m^2<0\) 时可增大该振幅；对 \(m^2>0\) 存在无毛定理（no-hair theorem）。混合边界条件下还得到排斥性卡西米尔力（repulsive Casimir force）的引力对偶，并认为其与宇宙监督猜想（cosmic censorship conjecture）一致。

Conclusion: 相关边界形变（relevant boundary deformations）可增强卡西米尔效应；二维的一般物质场受全息 g-定理给出的卡西米尔界限约束，高维则表现出质量依赖的增强或减弱行为。

Abstract: This paper investigates the holographic Casimir effect in AdS/BCFT with a brane-localized scalar field. The scalar field takes different values at the strip's two boundaries, leading to mixed boundary conditions. The massive brane-localized scalar field typically breaks the boundary conformal symmetries, allowing us to study the holographic Casimir effect in non-CFTs. In two-dimensional spacetime, we apply the holographic g-theorem to prove the holographic bound on the Casimir effect for general brane-localized matter fields. In higher dimensions, we find that the brane-localized scalar field reduces the Casimir amplitude when the mass squared \(m^2=0\), but can increase it when \(m^2 < 0\). Additionally, there is a no-hair theorem for the cases where \(m^2>0\). These findings indicate that relevant boundary deformations can enhance the Casimir effect. As a byproduct, we obtain a gravitational dual of the repulsive Casimir force under mixed boundary conditions and argue that it aligns with the cosmic censorship conjecture.

</details>


### [98] [Pseudo entropy from entanglement entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14305)
*Abhigyan Saha,Piotr Sułkowski,Tadashi Takayanagi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文通过 Cauchy-Riemann 方程、Kramers-Kronig 关系与态参数的解析延拓，建立伪熵（pseudo entropy）实部与虚部和普通纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪熵将纠缠熵从单一量子态推广到一对非正交态，且一般为复数；其虚部与实部如何由普通纠缠熵获取尚缺乏系统方法。

Method: 利用 Cauchy-Riemann 方程、Kramers-Kronig 关系及态参数解析延拓；对有限维 Hilbert 空间中全纯系数族，证明约化跃迁矩阵等同于复参数下的普通约化密度矩阵公式，并借助收敛 Taylor 级数及矩阵恒等式推导相关公式。

Result: 伪熵的实部与虚部可由实中点处纠缠熵的偶数阶与奇数阶导数给出；矩阵恒等式还给出过剩伪熵（excess pseudo entropy）、Renyi 熵及多项式系数族的插值公式。在共形场论（conformal field theory）中，Kramers-Kronig 关系用中心荷与边界态单点函数给出虚部的一阶矩；结果亦应用于边界态淬火、热态以及费米子与玻色子高斯态和淬火。

Conclusion: 普通纠缠熵在解析延拓与色散关系框架下足以重构伪熵的实部与虚部，为该复数熵量提供了可计算桥梁，并在共形场论与高斯态体系中给出具体应用。

Abstract: Pseudo entropy extends entanglement entropy from a single quantum state to a pair of nonorthogonal states and is generally complex. Taking advantage of Cauchy-Riemann equations, Kramers-Kronig relations, and analytic continuation in state parameters, we show how and to what extent real and imaginary parts of pseudo entropy can be derived from ordinary entanglement entropy. For families with holomorphic coefficients in finite-dimensional Hilbert spaces, the reduced transition matrix equals the ordinary reduced density matrix formula evaluated at complex parameters. Then a convergent Taylor series gives the real and imaginary parts of pseudo entropy from even and odd derivatives of entanglement entropy at the real midpoint. The matrix identity also gives formulae for excess pseudo entropy and Renyi entropies, and interpolation formulae for families with polynomial coefficients. We apply these results to boundary-state quenches and thermal states in conformal field theory, and to fermionic and bosonic Gaussian states and quenches. In conformal field theory, Kramers-Kronig relations give the first moment of the imaginary part in terms of the central charge and one-point functions for boundary states.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确交代研究动机，仅指出研究目标是厘清三角 Cherednik-Dunkl 算子与有理 Dunkl 算子所生成两个哈密顿量层级之间的关系。该问题属于可积系统与算子代数中层级对应性的探讨。

Abstract: In this paper we study the relations between the Hamiltonian hierarchy generated by trigonometric Cherednik-Dunkl operators and the one generated by rational Dunkl operators. We develop two nested structures relating these two hierarchies. The first nested relation reconstructs the higher rational spin Calogero-Sutherland Hamiltonians exactly from the trigonometric ones. The second nested relation reconstructs the higher trigonometric spin Calogero-Sutherland Hamiltonians as leading terms from the rational ones. In addition, we provide an explanation of the eigenvalues of the trigonometric spin Calogero-Sutherland Hamiltonians in terms of $N$-colored Young diagrams and Maya diagrams.

</details>


### [100] [Free Field Realization of $\mathcal{W}$-Algebra Associated with Exceptional Lie Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14345)
*Daichi Ide,Katsushi Ito,Shigeki Miyazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究例外李代数（exceptional Lie algebras）$E_6$、$E_7$、$E_8$、$F_4$ 所关联的 $\mathcal{W}$-代数（$\mathcal{W}$-algebra）的自由场实现（free field realization），通过秩 $r$ 由秩 $r-1$ 加一个自由玻色子（free boson）递推构造 $\mathcal{W}$-流（$\mathcal{W}$-currents）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅指出研究目标为构建例外李代数对应 $\mathcal{W}$-代数的自由场实现，并发展递推构造方法。
