Result: $\mathcal{W}E_6/\mathcal{W}E_7$ 代数与由 $\mathcal{W}A_5/\mathcal{W}E_6$ 实现的代数在自由场基（free field basis）变换下相同；$\mathcal{W}BC_r$ 代数的递推构造得到研究；计算了 $\mathcal{W}E_{6,7}$、$\mathcal{W}BC_{2,3}$、$\mathcal{W}F_4$ 代数生成元的 $\mathcal{W}$-荷（$\mathcal{W}$-charges），并以卡西米尔不变量（Casimir invariants）表示。

Conclusion: 本文建立了例外李代数 $E_6$、$E_7$、$E_8$、$F_4$ 相关 $\mathcal{W}$-代数的递推自由场实现，并给出了部分生成元的 $\mathcal{W}$-荷与卡西米尔不变量的关系式。

Abstract: We study the free field realization of the $\mathcal{W}$-algebra associated with the exceptional Lie algebras $E_6$, $E_7$, $E_8$, and $F_4$. We develop a recursive construction in which a $\mathcal{W}$-algebra of rank $r$ is obtained from a $\mathcal{W}$-algebra of rank $r-1$ together with a free boson. The $\mathcal{W}$-currents are constructed from the zero commutation relation with the screening charges. The $\mathcal{W}E_6/\mathcal{W}E_7$ algebra is constructed from the $\mathcal{W}D_5/\mathcal{W}D_6$ algebra and is shown to be the same as that realized from the $\mathcal{W}A_5/\mathcal{W}E_6$ algebra, up to a change of the free field basis. The spin-$8$ generator of the $\mathcal{W}E_8$ algebra is built from the $\mathcal{W}D_7$ algebra. The recursive construction of the $\mathcal{W}BC_r$ algebras is also studied. We then realize the $\mathcal{W}F_4$ algebra based on the $\mathcal{W}BC_3$ algebra. Furthermore, the $\mathcal{W}$-charges of the generators of the $\mathcal{W}E_{6,7}$, $\mathcal{W}BC_{2,3}$, and $\mathcal{W}F_4$ algebras are calculated and expressed in terms of the Casimir invariants.

</details>


### [101] [Entanglement Harvesting Landscapes: A Common Computational Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14401)
*Rahul Nigam,Ujjwala Vadrevu,Akshay Kulkarni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的微扰框架，用局域安鲁-德维特探测器（Unruh-DeWitt detector）研究量子场中的纠缠收获（entanglement harvesting），适用于任意探测器轨迹与量子场态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有纠缠收获研究多聚焦于单一探测器构型或特定物理情境，缺乏能统一处理任意轨迹与场态的通用方法。

Method: 将探测器响应表示为无量纲变量，并借助相应的威特曼函数（Wightman function），将微扰积分解析化简为数值高效形式。

Result: 该框架可系统刻画多维收获图景、相边界与鲁棒性；以闵可夫斯基真空（Minkowski vacuum）、热场态及匀加速观测者为代表案例，发现有限温度增强局域探测器响应与非局域交换振幅，从而扩大参数空间中的收获区域。

Conclusion: 收获图景给出探测器-场相互作用的全局刻画，展示温度与观测者运动如何重塑局域噪声与非局域场关联之间的竞争，且该框架统一且易于扩展。

Abstract: Entanglement harvesting provides an operational means of probing the nonlocal correlation structure of quantum fields through localized detector-field interactions. Although harvesting has been investigated in a wide variety of spacetime backgrounds and quantum field states, existing studies largely focus on individual detector configurations or specific physical settings. In this work, we develop a unified perturbative framework for entanglement harvesting with localized Unruh-DeWitt detectors that is applicable to arbitrary detector trajectories and quantum field states through the corresponding Wightman function. By expressing the detector response in terms of dimensionless variables and analytically reducing the perturbative integrals to numerically efficient representations, the framework enables a systematic exploration of multidimensional harvesting landscapes, phase boundaries, and harvesting robustness. As representative applications, we investigate inertial detectors in the Minkowski vacuum, thermal field states, and uniformly accelerated observers. We show that finite temperature enhances both the local detector response and the nonlocal exchange amplitude, leading, within the perturbative Gaussian-switching framework considered here, to an enlargement of the harvesting region in parameter space. The resulting harvesting landscapes provide a global characterization of detector-field interactions and demonstrate how temperature and observer motion reshape the competition between local detector noise and nonlocal field correlations. The framework developed here provides a unified and readily extensible methodology for investigating entanglement harvesting across a broad class of relativistic quantum systems.

</details>


### [102] [Caterpillar Degenerations of Spectral Curves and Double Gelfand-Zeitlin Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14423)
*George Y. Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 caterpillar（弱耦合）极限下，将含两个二阶不规则极点的 $(2,2)$ 型谱曲线分解为两条 $(1,2)$ 或 $(2,1)$ 型谱曲线，并将其与双 Gelfand–Zeitlin（DGZ）系统对应。作者证明了 DGZ 侧的 Duistermaat–Heckman 测度可等同于规范理论侧（由两个 quiver 粘合产生）的 Weyl 测度，并给出 Stokes 矩阵的显式表达及等变 Euler 类的识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标在于理解谱曲线在 caterpillar 或弱耦合极限下的分解机制，并揭示双 Gelfand–Zeitlin 系统与规范理论之间的测度对应关系。其动机还包括在不依赖 conformal blocks 的前提下，建立 isomonodromy tau 函数与微扰配分函数之间的联系，并从谱几何视角预测高阶 $P_{\mathrm{III}}$ tau 函数的微扰系数。

Method: 方法上采用谱曲线分解技术（将 $(2,2)$ 型曲线拆分为 $(1,2)$ 或 $(2,1)$ 型）与 DGZ 系统对应；引入 nodal caterpillar spectral network，用以计算 Stokes 矩阵（至归一化因子）；并将端点归一化的变化识别为 Coulomb 模空间上 fluctuation complex 的等变 Euler 类。同时结合谱曲线粘合技术与 DGZ 系统进行推广。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) DGZ 侧的 Duistermaat–Heckman 测度等同于规范理论侧由两 quiver 粘合得到的 Weyl 测度；(2) isomonodromic tau 函数的 Fourier 展开可解释为 DGZ 系统实极化下的 Peter–Weyl 展开；(3) 利用 caterpillar spectral network 得到 Stokes 矩阵的显式表达（至归一化因子），与已有解析公式一致；(4) 端点归一化变化产生 isomonodromy tau 与微扰配分函数的微扰系数，从而在不用 conformal blocks 的情况下给出二者的等同。

Conclusion: 结论指出，通过谱曲线与 DGZ 系统的粘合技术，可从谱几何角度预测猜想中的高阶 $P_{\mathrm{III}}$ isomonodromic tau 函数的微扰系数。该工作建立了测度、Stokes 矩阵、等变 Euler 类与微扰系数之间的系统性对应，并为高阶情形提供了可推广的框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the decomposition, in the caterpillar or weak-coupling limit, of a spectral curve with two irregular poles of order two, namely a spectral curve of type $(2,2)$, into two spectral curves each having one regular singularity(order-$1$ pole) and one irregular pole of order two, namely spectral curves of type $(1,2)$ or $(2,1)$. Such spectral curves can be identified with a double Gelfand--Zeitlin (DGZ) system. We show that the Duistermaat--Heckman measure on the DGZ side can be identified with the Weyl measure on the gauge-theory side arises from gluing two quivers. Furthermore, the Fourier expansion of the isomonodromic tau function can be interpreted as a Peter--Weyl expansion in the real polarization of the DGZ system. On the other hand, we introduce the nodal caterpillar spectral network. Applying the caterpillar spectral network, we then obtain an explicit expression for the Stokes matrix up to a normalization factor. This expression agrees with the existing analytic formulas. Moreover, we identify the variation of endpoint normalization arising in the Stokes computation with the equivariant Euler class of the fluctuation complex over the Coulomb moduli space. As a result, the variations produce both the perturbative coefficients of isomonodromy tau and the perturbative partition function, and thus give an identification of them without using conformal blocks. Finally, using the gluing technique for spectral curves and the DGZ system, we predict the perturbative coefficients of the conjectural higher-rank $P_{\mathrm{III}}$ isomonodromic tau function from the viewpoint of spectral geometry.

</details>


### [103] [Attraction and repulsion along extremal Einstein--two-Maxwell--dilaton branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14603)
*Ye Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究四维爱因斯坦引力（Einstein gravity）耦合一个伸缩子（dilaton）与两个具有相反符号伸缩子耦合的麦克斯韦场（Maxwell fields）时极端质量分支（extremal mass branch）的性质。作者证明控制极端质量的一阶非线性方程在全实电荷比直线上存在唯一正$C^2$解且为实解析解，从而在正电荷锥上确定唯一全局质量分支，并揭示了力与质量的符号转变由耦合乘积$ab=-1$支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端质量分支的存在性与唯一性此前缺乏严格保证：对一般耦合，极端质量由无已知闭式解的非线性一阶方程控制，因此需要证明解的存在唯一性以确定全局质量分支。同时，该分支上的吸引/排斥行为及质量差的符号规律尚未被系统刻画。

Method: 针对控制极端质量的非线性一阶方程，证明其在整个实电荷比直线上存在唯一正$C^2$解，且该解为实解析，从而由正电荷锥上的正性选出唯一全局质量分支。进一步分析该分支上力的符号、极端质量曲面的横向凸性（transverse convexity）以及任意有限正电荷分割下质量差的符号；并借助已知的维度标度（dimensional rescaling）将符号定理推广到更高维；最后通过径向重构（radial reconstruction）构造渐近平直外部解。

Result: 证明了该方程在全实电荷比直线上有唯一正$C^2$且实解析的解；在质量分支上，任意两个非比例正电荷矢量在$ab>-1$时吸引、$ab=-1$时领头力为零、$ab<-1$时排斥。该转变同时决定极端质量曲面的横向凸性与任意有限正电荷分割下质量差的符号；符号定理经维度标度推广到高维。径向重构表明质量分支上每一点对应一个具有有限面积内端点的渐近平直外部解，而光滑或解析视界延拓（horizon extension）则是限制耦合与标量流的独立条件。

Conclusion: 极端质量分支由正电荷锥上的正性唯一确定，且其力、凸性与质量差符号均由阈值$ab=-1$统一支配，该结论可经维度标度推广至高维。此外，渐近平直外部解的存在与视界可延拓性是相互独立的条件，后者额外限制耦合常数与标量流。

Abstract: We study extremal mass branches in four-dimensional Einstein gravity coupled to one dilaton and two Maxwell fields with opposite-sign dilaton couplings. For generic couplings the extremal mass is governed by a nonlinear first-order equation with no known closed-form solution. We prove that this equation has a unique positive $C^2$ solution on the full real charge-ratio line and that the solution is real analytic, so positivity on the entire positive charge cone selects a unique global mass branch. Along this branch, any two nonproportional positive charge vectors attract for $ab>-1$, have vanishing leading force at $ab=-1$, and repel for $ab<-1$. The same transition fixes the transverse convexity of the extremal mass surface and the sign of the mass difference under arbitrary finite positive-charge partitions. The sign theorem extends to higher dimensions through the known dimensional rescaling. A radial reconstruction then shows that every point of the mass branch defines an asymptotically flat exterior with a finite-area inner endpoint, while smooth or analytic horizon extension is a separate condition that restricts the couplings and scalar flow.

</details>


### [104] [Monopole fission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14617)
*Paul Sutcliffe*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本论文研究引入希格斯势（Higgs potential）后 SU(2) BPS 磁单极子（BPS monopole）鞍点解的裂变（fission）现象，并通过数值方法验证其与低速 BPS 磁单极子散射过程之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SU(2) 电荷为 N 的 BPS 磁单极子存在 4N 维模空间（moduli space）简并，引入希格斯势会消除该简并并产生排斥力。若模空间中某点具有足够对称性，则该点在引入希格斯势后会流向类似的非 BPS 鞍点解，其对称破缺微扰可导致磁单极子裂变，这一过程值得深入探究。

Method: 采用数值方法，演化 Yang-Mills-Higgs 理论的梯度流方程（gradient flow equations）；初始条件与对称破缺微扰由黎曼球面（Riemann spheres）之间的有理映射（rational maps）构造。

Result: 数值模拟得到的裂变结果与低速磁单极子动力学中、基于测地近似（geodesic approximation）所获得的 BPS 磁单极子散射过程的各组分相类似。

Conclusion: 对称破缺微扰下非 BPS 鞍点解的裂变过程，可为理解低速 BPS 磁单极子散射提供对应图像，表明二者在结构上存在相似性。

Abstract: There is a 4N-dimensional moduli space of SU(2) BPS monopoles with charge N, but introducing a Higgs potential removes this degeneracy and yields a repulsive force between monopoles. However, if a point in the BPS monopole moduli space is sufficiently symmetric, then it flows to a similar non-BPS saddle point solution once the Higgs potential is introduced. Monopole fission, into constituent monopoles with lower charges, results from symmetry breaking perturbations of such saddle point solutions. This fission is investigated numerically, by evolving the gradient flow equations of the Yang-Mills-Higgs theory, with initial conditions and symmetry breaking perturbations created using rational maps between Riemann spheres. The results resemble components of BPS monopole scattering processes obtained within the geodesic approximation for low speed monopole dynamics.

</details>


### [105] [Emergent Einstein-Cartan gravity from a spinor loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14627)
*Yadikaer Maitiniyazi,Shinya Matsuzaki,Kin-ya Oda,Yoshiki Uchida,Masatoshi Yamada*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在爱因斯坦-嘉当旋量引力（Einstein-Cartan spinor gravity）框架下，旋量圈（spinor loop）不仅生成局域洛伦兹（local-Lorentz, LL）规范场的动能与质量项，还生成标架（vierbein）两点函数，并自发诱导出包含宇宙学常数、Einstein-Hilbert项、Weyl平方项及挠率（torsion）相关项的单一有效作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不可逆标架假设（irreversible vierbein postulate）下，一阶变量中仅旋量在树图级别具有自身动能项，任何非零背景标架都会自发破缺局域洛伦兹对称性。此前工作已表明LL可作为隐藏局域对称性，本文旨在补全标架（vierbein）扇区，验证旋量圈是否同样生成标架两点函数并保持规范不变性。

Method: 通过计算旋量圈对标架两点函数的贡献，检验其是否满足背景规范不变性，并验证一般坐标变换（general-coordinate）与LL对称性的Ward-Takahashi恒等式。提取与方案无关（scheme-independent）的对数发散项，识别其中诱导引力的组成成分。

Result: 旋量圈生成标架两点函数，且满足两类对称性的Ward-Takahashi恒等式。方案无关的对数发散组合出诱导引力成分：度规扇区的宇宙学常数、Einstein-Hilbert项与Weyl平方项；全反对称挠率的质量项与高导数动能项。该挠率由线性标架涨落反对称部分的导数携带，对应自发破缺LL的拟Nambu-Goldstone玻色子（would-be Nambu-Goldstone boson），其最小耦合作用量中的耦合由一般坐标与LL规范不变性完全确定。

Conclusion: 五个诱导项构成单一有效作用量，显式地同时满足两种对称性，补全了隐藏局域洛伦兹涌现引力（hidden-local-Lorentz emergent-gravity）场景中标架扇区的描述。

Abstract: In Einstein-Cartan spinor gravity under the irreversible vierbein postulate, only the spinor among the first-order variables has a kinetic term of its own at tree level. Any nonzero background vierbein breaks the local-Lorentz (LL) symmetry spontaneously. Our previous work showed that the spinor loop generates the kinetic and mass terms of the LL gauge field, manifesting LL as a hidden local symmetry. Here we show that the spinor loop also generates the vierbein two-point function, while respecting the background gauge invariance and satisfying the Ward-Takahashi identities of the true gauge invariance, for both the general-coordinate and LL symmetries. The scheme-independent logarithmic divergence assembles the familiar induced-gravity ingredients: a cosmological constant, an Einstein-Hilbert and a Weyl-squared term for the metric sector, and a mass and a higher-derivative kinetic term for the totally antisymmetric torsion. In the vierbein sector, this torsion is carried by the derivative of the antisymmetric part of the linear vierbein fluctuation. This part is the would-be Nambu-Goldstone boson of the spontaneously broken LL symmetry, and its couplings in the minimally coupled action are all fixed by general-coordinate and LL gauge invariance. The five induced terms form a single effective action, manifestly invariant under both symmetries, completing the vierbein sector of the hidden-local-Lorentz emergent-gravity scenario.

</details>


### [106] [BCS Gap Equation on Riemannian Manifolds: A Heat Kernel Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14689)
*Levent Akant,Emine Ertugrul,O. Teoman Turgut*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将 BCS 超导理论推广至三维黎曼流形，通过路径积分与热核展开得到重整化能隙方程，并证明能隙随温度单调下降的普适 BCS 行为在弯曲空间中依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将传统平坦空间中的 BCS 超导理论（Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer theory）拓展到三维黎曼流形（three dimensional Riemannian manifold），考察曲率对能隙（gap）的影响，并严格处理能隙方程中的紫外发散（ultraviolet divergences）。

Method: 方法上采用路径积分（path integral）图像，先经 Hubbard-Stratonovich 变换再做鞍点近似（saddle point approximation）导出能隙方程；在度规长度尺度远大于愈合长度（healing length）的假设下求解，并利用热核展开（heat kernel expansion）技术实现系统且数学透明的重整化（renormalization）。在弱耦合极限（Δ≪μ）下对零温与有限温的重整化能隙方程进行渐近积分计算。

Result: 结果上求得体现曲率效应的缓变能隙函数，并建立了联系有限温能隙、零温能隙与温度的普适且与正则化无关（regularization-independent）的关系式；据此解析证明了能隙随温度升高单调减小（∂Δ/∂T<0），并在弯曲空间框架内复现了临界温度附近的标准普适 BCS 行为。

Conclusion: 结论是 BCS 超导的普适热力学特征可在弯曲空间中得到一致刻画，所提出的热核重整化方案为处理弯曲空间能隙方程的发散问题提供了系统途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) theory of superconductivity formulated on a three dimensional Riemannian manifold. We derive the gap equation in the path integral picture by employing a Hubbard-Stratonovich transformation followed by a saddle point approximation. Under the assumption that the length scale set by the metric is much larger than the healing length, we solve the gap equation to find a slowly varying gap function that exhibits the effects of curvature. To rigorously address the ultraviolet divergences inherent in the gap equation, we utilize heat kernel expansion techniques, which provide a systematic and mathematically transparent renormalization scheme. We explicitly evaluate the renormalized gap equation at both zero and finite temperatures, computing the relevant integrals asymptotically in the weak-coupling limit ($Δ\ll μ$). Furthermore, we establish a universal, regularization-independent relation connecting the finite-temperature gap to the zero-temperature gap and the temperature itself. Based on this fundamental relation, we analytically prove the monotonic decrease of the energy gap with increasing temperature ($\partial Δ/ \partial T < 0$), successfully recovering the standard universal BCS behavior near the critical temperature within a curved space framework.

</details>


### [107] [Hyperbolic monopole-type solutions in $SU(3)$ Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15109)
*Yuki Amari*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用Cho-Faddeev-Niemi分解与从$S^2$到旗流形（flag manifold）$F_3=SU(3)/U(1)^2$的调和映射，构造了四维欧氏空间中$SU(3)$纯Yang-Mills理论的$S^1$不变解，并得到自对偶与非自对偶两类解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究$SU(3)$纯Yang-Mills理论在四维欧氏空间中的解结构，借助Cho-Faddeev-Niemi分解与旗流形调和映射框架，探索携带$S^1$不变性的解及其与磁单极子（monopole）构型的联系。

Method: 采用Cho-Faddeev-Niemi分解，并结合从$S^2$到旗流形$F_3=SU(3)/U(1)^2$的调和映射构造$S^1$不变拟设（ansatz），由此将完整Yang-Mills方程约化为耦合的径向方程（coupled radial equations）并求解。

Result: 得到两类解：解析的自对偶（self-dual）解，可解释为双曲磁单极子（hyperbolic monopoles）的非相互作用团簇；以及数值的非自对偶解，可解释为双曲磁单极子-反磁单极子（hyperbolic monopole-antimonopole）束缚态。在束缚态的大质量极限（large-mass limit）下，其归一化作用量（normalized action）趋近平坦空间中相应的非Bogomolny $SU(3)$磁单极子的能量。

Conclusion: $S^1$不变拟设成功将Yang-Mills方程约化为可解的径向方程，所得自对偶与非自对偶解分别对应双曲磁单极子团簇与双曲磁单极子-反磁单极子束缚态，且后者在大质量极限下与平坦空间非Bogomolny $SU(3)$磁单极子能量相符。

Abstract: We construct $S^1$-invariant solutions of the $SU(3)$ pure Yang-Mills theory on four-dimensional Euclidean space using the Cho-Faddeev-Niemi decomposition and a harmonic map from $S^2$ into the flag manifold $F_3=SU(3)/U(1)^2$. The resulting ansatz reduces the full Yang-Mills equations to coupled radial equations. Solving the coupled equations, we derive two types of solutions: analytic self-dual solutions interpreted as noninteracting clusters of hyperbolic monopoles and non-self-dual numerical solutions that can be interpreted as hyperbolic monopole-antimonopole bound states. In the large-mass limit of the hyperbolic monopole-antimonopole bound states, their normalized action approaches the energy of the corresponding non-Bogomolny $SU(3)$ monopole in flat space.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum geometry of gravitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15237)
*M. Mehraeen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作通过弯曲时空量子场论揭示引力子（graviton）Wigner 函数与能动张量的量子几何结构，建立时空与相对论量子态流形的相空间几何框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画引力子的相空间几何，并突破手性涡旋效应（chiral vortical effect）的既有描述，为高能与引力物理中的多态希尔伯特空间几何探索提供路径。

Method: 采用弯曲时空量子场论（quantum field theory in curved spacetime），结合旋量-螺旋度形式（spinor-helicity formalism），将引力子场算符的极化模展开纳入量子几何描述，并应用于弱引力背景。

Result: 表明量子度规（quantum metric）与联络（connection）在描述超越手性涡旋效应的引力子输运中发挥作用，并阐明量子度规在归一化完美流体引力子能动张量中的作用。

Conclusion: 该框架亦可自然涵盖低自旋激发，从而为凝聚态与粒子物理中玻色子与费米子多体系统提供统一的量子几何处理。

Abstract: We uncover the quantum geometric structure of graviton Wigner functions and stress-energy tensors via quantum field theory in curved spacetime, revealing the phase-space geometry of spacetime and relativistic quantum-state manifolds. We show that the polarization mode expansion of the underlying graviton field operator is fully captured by quantum geometry, as encoded in the spinor-helicity formalism, thereby establishing the geometric framework from the outset. Applying this to a weak gravitational background, we demonstrate the roles of the quantum metric and connection in describing graviton transport beyond the chiral vortical effect. We also clarify the role of the quantum metric in normalizing the perfect-fluid graviton stress tensor within this approach. This work paves the path for explorations of multistate Hilbert-space geometry in high-energy and gravitational physics. In addition, this framework naturally encompasses lower-spin excitations, allowing for a unified quantum geometric treatment of bosonic and fermionic many-body systems in condensed matter and particle physics.

</details>


### [109] [Spectral Chaos from a Fluctuating Quantum Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15286)
*Chong-Sun Chu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该研究基于 horizon matrix model（视界矩阵模型），证明模糊球（fuzzy sphere）上 horizon geometry（视界几何）的量子涨落会解除 parton 态的高度简并，生成一个由微观模型决定的单-parton Hamiltonian 系综。该系综尽管具有结构化协方差，仍展现出由反幺正对称类（antiunitary symmetry class）支配的 GOE、GUE、GSE 统计，并实现 Pandey--Mehta 正交到幺正交叉，为黑洞谱混沌提供微观基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞被认为具有最大混沌性，其量子谱预期呈现普适的随机矩阵关联（random-matrix correlations），但此前这一预期主要依赖全息描述或人为引入随机性的多体模型，而非从视界自身微观自由度导出。论文旨在建立从量子视界几何到黑洞谱混沌的微观路径。

Method: 在 horizon matrix model 中，模糊球（fuzzy sphere）支撑高度简并的 parton 态，其多重性对应黑洞熵。作者让视界几何的量子涨落解除该简并，生成单-parton Hamiltonian 系综，并分析其算符结构与秩依赖协方差——二者由微观模型确定，而非由淬火耦合（quenched couplings）固定。随后按反幺正对称类对谱统计进行分类，并进行秩分辨方差分析。

Result: 尽管系综具有结构化协方差，其谱仍呈现由反幺正对称类决定的 GOE、GUE 与 GSE 统计，且相邻能隙统计（adjacent-gap statistic）随矩阵尺寸增大而愈发自平均（self-averaging）。模型实现了普适的 Pandey--Mehta 正交到幺正交叉（orthogonal-to-unitary crossover），交叉尺度由微观几何协方差决定。秩分辨方差分析显示，涨落强度在张量秩上广泛分布直至微观截断，为观测到的 Wigner--Dyson 普适性与有效非局域动力学提供了微观依据。

Conclusion: 反幺正对称直接作用于模糊球几何，因此条件 Hamiltonian 的 Dyson 类由底层量子几何的反幺正对称决定。这构成 JT gravity 中 Stanford--Witten 关系（几何与随机矩阵普适性之间关系）的微观对应物，并确立了从量子视界几何到黑洞谱混沌的微观路径。

Abstract: Black holes are conjectured to be maximally chaotic, with their quantum spectra expected to exhibit universal random-matrix correlations. This expectation, however, has so far rested largely on holographic descriptions or on many-body models in which randomness is introduced rather than derived from the microscopic degrees of freedom of the horizon itself. In the horizon matrix model, the fuzzy sphere supports highly degenerate parton states whose multiplicity accounts for the black-hole entropy. We show that quantum fluctuations of the horizon geometry lift this degeneracy and generate an ensemble of one-parton Hamiltonians. Both the operator structure and the rank-dependent covariance of this ensemble are determined by the microscopic model, rather than fixed by quenched couplings.
  Despite this structured covariance, the resulting spectra exhibit GOE, GUE, and GSE statistics dictated by their antiunitary symmetry classes, with the adjacent-gap statistic becoming increasingly self-averaging with matrix size. The model also realizes the universal Pandey--Mehta orthogonal-to-unitary crossover, with the crossover scale determined by the microscopic geometric covariance. A rank-resolved variance analysis shows that the fluctuation strength remains broadly distributed across tensor ranks up to the microscopic cutoff, providing a microscopic basis for the observed Wigner--Dyson universality and effectively nonlocal dynamics. The antiunitary symmetry acts directly on the fuzzy-sphere geometry, so the Dyson class of a conditional Hamiltonian is determined by the antiunitary symmetry of the underlying quantum geometry. This provides a microscopic counterpart of the Stanford--Witten relation between geometry and random-matrix universality in JT gravity. These results establish a microscopic route from quantum horizon geometry to black-hole spectral chaos.

</details>


### [110] [On the commuting charges and the Bethe ansatz for the quantised Kadomtsev-Petviashvili equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15316)
*Chiara Paletta,Alessandro Torrielli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究[4]所提形式下 Kadomtsev-Petviashvili（KP）方程的量子化版本，聚焦于高阶守恒荷 H₃ 的守恒性证明与有限体积内 Bethe ansatz（贝特拟设）两体问题的求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子化 KP 方程的可积结构（integrability）需要检验其高阶守恒荷是否在量子层面依然守恒，而这通常受紫外发散（ultraviolet divergence）影响；同时有限体积下的 Bethe ansatz 解有助于理解其谱结构。

Method: 在 [4] 提出的量子 KP 形式框架内：一方面给出第一高阶荷 H₃ 的表达式，并在正则化特定紫外发散后证明其与 H₀（总质量）、H₁（动量）、H₂（哈密顿量）的对易性；另一方面在有限体积（finite volume）中研究 Bethe ansatz，求解两体问题的一般解。证明过程中使用了由作者监督的 AI 辅助。

Result: 在相当一般的假设下，H₃ 的对易性结果不依赖于正则化方案（regulator）的选择；有限体积 Bethe ansatz 给出了两体问题的一般解。

Conclusion: 量子 KP 方程在正确处理紫外发散后可保持 H₃ 守恒且具正则化独立性，有限体积 Bethe ansatz 的两体解为进一步分析其谱性质提供了基础；AI 辅助证明在受监督条件下可参与此类推导。

Abstract: We study the quantised version of the Kadomtsev-Petviashvili equation in the formulation proposed in [4]. We analyse two aspects. One is the conservation of the first higher charge $H_3$ (besides the total mass $H_0$, the momentum $H_1$ and the Hamiltonian $H_2$). We find the expression of $H_3$ and prove commutativity, provided we regularise a specific ultraviolet divergence. The result then does not depend on the choice of a regulator, under fairly general assumptions. We then study the Bethe ansatz in finite volume and obtain a general solution to the two-body problem. We have used AI supervised by us to aid the proofs.

</details>


### [111] [A fluid dual to large $D$ membrane paradigm at first subleading order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15512)
*Supratim Halder,Manu Kurian,Mangesh Mandlik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在大 $D$ 膜范式下，构建了渐近平直时空中不带电黑洞的对偶相对论流体动力学，并在一阶次领头阶（first subleading order）上从膜方程中提取了输运系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大 $D$ 膜范式建立了黑洞动力学与非引力背景中余维一膜演化之间的对偶。但渐近平直时空缺乏类时渐近边界，因此无法照搬 AdS/CFT 流体-引力对偶的构造方式，需要新的流体描述框架。

Method: 在 $1/D$ 展开的一阶次领头阶上，于动力学膜上内禀地（intrinsically）构造有效流体，使其运动方程与次领头阶膜方程完全等价；采用朗道规范（Landau frame）进行流体动力学分析。

Result: 系统表现为受有效背景力影响的流体；非平衡粘性效应（viscous effects）自然出现，可从体粘性压强（bulk viscous pressure）与剪切应力张量（shear stress tensor）直接提取流体输运系数；流体呈现负压强，反映膜世界体（worldvolume）的固有表面张力（surface tension）。

Conclusion: 在渐近平直时空中，通过膜上内禀流体构造可实现黑洞与流体的对偶，并直接获得输运系数及负压强特征，为无类时边界情形下的流体-引力对偶提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The large $D$ membrane paradigm establishes a duality between the dynamics of black holes and the evolution of a codimension-one timelike membrane in a non-gravitational background. In this work, we formulate the relativistic fluid dynamics dual to an uncharged black hole in asymptotically flat spacetime at the first subleading order in the $1/D$ expansion. Due to the absence of a timelike asymptotic boundary, unlike in AdS/CFT fluid-gravity duality, we construct an effective fluid intrinsically on the dynamical membrane, whose equations of motion are exactly equivalent to the subleading order membrane equations. By performing this fluid-dynamical analysis in the Landau frame, we show that the system behaves as a fluid influenced by an effective background force. Out-of-equilibrium viscous effects emerge naturally, allowing us to extract the fluid transport coefficients directly from the bulk viscous pressure and shear stress tensor. Furthermore, the fluid exhibits a negative pressure, capturing the intrinsic surface tension of the $(D-1)$-dimensional membrane worldvolume.

</details>


### [112] [Black holes and phase transitions in Conformal Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15737)
*Luis Guajardo,Luis Gutiérrez Frez,Seyed Naseh Sajadi,Julio Oliva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究任意偶数维共形引力（conformal gravity）中静态黑洞的热力学性质，给出了质量表达式并验证热力学第一定律，发现正则系综中存在任意维度的零阶黑洞/黑洞相变（zeroth-order black-hole/black-hole phase transitions）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形引力中黑洞解及其热力学行为在任意偶数维度下的系统刻画尚不充分，尤其质量与“纯引力起源”的毛发参数（hair parameters）之间的关系，以及相变结构值得探讨。

Method: 针对拉格朗日量为 D=2k 维中 k 个 Weyl 张量（Weyl tensors）完全缩并的共形引力，利用适用的 Birkhoff 定理说明球对称情形下时空在局部 Weyl 重标度（Weyl rescalings）意义下静态且唯一；将解取为渐近局部 (A)dS 或平坦，视界推广为任意常曲率曲面 γ；重点分析渐近 AdS 情形，用若干毛发参数计算时空质量。

Result: 在渐近 AdS 情形下，无穷远处的宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）为积分常数；时空质量可由具纯引力起源的各类毛发参数表达；热力学第一定律（first law of thermodynamics）得到满足；正则系综（canonical ensemble）分析显示对任意维度均存在零阶黑洞/黑洞相变。

Conclusion: 共形引力中的静态黑洞在任意偶数维下具有一致的热力学描述，其质量由引力毛发参数决定，并普遍表现出零阶黑洞相变，表明此类相变结构不依赖于具体维度。

Abstract: We explore the thermodynamic properties of static black holes in conformal gravities in arbitrary, even dimensions. In the spherically symmetric case, up to local Weyl rescalings, these spacetimes are static and unique, due to a Birkhoff's theorem that applies for all conformal gravities whose Lagrangians are complete contractions of $k$ Weyl tensors in dimension $D=2k$. The solutions can be chosen to be asymptotically locally (A)dS or flat, and the horizon can be extended to be a surface of arbitrary constant curvature, $γ$. We focus on the asymptotically AdS case, for which the cosmological constant at infinity is an integration constant, and compute the mass of the spacetime in terms of the diverse hair parameters which have a purely gravitational origin. We show that the first law of thermodynamics is satisfied, and study phase transitions in the canonical ensemble, showing the existence of zeroth-order black-hole/black-hole phase transitions for arbitrary dimension.

</details>


### [113] [String charge density/bit threads correspondence and its S-duality in type IIB supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15929)
*Houwen Wu,Shuxuan Ying*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将守恒弦电荷密度的空间投影与比特线程（bit-thread）流对应起来，从而为比特线程赋予弦世界面描述，并用世界面导出流的最大熵通量计算黑洞熵。该构造在 BTZ 及十维 F1-NS5-P、D1-D5-P 系统中复现熵，并经 S-duality 检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特线程与弦电荷之间缺乏明确的对应关系，且黑洞熵与纠缠熵相等的信息流解释尚待统一。文章旨在建立该对应并给出两端熵相等的通量机制。

Method: 把守恒弦电荷密度的空间投影等同于比特线程流，借世界面导出流并求其最大熵通量；先复现 BTZ 熵，再推广至十维 F1-NS5-P 与 D1-D5-P 系统，并用 Type IIB S-duality 检验 F1 流到 D1 流的映射；最后提出 D1-D5 边界匹配猜想（boundary-matching conjecture）。

Result: BTZ 熵被复现；在十维系统中构造成立。S-duality 下局域流、度规与流范数随变，但最大物理通量不变并给出相同熵，构成对该对应的非平凡检验。

Conclusion: 在粗粒化图像中，每条弦电荷线定义一根携带熵的管；边界 CFT 纠缠熵与体黑洞熵共同固定管数与每管最大熵之积，并在固定管离散化下赋予每管相同容量，故二者描述同一守恒最大熵通量，从而给出纠缠熵与黑洞熵相等的信息流解释。

Abstract: We identify the spatial projection of a conserved string charge density with a bit-thread flow. This correspondence gives bit threads a string worldsheet description and allows us to compute black hole entropy from the maximal entropy flux of a worldsheet-derived current. We first reproduce the BTZ entropy and then extend the construction to the ten-dimensional F1-NS5-P and D1-D5-P systems. Under Type IIB S-duality, the F1 current maps to the D1 current. Although the local currents, metrics, and flow norms change, the maximal physical flux remains unchanged and gives the same entropy. This provides a nontrivial check of the correspondence. Finally, we propose a D1-D5 boundary-matching conjecture. In the coarse-grained picture, each string-charge line defines an entropy-carrying tube. The boundary CFT entanglement entropy and the bulk black hole entropy both fix the product of the number of tubes and the maximum entropy carried by each tube. For a fixed tube discretization, they assign the same capacity to each tube. They therefore describe the same conserved maximal entropy flux, providing an information-flow interpretation of the equality between entanglement entropy and black hole entropy.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Pauli Supported Invariants for Periodic Graphs-Derived Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14209)
*Sarah Chehade,Andrew Vlasic,Rebekah Herrman,Rick Mukherjee*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文引入一种由图局部邻域哈密顿量的泡利分解（Pauli decomposition）所导出的图不变量（graph invariant），并在周期图与非周期图（含准晶）框架下研究其不变性与逆命题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在通过泡利支撑（Pauli-support）数据，为基于图的哈密顿量与图量子系统提供算符论层面的比较机制。其动机在于将图局部结构信息编码为泡利串集合，从而建立图等价与量子系统比较之间的新联系。

Method: 对给定的有根 h-hop 邻域构造局部邻接算符（local adjacency operator），将其嵌入统一维度的希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space），并以非零系数出现的泡利串（Pauli strings）定义泡利支撑集。方法进一步推广至李闭包（Lie closures）、对易子（commutants）与 Cartan 型结构。

Result: 对周期图（periodic graphs），证明了在格兼容图等价（lattice-compatible graph equivalence）下的不变性，并在根分离假设（root-separation hypothesis）下建立了逆命题。框架可推广至有限局部复杂性（finite local complexity）的非周期图，并与准晶（quasicrystals）的切割-投影模型（cut-and-project models）及彭罗斯密铺（Penrose tilings）建立联系。

Conclusion: 所得不变量从量子信息视角给出了图基哈密顿量的算符论描述，并提供通过泡利支撑数据比较图基量子系统的新机制。

Abstract: We introduce a graph invariant obtained from Pauli decompositions of Hamiltonians derived from local graph neighborhoods. Given a rooted h-hop neighborhood, we construct a local adjacency operator, embed it into a common Hilbert space dimension, and define its Pauli-support set as the collection of Pauli strings appearing with nonzero coefficients. For periodic graphs, we prove invariance under lattice-compatible graph equivalence and establish converse results under root-separation hypothesis. The framework naturally extends to Lie closures, commutants, and Cartan-type structures generated by the associated Pauli supports. We further discuss extensions to aperiodic graphs with finite local complexity and make connections to cut-and-project models of quasicrystals and notably, Penrose tilings. From a quantum information perspective, the resulting invariants provide a operator-theoretic description of graph-based Hamiltonians and offer a new mechanism for comparing graph-based quantum systems through Pauli-support data.

</details>


### [115] [Liouville integrability of Beltrami-Maxwell fields with sources: A geometric theory of magnetic surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14242)
*Shin-itiro Goto*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在四维闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）中研究含非平凡源的经典麦克斯韦场（Maxwell fields），从接触几何（contact geometry）与辛几何（symplectic geometry）角度进行分析，并构造可积系统实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的出发点是探讨含源麦克斯韦场解与接触几何、辛几何之间的结构关联，尤其是如何借助三维流形上的 Beltrami 场（Beltrami fields）构造特定类型的解。

Method: 将三维黎曼流形（Riemannian manifold）上的接触形式（contact forms）视为 Beltrami 场，以此构造若干类含源麦克斯韦场解；并利用接触流形的辛化（symplectization）得到四维辛流形，从而把麦克斯韦系统表述为刘维尔可积哈密顿系统（Liouville integrable Hamiltonian system）。

Result: 构造出多类含源麦克斯韦场解并给出若干应用：其一为超导体 London 方程（London equations）的解；其二为当沿磁场矢量存在非平凡守恒量时，麦克斯韦系统可在辛化所得四维辛流形上视为可积哈密顿系统，Lundquist 模型及 ABC 流（ABC flow）模型的受限情形等均被证明在此意义下可积。

Conclusion: 含源经典麦克斯韦场可通过 Beltrami 场与接触/辛几何建立联系，并在特定守恒量条件下呈现刘维尔可积结构，涵盖超导 London 方程与多种典型磁场模型。

Abstract: We study classical Maxwell fields with non-trivial sources in four-dimensional Minkowski spacetime. In particular, contact and symplectic geometric aspects are discussed. As a point of departure, it is shown that a few classes of solutions with sources are constructed from the so-called Beltrami fields, where these fields are contact forms on three-dimensional Riemannian manifolds. In addition, several applications in these classes are provided. One is to give a solution to the London equations for superconductors. Another one is that, if there is a non-trivial conserved quantity along the magnetic vector field, then the Maxwell system can be viewed as a Liouville integrable Hamiltonian system on a four-dimensional symplectic manifold, where this manifold is obtained by a symplectization of the contact manifold. As examples, Maxwell fields with the Lundquist model, a restricted case of the ABC flow model, and so on, are shown to be integrable in the above sense.

</details>


### [116] [Boundary transmission and excursion geometry in the Lorentz mirror model: a numerical study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14949)
*Jian Gu,Qin Hao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过有限体积（finite-volume）数值实验研究平面洛伦兹镜面模型（planar Lorentz mirror model）中的边界透射与游走几何，刻画了穿透分位数、板透射概率、横向范围与重复访问等有限体积输运特征，但未确定渐近标度指数（asymptotic scaling exponent），亦未解决无限平面局域化（infinite-plane localization）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画平面洛伦兹镜面模型中的边界透射（boundary transmission）与游走几何（excursion geometry）行为。作者关注有限体积下穿透、约束与路径结构随镜面密度及空位（vacancies）变化的规律，而非直接求解渐近标度或无限平面局域化。

Method: 采用有限体积数值实验（finite-volume numerical experiments）。具体包括：测量圆柱穿透分位数（cylinder penetration quantiles）随周长的变化；估计板透射概率（slab-transmission probabilities）并给出指数约束界（exponential confinement bounds）及统计置信度；统计条件游走的横向范围（transverse ranges）与重复访问比例（repeated-visit fractions）；计算端点标签碰撞概率（endpoint-label collision probabilities）；以及实施重复顶点处的重路由（rerouting）。

Result: 圆柱穿透分位数在模拟范围内随周长近似线性增长，且在存在空位时归一化分位数呈系统性上移。板透射概率估计给出在测试宽度上带量化统计置信度的指数约束界。条件到达远端边界的平面游走，其横向范围与出口半径成正比，重复访问比例随镜面密度显著变化。端点标签碰撞概率明显高于一般粘合下界（general gluing lower bound），但仅解释观测到的同列穿越概率（same-column crossing probability）的一部分。重复顶点处的重路由保持出口不变，同时显著缩短采样路径。

Conclusion: 研究结果刻画了有限体积输运与游走几何特征，包括穿透分位数的线性增长、指数约束界、横向范围的比例关系及重路由的路径缩短效应。但该工作未确定渐近标度指数（asymptotic scaling exponent），也未解决无限平面局域化（infinite-plane localization）问题。

Abstract: We study boundary transmission and excursion geometry in the planar Lorentz mirror model through finite-volume numerical experiments. Cylinder penetration quantiles grow approximately linearly with circumference over the simulated range, with a systematic upward drift in the normalized quantiles when vacancies are present. Estimated slab-transmission probabilities yield exponential confinement bounds with quantified statistical confidence at the tested widths. Planar excursions conditioned to reach a distant boundary exhibit transverse ranges proportional to the exit radius, while their repeated-visit fractions vary strongly with mirror density. Endpoint-label collision probabilities substantially exceed a general gluing lower bound, although they account for only part of the observed same-column crossing probability. Finally, rerouting at repeated vertices preserves the exit while substantially shortening the sampled paths. These findings characterize finite-volume transport and excursion geometry without determining an asymptotic scaling exponent or resolving infinite-plane localization.

</details>


### [117] [Certified robustness of quantum spin textures under magnetic-field perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14172)
*Raúl Sánchez Galán,Robert Wieser*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该工作结合局域自旋期望值的几何与谱微扰理论，给出磁场扰动保持基态斯格明子电荷（skyrmion charge）的定量界限，并论证几何阈值与谱隙保护的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是磁场变化到何种程度会使量子自旋织构（quantum spin texture）失去其斯格明子电荷，需要明确保护该拓扑电荷的条件及其失效边界。

Method: 对三角化的自旋-s织构，将局域自旋期望值的几何与谱微扰理论（spectral perturbation theory）结合，在允许不等误差界与固定边界矩的条件下，确定使重构拓扑电荷变为 ill-defined 的最小局域矩变化，并由此导出磁场扰动的显式界限。

Result: 所得几何阈值给出保持基态电荷的磁场扰动显式界限，当谱隙与几何余量（geometric margin）具有正极限时呈 1/N 标度；在含交换与 Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya 相互作用的自旋-1/2 有限系统中，一个严格验证的七自旋例子显示电荷在单一确证磁场处发生改变，而此时激发隙与所有局域自旋极化仍被限制在远离零的范围内，且几何余量与场证书在该交叉点线性消失。

Conclusion: 结果提供了织构稳定性的定量证书，并厘清了谱隙保护（spectral-gap protection）的适用限度。

Abstract: How much can a magnetic field change before a quantum spin texture loses its skyrmion charge? We address this question by combining the geometry of local spin expectation values with spectral perturbation theory. For a triangulated spin-$s$ texture, we determine the smallest change in its local moments that makes the reconstructed topological charge ill-defined, allowing unequal error bounds and fixed boundary moments. This geometric threshold yields explicit bounds on magnetic-field perturbations that preserve the ground-state charge, with $1/N$ scaling when the gap and geometric margin have positive limits. We apply the bounds to finite spin-$1/2$ systems with exchange and Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interactions. In a rigorously verified seven-spin example, the charge changes at a single certified field while the excitation gap and all local spin polarizations remain bounded away from zero; at such a crossing the geometric margin and the field certificate vanish linearly. The results provide quantitative certificates of texture stability and clarify the limitations of spectral-gap protection.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.GA'></div>

# astro-ph.GA [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [The New Dark Matter Density Profile from JWST JADES Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14071)
*Aritra Sanyal,Farook Rahaman*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 基于JWST JADES DR3中587个星系（1.5≤z≤3.5）的NIRSpec光谱，首次直接推得暗物质密度剖面；该剖面无尖点、完全解析且依赖红移，其中心密度在宇宙正午期间近乎恒定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暗物质密度剖面多依赖间接推断或模拟，缺乏由高红移星系光谱直接观测导出的结果，尤其在宇宙正午（cosmic noon）时期的观测约束仍属空白。

Method: 从各NIRSpec G235M/G395M光谱中提取Hα发射线速度弥散σ_ha，按Δz=0.5分为四个红移bin并堆叠星系，在广义相对论（General Theory of Relativity, GTR）框架下重建代表性星系群旋转曲线；拟合四参数修正指数模型并推导精确GTR能量密度，将其约化为系数由JWST观测闭式确定的[2/3] Padé近似（Padé approximant）。

Result: 所得剖面无尖点（cusp-free）、完全解析且随红移变化，满足能量条件、因果性与轨道稳定性；中心密度ρ(0)在z=1.5–3.5间变化小于15%，而渐近旋转速度下降达35%。

Conclusion: 结果表明宇宙正午存在一个与重子演化解耦的普适暗物质核心饱和密度（universal dark matter core saturation density）。

Abstract: We present the first dark matter density profile derived directly from James Webb Space Telescope JADES Data Release~3 NIRSpec observations of $N = 587$ galaxies spanning the cosmic noon epoch $1.5 \leq z \leq 3.5$. From each NIRSpec G235M/G395M spectrum we extract the H$α$ emission-line velocity dispersion $σ_{\rm ha}$, stack galaxies in four redshift bins of $Δz = 0.5$, and reconstruct representative group rotation curves within the General Theory of Relativity. Fitting the four-parameter modified exponential model and deriving the exact GTR energy density, we reduce the profile to a compact $[2/3]$ Padé approximant with all coefficients determined in closed form from JWST observations. The profile is cusp-free, fully analytic, and redshift-dependent. Energy conditions, causality, and orbital stability are all satisfied. Most strikingly, the central density $ρ(0)$ varies by less than $15\%$ across $z = 1.5$--$3.5$ despite a $35\%$ decline in the asymptotic rotation velocity, revealing a universal dark matter core saturation density at cosmic noon decoupled from baryonic evolution.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Singular automorphic products and BKM algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15658)
*Kaiwen Sun,Haowu Wang,Brandon Williams*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文回答了 Borcherds 于 1995 年提出的问题：奇异权全纯自守积（holomorphic automorphic products of singular weight）是否只有有限多个。作者证明，在形如 $2U\oplus L$ 的格（lattices）上，在共轭意义下恰有 27 个这样的积，并通过与 BKM 超代数（BKM superalgebra）及顶点算子超代数（vertex operator superalgebras）的联系完成分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 继证明月光猜想（moonshine conjecture）之后，Borcherds 于 1995 年提出奇异权全纯自守积是否仅有有限多个的问题。该问题关涉自守形式、格与李超代数之间的深层联系，本文旨在给出这一分类问题的完整答案。

Method: 分类方法将每个积在标准一维尖点（1-dimensional cusp）处等同于某个 BKM 超代数的超分母（superdenominator），该超代数带有仿射李超代数（affine Lie superalgebra）$\hat{\mathfrak{g}}$ 上的分次模结构。辅助代数 $\hat{\mathfrak{g}}$ 满足的强条件将可能性缩减至初始的 1372 个候选，再经排除论证（elimination argument）筛选。

Result: 在形如 $2U\oplus L$ 的格上，共轭意义下恰有 27 个奇异权全纯自守积。强条件将候选缩减为 1372 个，排除论证后仅剩 85 个代数 $\hat{\mathfrak{g}}$，它们对应上述 27 个积，且与若干特殊的顶点算子超代数族密切相关。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了将仿射李超代数系统推广至具模性（modularity）的双曲型（hyperbolic ones）的框架，推广了 Feingold 与 Frenkel（1983）的工作。

Abstract: After proving the moonshine conjecture, Borcherds asked in 1995 whether there exist only finitely many holomorphic automorphic products of singular weight. We show that, up to conjugation, there are exactly 27 such products on lattices of the form $2U\oplus L$. The classification proceeds by identifying each product at a standard $1$-dimensional cusp with the superdenominator of a BKM superalgebra that carries a graded module structure over an affine Lie superalgebra $\hat{\mathfrak{g}}$. The auxiliary algebras $\hat{\mathfrak{g}}$ are shown to satisfy strong conditions that reduce the possibilities to an initial list of 1372 candidates. An elimination argument leaves exactly 85 algebras $\hat{\mathfrak{g}}$ that correspond to the 27 products. This classification is closely connected with certain distinguished families of vertex operator superalgebras. Our results provide a systematic framework for extending affine Lie superalgebras to hyperbolic ones with modularity, generalizing the work of Feingold and Frenkel (1983).

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [The price of locality: Maxwell-Cattaneo charge transport in Schwinger-Keldysh effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13378)
*Andrea Amoretti,Matteo Anselmi,Daniel K. Brattan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文确定非线性麦克斯韦-卡塔内奥（Maxwell-Cattaneo）电荷输运理论在何种条件下可嵌入具有特定修正动力学 KMS 对称性的局部高斯 Schwinger-Keldysh 框架，并给出相应的随机准流体力学描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在厘清非线性 Maxwell-Cattaneo 电荷输运理论的量子-统计场论嵌入问题，特别是将附加矢量视为内禀耗散量后对熵流分析与对称性约束所带来的影响。

Method: 采用局部高斯 Schwinger-Keldysh 嵌入方案，结合修正的动力学 KMS（Kubo-Martin-Schwinger）变换，并进行熵流（entropy-current）分析与静水力学生成泛函（hydrostatic generating functional）分析。

Result: 将附加矢量视为内禀耗散量会根本性地改变熵流分析：静水力学生成泛函不再能固定所有熵流改进项，这些改进项须独立给定；同时，修正 KMS 变换成为作用量对称性会带来流体力学分析之外的额外约束。

Conclusion: 研究给出并解释了相应的随机准流体力学（stochastic quasi-hydrodynamics）表述，明确了非线性 Maxwell-Cattaneo 输运理论在耗散性与 KMS 对称性约束下的嵌入条件。

Abstract: We determine the conditions under which the nonlinear Maxwell-Cattaneo theory of charge transport admits a local Gaussian Schwinger-Keldysh embedding with a particular modified dynamical KMS symmetry. We show that treating the additional vector as intrinsically dissipative fundamentally modifies the entropy-current analysis: the hydrostatic generating functional no longer fixes all entropy-current improvements, which must instead be supplied independently. Subsequently we discuss the additional constraints not contained in the hydrodynamic analysis that are imposed by the modified KMS transformation becoming a symmetry of the action. We both interpret and supply the corresponding stochastic quasi-hydrodynamics.

</details>


### [121] [Quantum Stochastic Walks on the Permutation Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13387)
*Feng He,Arthur Hutsalyuk,Giuseppe Mussardo,Andrea Stampiggi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文将洗牌问题表述为对称群 $S_n$ 上的随机游走，把经典转移矩阵对应到置换哈密顿量所生成的量子游走，并嵌入量子随机游走（quantum stochastic walk）框架，证明量子相干性可加速混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是探究“有序让位于随机”的速度，以及量子相干性（quantum coherence）能否加速这一过程。作者以洗牌（card shuffling）这一范式问题为切入点，将经典洗牌与量子动力学联系起来。

Method: 作者首先把 Diaconis 与 Shahshahani 研究的随机对换游走（random-transposition walk）及更一般的由 $S_n$ 共轭类（conjugacy classes）生成的游走改写为连续时间形式；随后将每个经典游走的转移矩阵与生成对应酉量子游走的置换哈密顿量（permutation Hamiltonian）对应，并把问题嵌入相干动力学与耗散过程竞争的量子随机游走中。

Result: 纯酉演化通常不收敛到经典意义下的均匀分布，因为相干性保留而非抹除信息。在量子随机游走设定下，相干性有助于随机化：论文证明它只能减小与计算基（computational basis）下均匀分布的距离，从而可加速混合；对最慢模式（slowest mode）的分析给出了产生显著加速所需耦合强度的判据；数值结果揭示了量子与经典混合时间之比可坍缩为单参数形式。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在置换群（permutation groups）上的游走中，相干性与耗散可以协同作用，共同促成随机性的涌现。

Abstract: How rapidly does order give way to randomness, and can quantum coherence accelerate this process? We address these questions through the paradigmatic problem of card shuffling, formulated as a random walk on the symmetric group $S_n$. We first recast the random-transposition walk studied by Diaconis and Shahshahani, as well as more general walks generated by conjugacy classes of $S_n$, in continuous time. We then identify the transition matrix of each classical walk with a permutation Hamiltonian generating a corresponding unitary quantum walk. Purely unitary evolution, however, does not generically converge to the uniform distribution in the classical sense of mixing: coherence preserves information rather than erasing it. We therefore embed the problem into a quantum stochastic walk, where coherent dynamics competes with the dissipative process responsible for classical mixing. In this setting, quantum coherence assists randomization. We prove that it can only decrease the distance from the uniform distribution in the computational basis and can therefore accelerate mixing. An analysis of the slowest mode yields a criterion for the coupling strength required to produce an appreciable speedup. Finally, numerical results reveal a scaling collapse of the ratio between quantum and classical mixing times onto a simple one-parameter form. Our results illustrate how coherence and dissipation can cooperate in the emergence of randomness in walks on permutation groups.

</details>


### [122] [A New Framework for Interfacial Statistics: Exact n-Point Correlations of Gaussian Level Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14586)
*Aleksei M. Cherkasov,Kirill M. Gerke,Aleksey Khlyupin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作推导出高斯水平集界面（Gaussian level-set interfaces）高阶关联的精确解析表达式，建立体相场统计与界面几何之间的直接联系，并借此实现无序介质重构、高斯泡沫建模与记忆增强各向异性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出目标在于打通体相场统计（bulk field statistics）与界面几何（interface geometry）之间的关联，从而为复杂输运表征、随机重构与材料设计等提供理论基础。

Method: 方法为解析推导高斯水平集界面的高阶关联函数（higher-order correlations）精确表达式，并以此框架构建高斯泡沫（Gaussian foams）——具有可调形貌的双阈值结构界面（double-thresholded interfaces）。

Result: 框架支持高效重构无序介质（disordered media）、细致建模高斯泡沫，以及分析反映表面结构方向持续性的记忆增强各向异性（memory-enhanced anisotropy）。

Conclusion: 结果在表征复杂输运、指导随机重构、设计目标性能材料、量化关联函数的信息含量以及建模不规则边界上的方向性过程等方面开辟了新的可能。

Abstract: We derive exact analytical expressions for higher-order correlations of Gaussian level-set interfaces, establishing a direct link between bulk field statistics and interface geometry. This framework enables efficient reconstruction of disordered media, detailed modeling of {Gaussian foams}---structured, double-thresholded interfaces with tunable morphology---and analysis of {memory-enhanced anisotropy} reflecting directional persistence in surface structure. These results open new possibilities for characterizing complex transport, guiding stochastic reconstructions, designing materials with desired properties, quantifying the information content of correlation functions and modelling directional processes on irregular boundaries.

</details>


### [123] [Shannon Entropy as an Order Parameter for the Two-Dimensional Confined Coulomb Systems: Exact Balance Law, Topological Charge Sum Rule, and Boundary Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15428)
*Goergiy K. Lavrov,Eduard G. Nikonov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文基于Voronoi拓扑电荷分布的Shannon熵，为硬壁圆盘上二维Thomson问题的已知最小能量构型建立了完整的信息论框架，并证明了两条对所有N=12至10^5均成立的精确结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的在于以信息论（information theory）视角刻画二维Thomson问题最小能量构型的拓扑结构，通过Shannon熵将总熵分解为体相、边界与混合贡献，并用拓扑电荷求和规则导出边界粒子配位数与体相电荷之间的精确关系。

Method: 方法以Voronoi拓扑电荷分布（Voronoi topological charge distribution）的Shannon熵为核心，提出无近似的熵平衡律（entropy balance law）与拓扑电荷求和规则（topological charge sum rule），并结合体相与边界配位数的分离进行推导，所有恒等式经数值验证至机器精度。

Result: 证明总电荷等于边界粒子数的两倍加六；边界熵受二元最大值约束，其饱和等价于每个边界粒子的体相电荷趋于负二分之一，且该框架将该情形限制在边界粒子数至少为十二的情况。数值上定出体相成核在56个粒子、全局无序度峰值在146、边界饱和起始于约1000之后；框架可推广至闭合球面几何，其中总电荷固定为十二。

Conclusion: 该框架为二维Thomson问题提供了精确且可数值验证的信息论描述，揭示体相成核、无序度极大与边界饱和的特征尺度，并可自然推广到球面几何中总电荷固定为十二的情形。

Abstract: We establish a complete information-theoretic framework for the known minimum-energy configurations of the two-dimensional Thomson problem on a hard-wall disk, based on the Shannon entropy of the Voronoi topological charge distribution. Two exact results are proven and hold for all studied systems of size $N=12$--$10^5$. First, an entropy balance law decomposes the total entropy into bulk, boundary, and mixing contributions without approximation. Second, a topological charge sum rule fixes the total charge at twice the number of boundary particles plus six; together with the observed separation of bulk and boundary coordination numbers, it yields exact relations between the populations of three- and four-coordinated boundary particles and the bulk charge. We further prove that the boundary entropy is bounded by the binary maximum and that saturation of this bound is equivalent to the bulk charge per boundary particle approaching minus one half, a regime that the framework itself restricts to boundaries of at least twelve particles. All identities are verified numerically to machine precision, locating bulk nucleation at $56$ particles, the maximum of global disorder at $146$, and the onset of boundary saturation beyond about $1000$. The framework carries over to closed spherical geometry, where the total charge is fixed at twelve.

</details>


### [124] [Modulated structures in discrete approximations to a three-dimensional cholesteric model on a recursive lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15891)
*William de Castilho*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究三维取向空间中手性向列（chiral nematic）格点模型的有限态近似，在根 Cayley 树上将统计问题化为分支配分函数的非线性递推，并在无穷配位数极限下化为 softmax 映射。通过对 Cartesian 三态、平面四态与非平面四态离散化的分析，报告了各类吸引子及其稳定性图，并强调其非平衡相图性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性向列格点模型的连续取向自由度难以直接求解，需要有限态近似与可解析的递推结构来考察手性参数对有序与调制行为的影响。采用根 Cayley 树与无穷配位数极限，可将统计问题约化为映射动力学，从而分析吸引子结构。

Method: 模型以旋转四极相互作用（rotated quadrupolar interaction）表述，给出直接的角度手性参数 Δ，并在 director 限于 xy 平面时退化为通常的平面手性向列形式。在根 Cayley 树上将统计问题写成分支配分函数的非线性递推；当 rJ_Q 固定且配位数趋于无穷时，递推变为对态概率的 softmax 映射。分析了 Cartesian 三态、平面四态与非平面四态三种离散化。

Result: Cartesian 三态离散化支持有序吸引子与周期二吸引子；平面四态离散化呈现一系列可公度与更长周期的调制吸引子，其无序不动点在 t=9/16 处经一对相位为 q=±2Δ 的复共轭特征值失去稳定性，故对任意非零手性都选出调制临界模。非平面四态集合本质各向异性，直接重建所述模型时支持显著的周期三以及周期二和更高周期循环。

Conclusion: 由于吸引子稳定性本身不足以确立全局热力学稳定性，文中给出的图应被解释为吸引子/稳定性图（attractor/stability diagrams）而非平衡相图（equilibrium phase diagrams）。

Abstract: We study finite-state approximations to a chiral nematic lattice model whose local directors are embedded in three-dimensional orientational space. The model analyzed here is formulated in terms of a rotated quadrupolar interaction, which provides a direct angular chirality parameter $Δ$ and reduces to the usual planar chiral-nematic form for directors confined to the xy plane. On a rooted Cayley tree, the statistical problem is written as a nonlinear recursion for branch partition functions. In the infinite-coordination limit, with $rJ_Q$ fixed, the recursion becomes a softmax map for the state probabilities. We analyze Cartesian three-state, planar four-state, and non-planar four-state discretizations. The first supports ordered and period-two attractors, whereas the planar four-state discretization displays a sequence of commensurate and longer-period modulated attractors. Its disordered fixed point loses stability at $t=9/16$ through a complex-conjugate pair with phase $q=\pm 2Δ$, so a modulated critical mode is selected for every nonzero chirality. The non-planar four-state set is intrinsically anisotropic and, when the stated model is reconstructed directly, supports prominent period-three as well as period-two and higher-period cycles. Because attractor stability does not by itself establish global thermodynamic stability, the diagrams reported here are interpreted as attractor/stability diagrams rather than equilibrium phase diagrams.

</details>


### [125] [Bridging Control, Inference, Transport, and Thermodynamics: From Theory to Applications in Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15897)
*Emmy Blumenthal,Nikolas Claussen,Benjamin Eysenbach,Catherine Ji,Gautam Reddy,Colin Scheibner,Benjamin Sorkin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本综述串联控制论（control theory）、最优传输（optimal transport）、概率推断（probabilistic inference）、非平衡热力学（non-equilibrium thermodynamics）与机器学习（machine learning）五个领域，指出其共同主题为在动力学或统计约束下优化类自由能泛函（free-energy-like functionals），并给出强化学习、变分推断与生成建模中的应用示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年从高维数据中学习复杂结构的方法快速发展，凸显了物理学、应用数学与机器学习子学科之间的根本联系。然而这些思想常以不同语言表述，缺乏统一的概念线索，因此有必要将其整合梳理。

Method: 采用综述（review）与引导式巡览（guided tour）的方式，从物理学原理出发，将五个领域中以不同表述出现的思想汇聚到同一概念主线上，即受动力学或统计约束的类自由能泛函优化。论文面向不熟悉相关主题的读者，不预设先备知识。

Result: 归纳出一条贯穿五个领域的共同概念线索，并展示了该线索在强化学习（reinforcement learning）、变分推断（variational inference）与生成建模（generative modeling）中的若干选定应用。

Conclusion: 控制论、最优传输、概率推断、非平衡热力学与机器学习可通过“约束下类自由能泛函优化”这一主线建立统一联系，表明跨学科的概念融合具有方法论价值。

Abstract: The last decade has seen the development of powerful methods for learning complex structure from high-dimensional data. These advances have brought to the foreground fundamental connections between subdisciplines of physics, applied mathematics, and machine learning. In this review, we bring together some of these ideas, often expressed in different languages, to highlight a conceptual thread that links five distinct fields: control theory, optimal transport, probabilistic inference, non-equilibrium thermodynamics, and machine learning. A common theme is the optimization of free-energy-like functionals under dynamical or statistical constraints. We offer a guided tour through this thread and present selected applications in reinforcement learning, variational inference, and generative modeling. The review does not assume prior familiarity with these topics, and begins with principles originating from physics.

</details>
